import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Lock,
  Mail,
  Building2,
  User,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  KeyRound,
  X,
  FileText,
  BadgePercent,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  loginApi,
  registerApi,
  forgotPasswordApi,
  resetPasswordApi,
} from '../services/api';

export default function AuthModal({ isOpen, onClose, initialView = 'login', onAuthSuccess }) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const [view, setView] = useState(initialView); // 'login' | 'register' | 'forgot' | 'reset'
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState(null);

  // Form states
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState('');
  const [fiscalId, setFiscalId] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState('ADMIN');
  const [resetToken, setResetToken] = useState('');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');

  const clearMessages = () => {
    setError(null);
    setSuccessMsg(null);
  };

  const handleQuickDemoLogin = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    clearMessages();
    const res = await loginApi('auditor@konigpartners.es', 'AuditPass2026!');
    setLoading(false);
    if (res.success) {
      if (onAuthSuccess) onAuthSuccess(res.user);
      onClose();
    } else {
      setError(res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleLoginSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    clearMessages();
    const res = await loginApi(email, password);
    setLoading(false);
    if (res.success) {
      if (onAuthSuccess) onAuthSuccess(res.user);
      onClose();
    } else {
      setError(res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleRegisterSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    clearMessages();
    const res = await registerApi({
      email,
      password,
      fullName,
      companyName,
      fiscalId,
      role,
    });
    setLoading(false);
    if (res.success) {
      if (onAuthSuccess) onAuthSuccess(res.user);
      onClose();
    } else {
      setError(res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleForgotSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    clearMessages();
    const res = await forgotPasswordApi(email);
    setLoading(false);
    if (res.success) {
      setSuccessMsg(res.message);
      if (res.demoToken) {
        setResetToken(res.demoToken);
        setView('reset');
      }
    } else {
      setError(res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleResetSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    clearMessages();
    const res = await resetPasswordApi(resetToken, newPassword);
    setLoading(false);
    if (res.success) {
      setSuccessMsg(res.message);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setView('login');
        setPassword(newPassword);
        clearMessages();
      }, 1500);
    } else {
      setError(res.error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fade-in">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        {/* Cabecera modal */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-zinc-800/80 bg-zinc-900/40">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-blue-600/10 border border-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 tracking-tight">FINDAFINITY</h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">Acceso Seguro de Despacho & Pyme</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-800/50 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Notificaciones de error o éxito */}
        {error && (
          <div className="mx-6 mt-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/60 flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-rose-300">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-rose-400 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}
        {successMsg && (
          <div className="mx-6 mt-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800/60 flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-emerald-300">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-400 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{successMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Contenido según vista */}
        <div className="p-6">
          {/* ======================= VISTA LOGIN ======================= */}
          {view === 'login' && (
            <div>
              <div className="mb-5 text-center">
                <h4 className="text-base font-semibold text-zinc-100">Iniciar Sesión</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
                  Accede a tu Staging Radar de conciliación contable
                </p>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleLoginSubmit} className="space-y-3.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Correo Electrónico Profesional
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500" />
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      required
                      value={email}
                      onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="auditor@despacho.es"
                      className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 transition-colors"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                    <label className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">
                      Contraseña
                    </label>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => { setView('forgot'); clearMessages(); }}
                      className="text-[11px] text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors"
                    >
                      ¿Olvidaste tu contraseña?
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500" />
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      required
                      value={password}
                      onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="••••••••••••"
                      className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 transition-colors"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full mt-2 py-2 px-4 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Validando credenciales...' : 'Iniciar Sesión'}
                  <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </form>

              {/* Botón de Acceso Rápido con Cuenta Demo */}
              <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-zinc-800/80">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleQuickDemoLogin}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-2 px-3 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800/80 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-xs text-zinc-300 font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors"
                >
                  <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                  Acceso Rápido con Despacho Demo (König & Asoc.)
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-4 text-center">
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                  ¿Aún no tienes cuenta?{' '}
                  <button
                    onClick={() => { setView('register'); clearMessages(); }}
                    className="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 font-medium"
                  >
                    Registrar nuevo despacho o pyme
                  </button>
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ======================= VISTA REGISTER ======================= */}
          {view === 'register' && (
            <div>
              <div className="mb-4 text-center">
                <h4 className="text-base font-semibold text-zinc-100">Crear Cuenta Profesional</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                  Alta inmediata de despacho contable o empresa
                </p>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleRegisterSubmit} className="space-y-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Nombre Completo del Auditor / Administrador
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <User className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500" />

                      onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}

                    <input
                      type="email"
                      required
                      value={email}
                      onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="auditor@mendezaudit.es"
                      className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"

                      required
                      minLength={6}
                      value={password}
                      onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="••••••••••••"
                      className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Perfil Operativo
                  </label>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setRole('ADMIN')}
                      className={`py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border text-center transition-colors ${
                        role === 'ADMIN'
                          ? 'bg-blue-600/20 border-blue-500/50 text-blue-300'
                          : 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'

                      className={`py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border text-center transition-colors ${
                        role === 'AUDITOR'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-600/20 border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-300'
                          : 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      Auditor Contable
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full mt-2 py-2 px-4 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Creando cuenta...' : 'Registrar Despacho & Acceder'}
                  <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </form>

              <div className="mt-4 text-center">
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                  ¿Ya tienes cuenta?{' '}
                  <button
                    onClick={() => { setView('login'); clearMessages(); }}
                    className="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 font-medium"
                  >
                    Iniciar sesión
                  </button>
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ======================= VISTA FORGOT PASSWORD ======================= */}
          {view === 'forgot' && (
            <div>
              <div className="mb-4 text-center">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto mb-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600/10 border border-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400">
                  <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <h4 className="text-base font-semibold text-zinc-100">Recuperar Contraseña</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
                  Introduce tu correo registrado para restablecer tus credenciales
                </p>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleForgotSubmit} className="space-y-3.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Correo Electrónico
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500" />
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      required
                      value={email}
                      onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="auditor@despacho.es"
                      className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Generando solicitud...' : 'Enviar Instrucciones'}
                  <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </form>

              <div className="mt-4 text-center">
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setView('login'); clearMessages(); }}
                  className="text-xs text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 transition-colors"
                >
                  ← Volver al inicio de sesión
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ======================= VISTA RESET PASSWORD ======================= */}
          {view === 'reset' && (
            <div>
              <div className="mb-4 text-center">
                <h4 className="text-base font-semibold text-zinc-100">Nueva Contraseña</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
                  Introduce el token y tu nueva clave de acceso
                </p>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleResetSubmit} className="space-y-3.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Token de Recuperación
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={resetToken}
                    onChange={(e) => setResetToken(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Pega aquí el token recibido"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Nueva Contraseña
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500" />
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      required
                      minLength={6}
                      value={newPassword}
                      onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="••••••••••••"
                      className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Actualizando...' : 'Guardar Nueva Contraseña'}
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\AuthScreen.jsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Layers, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Lock, 
  Mail, 
  User, 
  Building2, 
  KeyRound, 
  ArrowRight, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle,
  Sparkles,
  Server
} from 'lucide-react';
import { loginApi, registerApi, forgotPasswordApi, resetPasswordApi } from '../services/api';

export function AuthScreen({ onAuthSuccess, backendOnline }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('login'); // 'login' | 'register' | 'forgot' | 'reset'
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState(null);
  const [infoMessage, setInfoMessage] = useState(null);

  // Formulario Login
  const [loginEmail, setLoginEmail] = useState('');
  const [loginPassword, setLoginPassword] = useState('');

  // Formulario Registro
  const [regFullName, setRegFullName] = useState('');
  const [regEmail, setRegEmail] = useState('');
  const [regPassword, setRegPassword] = useState('');
  const [regCompanyName, setRegCompanyName] = useState('');
  const [regFiscalId, setRegFiscalId] = useState('');

  // Formulario Recuperación
  const [forgotEmail, setForgotEmail] = useState('');
  const [resetToken, setResetToken] = useState('');
  const [resetNewPassword, setResetNewPassword] = useState('');

  const clearMessages = () => {
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setInfoMessage(null);
  };

  const handleTabChange = (tab) => {
    clearMessages();
    setActiveTab(tab);
  };

  const handleLoginSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    clearMessages();
    setLoading(true);

    const res = await loginApi(loginEmail, loginPassword);
    setLoading(false);

    if (res.success) {
      onAuthSuccess(res.user);
    } else {
      setErrorMessage(res.error || 'Credenciales no válidas.');
    }
  };

  const handleRegisterSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    clearMessages();
    setLoading(true);

    const res = await registerApi({
      email: regEmail,
      password: regPassword,
      fullName: regFullName,
      companyName: regCompanyName,
      fiscalId: regFiscalId,
      role: 'ADMIN',
    });
    setLoading(false);

    if (res.success) {
      onAuthSuccess(res.user);
    } else {
      setErrorMessage(res.error || 'Error al completar el registro.');
    }
  };

  const handleQuickLoginAdmin = async () => {
    clearMessages();
    setLoginEmail('admin@findaffinity.com');
    setLoginPassword('AdminPass2026!');
    setLoading(true);

    const res = await loginApi('admin@findaffinity.com', 'AdminPass2026!');
    setLoading(false);
    if (res.success) {
      onAuthSuccess(res.user);
    } else {
      setErrorMessage(res.error || 'No se pudo iniciar sesión con la cuenta de administrador.');
    }
  };

  const handleQuickLoginDemo = async () => {
    clearMessages();
    setLoginEmail('auditor@konigpartners.es');
    setLoginPassword('AuditPass2026!');
    setLoading(true);

    const res = await loginApi('auditor@konigpartners.es', 'AuditPass2026!');
    setLoading(false);
    if (res.success) {
      onAuthSuccess(res.user);
    } else {
      setErrorMessage(res.error || 'No se pudo iniciar sesión con el despacho demo.');
    }
  };

  const handleForgotSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    clearMessages();
    setLoading(true);

    const res = await forgotPasswordApi(forgotEmail);
    setLoading(false);

    if (res.success) {
      setInfoMessage(res.message || 'Se ha generado un token de recuperación.');
      if (res.demoToken) {
        setResetToken(res.demoToken);
        setActiveTab('reset');
      }
    } else {
      setErrorMessage(res.error || 'Error en la solicitud.');
    }
  };

  const handleResetSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    clearMessages();
    setLoading(true);

    const res = await resetPasswordApi(resetToken, resetNewPassword);
    setLoading(false);

    if (res.success) {
      setInfoMessage('Contraseña restablecida exitosamente. Inicia sesión con tu nueva clave.');
      setActiveTab('login');
    } else {
      setErrorMessage(res.error || 'Error al restablecer contraseña.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#08080a] flex flex-col justify-between text-zinc-100 font-sans selection:bg-emerald-500/30">
      {/* Top Brand Nav */}
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-800/80 bg-[#0c0c0e]/90 backdrop-blur px-6 py-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-400">
            <Layers className="h-4 w-4" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-bold text-base tracking-tight text-white font-sans">
            FINDAFINITY
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 font-mono hidden sm:inline ml-1 border-l border-zinc-800 pl-2">
            CONCILIACIÓN CONTABLE B2B
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <span className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${backendOnline ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-zinc-600'}`} />
          <span className="text-zinc-400 font-mono text-[11px]">
            {backendOnline ? 'Servidor Conectado' : 'Modo Autónomo'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Centered Container */}
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-10">
        <div className="max-w-[480px] w-full bg-[#101013] border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl relative">
          
          {/* Logo & Headline */}
          <div className="text-center mb-6">
            <div className="inline-flex h-11 w-11 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 items-center justify-center text-emerald-400 mb-3 shadow-sm">
              <Layers className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white font-sans">
              FINDAFINITY
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
              Plataforma de Conciliación Contable, Bancaria y Pasarelas
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Large Tab Switcher */}
          {(activeTab === 'login' || activeTab === 'register') && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 p-1 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg mb-6 text-xs font-medium">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleTabChange('login')}
                className={`py-2 rounded-md transition-colors ${
                  activeTab === 'login'
                    ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                }`}
              >
                Iniciar Sesión
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleTabChange('register')}
                className={`py-2 rounded-md transition-colors ${
                  activeTab === 'register'
                    ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                }`}
              >
                Registrar Despacho / Empresa
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Messages */}
          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="mb-5 p-3 rounded-md bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/60 text-rose-300 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-rose-400" />
              <span>{errorMessage}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {infoMessage && (
            <div className="mb-5 p-3 rounded-md bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800/60 text-emerald-300 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>{infoMessage}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* VISTA 1: INICIAR SESIÓN */}
          {activeTab === 'login' && (
            <form onSubmit={handleLoginSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1.5">
                  Correo Electrónico
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={loginEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setLoginEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="auditor@tudespacho.es"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                  <label className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium">
                    Contraseña
                  </label>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleTabChange('forgot')}
                    className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 hover:text-emerald-400 transition-colors"
                  >
                    ¿Olvidaste tu contraseña?
                  </button>
                </div>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    value={loginPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setLoginPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="••••••••••••"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full mt-2 py-2.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 active:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {loading ? 'Accediendo...' : 'Iniciar Sesión en el Despacho'}
                <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </button>

              {/* Botones de Acceso Rápido */}
              <div className="pt-4 border-t border-zinc-800/80 space-y-2">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider text-center font-medium">
                  Accesos Directos de Evaluación
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleQuickLoginAdmin}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-1.5 px-3 rounded-md bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800/80 border border-blue-900/50 text-[11px] font-medium text-blue-300 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-blue-400" />
                  <span>Acceso Rápido: Cuenta ADMIN (Ver Todas las Compañías)</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleQuickLoginDemo}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-1.5 px-3 rounded-md bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800/80 border border-zinc-800 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-300 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                  <span>Acceso Rápido: Despacho Auditor Demo (KÖNIG & ASOCIADOS)</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}

          {/* VISTA 2: REGISTRAR DESPACHO / EMPRESA */}
          {activeTab === 'register' && (
            <form onSubmit={handleRegisterSubmit} className="space-y-3.5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                  Nombre Completo del Auditor / Administrador
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <User className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={regFullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegFullName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Carlos Méndez"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                  Correo Electrónico Profesional
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={regEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="carlos@despachomendez.es"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                  Contraseña (mínimo 6 caracteres)
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    minLength={6}
                    value={regPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="••••••••••••"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                    Nombre del Despacho / Empresa
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Building2 className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={regCompanyName}
                      onChange={(e) => setRegCompanyName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Méndez & Asociados SLP"
                      className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                    CIF / NIF Fiscal
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={regFiscalId}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegFiscalId(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                    placeholder="B-12345678"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 font-mono placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full mt-3 py-2.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 active:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {loading ? 'Creando Despacho...' : 'Crear Cuenta y Acceder a FINDAFINITY'}
                <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </button>

              <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 text-center mt-2">
                Al registrarte se crea automáticamente el espacio de tu despacho con rol de Administrador.
              </div>
            </form>
          )}

          {/* VISTA 3: RECUPERAR CONTRASEÑA */}
          {activeTab === 'forgot' && (
            <form onSubmit={handleForgotSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mb-2">
                Introduce el correo electrónico asociado a tu cuenta para recibir las instrucciones de restablecimiento.
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1.5">
                  Correo Electrónico
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={forgotEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setForgotEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="auditor@tudespacho.es"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleTabChange('login')}
                  className="w-1/3 py-2 rounded-md bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-800 text-xs text-zinc-300 transition-colors"
                >
                  Volver
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-2/3 py-2 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Enviando...' : 'Solicitar Token'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}

          {/* VISTA 4: RESTABLECER CONTRASEÑA */}
          {activeTab === 'reset' && (
            <form onSubmit={handleResetSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1.5">
                  Token de Restablecimiento
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <KeyRound className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={resetToken}
                    onChange={(e) => setResetToken(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Pega el token recibido"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 font-mono placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1.5">
                  Nueva Contraseña
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    minLength={6}
                    value={resetNewPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setResetNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="••••••••••••"
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleTabChange('login')}
                  className="w-1/3 py-2 rounded-md bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-800 text-xs text-zinc-300 transition-colors"
                >
                  Volver
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-2/3 py-2 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Restableciendo...' : 'Guardar Nueva Contraseña'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}

        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-zinc-800/80 bg-[#0c0c0e]/80 py-3.5 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500">
        <div className="max-w-[1200px] mx-auto px-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px]">
          <span>FINDAFINITY &bull; Zero-Liability Audit Engine &bull; Cuadre PGC a 0.00 €</span>
          <span>Soporte Oficial: Wolters Kluwer A3 &bull; Sage 50 &bull; DELSOL Contasol</span>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\ClientModal.jsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Building2, X, Plus, Trash2, AlertTriangle, Check } from 'lucide-react';

export function ClientModal({
  isOpen,
  mode = 'add', // 'add' | 'delete'
  company = null,
  onClose,
  onAddClient,
  onDeleteClient,
}) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [fiscalId, setFiscalId] = useState('');
  const [defaultErp, setDefaultErp] = useState('A3');
  const [accountLength, setAccountLength] = useState(9);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleAddSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setError('El nombre de la empresa es obligatorio.');
      return;
    }
    if (!fiscalId.trim()) {
      setError('El CIF / NIF es obligatorio.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onAddClient({
        name: name.trim(),
        fiscalId: fiscalId.trim().toUpperCase(),
        defaultErp,
        accountLength: parseInt(accountLength, 10) || 9,
      });
      setName('');
      setFiscalId('');
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || 'Error al guardar la empresa cliente');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = async () => {
    if (!company) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onDeleteClient(company.id);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || 'Error al eliminar la empresa cliente');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-[#121215] border border-zinc-800 rounded-xl max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-1 rounded-md text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
        >
          <X className="h-4 w-4" />
        </button>

        {mode === 'add' ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 mb-4">
              <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400">
                <Building2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100">
                  Añadir Empresa Cliente
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                  Nueva sociedad o negocio para gestionar su contabilidad
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="mb-4 p-2.5 rounded bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/60 text-rose-300 text-xs">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleAddSubmit} className="space-y-3.5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                  Razón Social / Nombre Comercial
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="ej. Distribuciones Mediterráneo SL"
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-700"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                  NIF / CIF Fiscal
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="ej. B-88776655"
                  value={fiscalId}
                  onChange={(e) => setFiscalId(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 font-mono uppercase focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-700"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                    ERP Contable
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={defaultErp}
                    onChange={(e) => setDefaultErp(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-700"
                  >
                    <option value="A3">Wolters Kluwer A3</option>
                    <option value="SAGE">Sage 50</option>
                    <option value="CONTASOL">DELSOL Contasol</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-medium mb-1">
                    Dígitos Subcuenta
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={accountLength}
                    onChange={(e) => setAccountLength(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-700"
                  >
                    <option value={8}>8 dígitos</option>
                    <option value={9}>9 dígitos</option>
                    <option value={10}>10 dígitos</option>
                    <option value={12}>12 dígitos</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-800/80">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-800 text-xs text-zinc-300 transition-colors"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors shadow-sm"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Guardando...' : 'Crear Empresa Cliente'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 mb-4">
              <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-rose-400">
                <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100">
                  Eliminar Empresa Cliente
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                  Acción irreversible de supresión contable
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="mb-4 p-2.5 rounded bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/60 text-rose-300 text-xs">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="bg-zinc-900/60 border border-zinc-800/80 rounded-lg p-3 mb-4">
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-200">
                {company?.name}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                CIF: {company?.fiscalId || '—'} • ERP: {company?.defaultErp || 'A3'}
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
              ¿Estás seguro de que deseas eliminar esta empresa cliente? Se borrarán todos los lotes de conciliación, movimientos y asientos exportables asociados a ella.
            </p>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-800/80">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-800 text-xs text-zinc-300 transition-colors"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDeleteConfirm}
                disabled={loading}
                className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{loading ? 'Eliminando...' : 'Eliminar Empresa'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\DoubleEntryViewer.jsx
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, ArrowRight, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatEuro, formatEuroNoSymbol } from '../utils/formatters';

export function DoubleEntryViewer({ journal, transactionId }) {
  if (!journal || !journal.lines || journal.lines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="p-3 text-xs text-zinc-400 bg-zinc-950/60 rounded border border-zinc-800">
        No se ha generado pre-asiento para esta partida aún.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const totalDebit = journal.lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + (Number(line.debitEur) || 0), 0);
  const totalCredit = journal.lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + (Number(line.creditEur) || 0), 0);
  const imbalance = Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit);
  const isPerfectSquare = imbalance < 0.005;

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#0e0e11] border border-zinc-800/90 rounded-md p-3.5 my-2 text-xs shadow-inner">
      {/* Header of Entry */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between pb-2 mb-2.5 border-b border-zinc-800/60 gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <BookOpen className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400" />
          <span className="font-semibold text-zinc-200">
            Pre-Asiento de Partida Doble PGC
          </span>
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 border border-zinc-700/40">
            Asiento #{String(journal.entryNumber || 1).padStart(4, '0')} &bull; Fecha: {journal.date}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Verification Checksum */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px]">
          {isPerfectSquare ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-400 font-medium">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Cuadre PGC: 0,00 €
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-rose-400 font-medium">
              <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Descuadre detectado: {formatEuro(imbalance)}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Accounting Lines Table */}
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-zinc-800/70 text-[10px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
              <th className="py-1.5 px-2">Subcuenta</th>
              <th className="py-1.5 px-2">Concepto Contable Oficial</th>
              <th className="py-1.5 px-2 text-right">Debe (€)</th>
              <th className="py-1.5 px-2 text-right">Haber (€)</th>
              <th className="py-1.5 px-2 text-center">Contrapartida</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-900 font-mono text-[11px]">
            {journal.lines.map((line, idx) => {
              const isDebit = Number(line.debitEur) > 0;
              const isFee = line.accountCode.startsWith('626') || line.accountCode.startsWith('669');
              const isBank = line.accountCode.startsWith('572');
              const isCustomer = line.accountCode.startsWith('430');

              return (
                <tr 
                  key={idx}
                  className="hover:bg-zinc-900/40 transition-colors"
                >
                  {/* Account Code */}
                  <td className="py-1.5 px-2 font-medium">
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] ${
                      isBank ? 'bg-blue-950/40 text-blue-300 border border-blue-900/50' :
                      isFee ? 'bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-300 border border-amber-900/50' :
                      isCustomer ? 'bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-900/50' :
                      'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300'
                    }`}>
                      {line.accountCode}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  {/* Concept */}
                  <td className="py-1.5 px-2 text-zinc-300 font-sans text-xs">
                    {line.concept}
                  </td>

                  {/* Debit */}
                  <td className="py-1.5 px-2 text-right tabular-nums">
                    {Number(line.debitEur) > 0 ? (
                      <span className="text-zinc-100 font-semibold">
                        {formatEuroNoSymbol(line.debitEur)}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-zinc-600">0,00</span>
                    )}
                  </td>

                  {/* Credit */}
                  <td className="py-1.5 px-2 text-right tabular-nums">
                    {Number(line.creditEur) > 0 ? (
                      <span className="text-zinc-100 font-semibold">
                        {formatEuroNoSymbol(line.creditEur)}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-zinc-600">0,00</span>
                    )}
                  </td>

                  {/* Contrapartida */}
                  <td className="py-1.5 px-2 text-center text-zinc-400 text-[10px]">
                    {line.contrapartida || '—'}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>

          {/* Table Footer: Sum Totals */}
          <tfoot>
            <tr className="border-t border-zinc-700/80 bg-zinc-950/80 font-mono font-semibold text-[11px]">
              <td colSpan={2} className="py-2 px-2 text-right uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-sans">
                Totales de Control Contable:
              </td>
              <td className="py-2 px-2 text-right tabular-nums text-zinc-100">
                {formatEuroNoSymbol(totalDebit)} €
              </td>
              <td className="py-2 px-2 text-right tabular-nums text-zinc-100">
                {formatEuroNoSymbol(totalCredit)} €
              </td>
              <td className="py-2 px-2 text-center">
                <span className={`inline-block px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono ${
                  isPerfectSquare ? 'text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/40' : 'text-rose-400 bg-rose-950/40'
                }`}>
                  Δ {imbalance.toFixed(2)} €
                </span>
              </td>
            </tr>
          </tfoot>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\ExportPanel.jsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { 
  Download, 
  Copy, 
  Check, 
  FileCode, 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  Database, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Eye,
  FileCheck2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { 
  generateA3Suenlace, 
  generateSageXdiario, 
  generateContasolXlsx,
  downloadTextFile, 
  downloadBinaryFile 
} from '../utils/exporters';
import { formatEuro } from '../utils/formatters';

export function ExportPanel({
  transactions,
  selectedErp,
  onSelectErp,
  selectedCompany,
  batchId,
}) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [downloadSuccess, setDownloadSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Filtrar partidas conciliables (Verdes o aprobadas)
  const validJournals = useMemo(() => {
    return transactions
      .filter(tx => (tx.status === 'GREEN' || tx.isApproved) && tx.journal)
      .map(tx => tx.journal);
  }, [transactions]);

  // Totales contables
  const auditMetrics = useMemo(() => {
    let totalLines = 0;
    let totalDebit = 0;
    let totalCredit = 0;

    validJournals.forEach(j => {
      j.lines.forEach(l => {
        totalLines += 1;
        totalDebit += Number(l.debitEur) || 0;
        totalCredit += Number(l.creditEur) || 0;
      });
    });

    return {
      entriesCount: validJournals.length,
      linesCount: totalLines,
      totalDebit,
      totalCredit,
      imbalance: Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit),
    };
  }, [validJournals]);

  // Previsualización de texto según ERP
  const previewText = useMemo(() => {
    if (validJournals.length === 0) {
      return '// Esperando asientos conciliados para exportar.';
    }

    const accLen = selectedCompany?.accountLength || 9;
    if (selectedErp === 'A3') {
      return generateA3Suenlace(validJournals, accLen);
    }
    if (selectedErp === 'SAGE') {
      return generateSageXdiario(validJournals, accLen);
    }
    // Para Contasol se muestra la estructura CSV correspondiente
    const header = 'Diario;Fecha;Subcuenta;Concepto;Documento;Debe;Haber';
    const lines = [header];
    validJournals.forEach(j => {
      const [y, m, d] = j.date.split('-');
      const dateFormatted = `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
      j.lines.forEach(l => {
        lines.push(`${j.entryNumber || 1};${dateFormatted};${l.accountCode};${l.concept};${l.documentNumber};${Number(l.debitEur).toFixed(2)};${Number(l.creditEur).toFixed(2)}`);
      });
    });
    return lines.join('\r\n');
  }, [validJournals, selectedErp, selectedCompany]);

  // Manejador de descarga de archivo oficial (prioriza backend si hay lote activo)
  const handleDownload = async () => {
    if (validJournals.length === 0) return;

    if (batchId) {
      try {
        const url = `http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1/batches/${batchId}/export/${selectedErp}?actor=auditor@gestoria.es`;
        const res = await fetch(url);
        if (res.ok) {
          const blob = await res.blob();
          const disposition = res.headers.get('content-disposition');
          let fname = `EXPORT_${((selectedCompany?.fiscalId || 'CLIENT')).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '')}_${selectedErp}`;
          if (disposition && disposition.includes('filename=')) {
            fname = disposition.split('filename=')[1].replace(/"/g, '').trim();
          } else {
            fname += selectedErp === 'CONTASOL' ? '.xlsx' : selectedErp === 'SAGE' ? '.csv' : '.txt';
          }
          const blobUrl = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          const a = document.createElement('a');
          a.href = blobUrl;
          a.download = fname;
          document.body.appendChild(a);
          a.click();
          a.remove();
          window.URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
          setDownloadSuccess(true);
          setTimeout(() => setDownloadSuccess(false), 2500);
          return;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Descargando mediante generador autónomo en cliente:', err);
      }
    }

    const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, '');
    const clientCode = ((selectedCompany?.fiscalId || 'CLIENT')).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '');

    if (selectedErp === 'A3') {
      const filename = `SUENLACE_${clientCode}_${dateStr}.txt`;
      downloadTextFile(previewText, filename, 'text/plain;charset=windows-1252');
    } else if (selectedErp === 'SAGE') {
      const filename = `XDIARIO_${clientCode}_${dateStr}.csv`;
      downloadTextFile(previewText, filename, 'text/csv;charset=windows-1252');
    } else if (selectedErp === 'CONTASOL') {
      const filename = `CONTASOL_DIARIO_${clientCode}_${dateStr}.xlsx`;
      const binaryData = generateContasolXlsx(validJournals, selectedCompany?.accountLength || 9);
      downloadBinaryFile(binaryData, filename, 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet');
    }

    setDownloadSuccess(true);
    setTimeout(() => setDownloadSuccess(false), 2500);
  };

  const handleCopy = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(previewText);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#101013] border border-zinc-800/80 rounded-lg p-4 sm:p-5">
      {/* Panel Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-zinc-800/50">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Database className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-400" />
            Exportación Oficial a ERP de Contabilidad
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            Generación certificada de asientos auditados sin descuadre para inyección directa en el software contable.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* ERP Switcher Tabs */}
        <div className="inline-flex rounded-md p-1 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 self-start md:self-auto">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSelectErp('A3')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              selectedErp === 'A3'
                ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            Wolters Kluwer A3 (Suenlace)
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSelectErp('SAGE')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              selectedErp === 'SAGE'
                ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            Sage 50 (XDIARIO)
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSelectErp('CONTASOL')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              selectedErp === 'CONTASOL'
                ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            DELSOL Contasol (.xlsx)
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Audit Stats Banner */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-4">
        <div className="bg-zinc-900/60 border border-zinc-800/60 rounded p-2.5">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Asientos a Emitir</div>
          <div className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 mt-0.5">
            {auditMetrics.entriesCount}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-zinc-900/60 border border-zinc-800/60 rounded p-2.5">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Total Líneas Diario</div>
          <div className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 mt-0.5">
            {auditMetrics.linesCount}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-zinc-900/60 border border-zinc-800/60 rounded p-2.5">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Volumen Contabilizado</div>
          <div className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 mt-0.5">
            {formatEuro(auditMetrics.totalDebit)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-zinc-900/60 border border-zinc-800/60 rounded p-2.5">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Estado de Cuadre PGC</div>
          <div className={`font-mono text-sm font-semibold mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 ${
            auditMetrics.imbalance < 0.005 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'
          }`}>
            <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            {auditMetrics.imbalance < 0.005 ? 'Cuadre 0,00 €' : `Descuadre ${formatEuro(auditMetrics.imbalance)}`}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Code / Content Live Preview */}
      <div className="border border-zinc-800 rounded-md overflow-hidden bg-[#0c0c0e]">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2 border-b border-zinc-800/80 bg-zinc-950/70 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-400">
            <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-300">
              {selectedErp === 'A3' ? 'SUENLACE_A3.txt (Ancho Fijo / Delimitado)' :
               selectedErp === 'SAGE' ? 'XDIARIO.csv (Estructura Sage 50)' :
               'CONTASOL_DIARIO.xlsx (Vista Previa Estructurada)'}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopy}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-700/60 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 font-medium transition-colors"
            >
              {copied ? <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />}
              <span>{copied ? 'Copiado' : 'Copiar'}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleDownload}
              disabled={validJournals.length === 0}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-white text-zinc-900 text-[11px] font-semibold transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              {downloadSuccess ? (
                <FileCheck2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
              ) : selectedErp === 'CONTASOL' ? (
                <FileSpreadsheet className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              ) : (
                <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              )}
              <span>{downloadSuccess ? 'Descargado' : 'Descargar 1-Clic'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Live Monospace Text Box */}
        <div className="p-3 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre select-all bg-[#08080a]">
          {previewText}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\HeaderNav.jsx
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import { 
  Building2, 
  Database, 
  Layers,
  ShieldCheck,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  LogOut,
  User as UserIcon
} from 'lucide-react';

export function HeaderNav({
  companies = [],
  selectedCompany = null,
  onSelectCompany,
  selectedErp = 'A3',
  onSelectErp,
  currentUser,
  onLogout,
  currentView = 'radar',
  onToggleView,
  onOpenAddClientModal,
  onOpenDeleteClientModal,
}) {
  const isSuperAdmin = currentUser?.role === 'SUPERADMIN';
  const isAdminView = currentView === 'admin';

  return (
    <header className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-[#0c0c0e] sticky top-0 z-40 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="max-w-[1600px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-3 gap-4">
          
          {/* Lado Izquierdo: Marca, Conmutador y Contexto de Trabajo */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4">
            {/* Logo & App Name */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 mr-1">
              <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-400">
                <Layers className="h-4 w-4" />
              </div>
              <span className="font-bold text-base tracking-tight text-white font-sans">
                FINDAFINITY
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Selector de Vistas EXCLUSIVO para SUPERADMIN */}
            {isSuperAdmin && onToggleView && (
              <>
                <div className="hidden sm:block h-5 w-px bg-zinc-800" />
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md p-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onToggleView('radar')}
                    className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                      !isAdminView
                        ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-white shadow-sm'
                        : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Radar Conciliación
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onToggleView('admin')}
                    className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                      isAdminView
                        ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                        : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-blue-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>Licencias & Empresas</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </>
            )}

            {/* CONTEXTO DE CONCILIACIÓN: Se oculta completamente cuando estamos en el Panel de Administrador Global */}
            {!isAdminView && (
              <>
                <div className="hidden sm:block h-5 w-px bg-zinc-800" />

                {/* Selector de Empresa Cliente con botones de Añadir (+) y Quitar (-) */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5">
                  <Building2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400 shrink-0" />
                  
                  {companies && companies.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="flex flex-col">
                        <span className="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 leading-none mb-0.5">
                          Empresa Cliente
                        </span>
                        <select
                          value={selectedCompany ? selectedCompany.id : ''}
                          onChange={(e) => {
                            const comp = companies.find(c => c.id === e.target.value);
                            if (comp && onSelectCompany) onSelectCompany(comp);
                          }}
                          className="bg-transparent text-xs text-zinc-100 font-medium focus:outline-none cursor-pointer pr-2 max-w-[210px] truncate"
                        >
                          {companies.map((c) => (
                            <option key={c.id} value={c.id} className="bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-200">
                              {c.name} {c.fiscalId ? `(${c.fiscalId})` : ''}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>

                      {/* Botón Añadir Empresa (+) */}
                      {onOpenAddClientModal && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={onOpenAddClientModal}
                          className="p-1 rounded bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                          title="Añadir nueva Empresa Cliente"
                        >
                          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      )}

                      {/* Botón Quitar Empresa (-) */}
                      {selectedCompany && onOpenDeleteClientModal && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onOpenDeleteClientModal(selectedCompany)}
                          className="p-1 rounded bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-rose-950/80 border border-transparent hover:border-rose-800/60 text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-400 transition-colors"
                          title={`Eliminar empresa cliente ${selectedCompany.name}`}
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400 italic">
                        Sin empresas cliente
                      </span>
                      {onOpenAddClientModal && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={onOpenAddClientModal}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white text-[11px] font-semibold transition-colors shadow-sm"
                        >
                          <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
                          <span>Añadir Empresa</span>
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Selector ERP Destino */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5">
                  <Database className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400 shrink-0" />
                  <div className="flex flex-col">
                    <span className="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 leading-none mb-0.5">
                      Exportar a
                    </span>
                    <select
                      value={selectedErp}
                      onChange={(e) => onSelectErp && onSelectErp(e.target.value)}
                      className="bg-transparent text-xs text-zinc-100 font-medium focus:outline-none cursor-pointer pr-2"
                    >
                      <option value="A3" className="bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-200">
                        Wolters Kluwer A3
                      </option>
                      <option value="SAGE" className="bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-200">
                        Sage 50 / Despachos
                      </option>
                      <option value="CONTASOL" className="bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-200">
                        DELSOL Contasol
                      </option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Lado Derecho: Usuario y Salida */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {currentUser && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pl-3 border-l border-zinc-800">
                <div className="hidden sm:flex flex-col text-right">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-200">
                    {currentUser.full_name || currentUser.email}
                  </span>
                  <span className={`text-[10px] font-sans font-medium ${
                    isSuperAdmin ? 'text-blue-400 font-semibold' : 'text-zinc-400'
                  }`}>
                    {currentUser.role === 'SUPERADMIN' ? 'Administrador Global' :
                     currentUser.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'Administrador' :
                     currentUser.role === 'AUDITOR' ? 'Auditor Contable' : 'Consulta'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onLogout}
                  className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors"
                  title="Cerrar sesión"
                >
                  <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\IngestionZone.jsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { 
  UploadCloud, 
  FileText, 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  Sparkles, 
  Check, 
  AlertCircle, 
  RefreshCw,
  Layers
} from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadNorma43File } from '../services/api';

export function IngestionZone({
  onLoadDemoBatch,
  onFileProcessed,
  isProcessing,
}) {
  const [isDragOver, setIsDragOver] = useState(false);
  const [ingestionTelemetry, setIngestionTelemetry] = useState(null);
  const [localProcessing, setLocalProcessing] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

  const handleDragOver = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragOver(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragOver(false);
  };

  const handleDrop = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragOver(false);
    const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
    if (files.length > 0) {
      processSelectedFile(files[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files.length > 0) {
      processSelectedFile(e.target.files[0]);
    }
  };

  const processSelectedFile = async (file) => {
    setLocalProcessing(true);
    setIngestionTelemetry({
      filename: file.name,
      sizeKb: (file.size / 1024).toFixed(1),
      movements: 'Analizando...',
      isValid: true,
