      errors: [],
    });

    try {
      if (onFileProcessed) {
        const result = await onFileProcessed(file);
        if (result && result.total_movements !== undefined) {
          setIngestionTelemetry({
            filename: file.name,
            sizeKb: (file.size / 1024).toFixed(1),
            movements: `${result.total_movements} detectados`,
            isValid: true,
            errors: [],
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setIngestionTelemetry({
        filename: file.name,
        sizeKb: (file.size / 1024).toFixed(1),
        movements: 0,
        isValid: false,
        errors: [err.message || 'Error al procesar el archivo contable.'],
      });
    } finally {
      setLocalProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#101013] border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg p-4 sm:p-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-4 pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800/60">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100">
            Importar Extractos y Ventas
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            Sube extractos bancarios o reportes de cobros para conciliarlos automáticamente.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Botón de datos de prueba */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onLoadDemoBatch}
          disabled={isProcessing || localProcessing}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700/80 active:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-700/60 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-100 transition-colors shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-400" />
          Cargar Lote de Prueba
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {/* Dropzone */}
        <div
          onDragOver={handleDragOver}
          onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
          onDrop={handleDrop}
          onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
          className={`lg:col-span-2 border border-dashed rounded-md p-5 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
            isDragOver 
              ? 'border-zinc-500 bg-zinc-900/80' 
              : 'border-zinc-800 hover:border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900/30 hover:bg-zinc-900/50'
          }`}
        >
          <input
            ref={fileInputRef}
            type="file"
            onChange={handleFileChange}
            accept=".n43,.txt,.csv,.xlsx"
            className="hidden"
          />

          <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-full bg-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-400 mb-2">
            {localProcessing ? (
              <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-zinc-300" />
            ) : (
              <UploadCloud className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-300" />
            )}
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-200 font-medium">
            Arrastra aquí tus archivos o haz clic para seleccionarlos
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 mt-1">
            Formatos soportados: .n43, .csv, .xlsx, .txt
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Estado / Resumen de Carga */}
        <div className="bg-zinc-900/50 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 font-medium pb-2 border-b border-zinc-800/60 mb-2.5">
              ESTADO DEL ARCHIVO
            </div>

            {ingestionTelemetry ? (
              <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-zinc-400">Archivo:</span>
                  <span className="text-zinc-200 font-mono text-[11px] truncate max-w-[150px]" title={ingestionTelemetry.filename}>
                    {ingestionTelemetry.filename}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-zinc-400">Tamaño:</span>
                  <span className="text-zinc-200 font-mono text-[11px]">
                    {ingestionTelemetry.sizeKb} KB
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-zinc-400">Movimientos:</span>
                  <span className="text-zinc-200 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium text-emerald-400">
                    {ingestionTelemetry.movements} detectados
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-2 border-t border-zinc-800/60">
                  {ingestionTelemetry.isValid ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-emerald-400">
                      <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      <span>Archivo verificado correctamente</span>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-amber-400">
                      <AlertCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      <span>{ingestionTelemetry.errors[0] || 'Aviso en la lectura'}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="py-6 text-center text-zinc-400 text-xs">
                <p>Ningún archivo seleccionado.</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] mt-1 text-zinc-400">
                  Puedes cargar el lote de prueba para ver el funcionamiento.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\PeriodFilter.jsx
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import { Calendar, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

const MONTHS = [
  { value: 1, label: 'Enero' },
  { value: 2, label: 'Febrero' },
  { value: 3, label: 'Marzo' },
  { value: 4, label: 'Abril' },
  { value: 5, label: 'Mayo' },
  { value: 6, label: 'Junio' },
  { value: 7, label: 'Julio' },
  { value: 8, label: 'Agosto' },
  { value: 9, label: 'Septiembre' },
  { value: 10, label: 'Octubre' },
  { value: 11, label: 'Noviembre' },
  { value: 12, label: 'Diciembre' },
];

export function PeriodFilter({
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  periodType,        // 'ALL' | 'Q1' | 'Q2' | 'Q3' | 'Q4' | 'CUSTOM'
  onSelectPeriodType,
  startMonth,
  onSelectStartMonth,
  endMonth,
  onSelectEndMonth,
}) {
  const handlePresetChange = (preset) => {
    onSelectPeriodType(preset);
    if (preset === 'ALL') {
      onSelectStartMonth(1);
      onSelectEndMonth(12);
    } else if (preset === 'Q1') {
      onSelectStartMonth(1);
      onSelectEndMonth(3);
    } else if (preset === 'Q2') {
      onSelectStartMonth(4);
      onSelectEndMonth(6);
    } else if (preset === 'Q3') {
      onSelectStartMonth(7);
      onSelectEndMonth(9);
    } else if (preset === 'Q4') {
      onSelectStartMonth(10);
      onSelectEndMonth(12);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 bg-[#0d0d10] border-b border-zinc-800/80 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs">
      {/* Icono de Calendario */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-zinc-400 font-medium pl-1">
        <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400" />
        <span>Periodo:</span>
      </div>

      {/* Selector de Año */}
      <select
        value={selectedYear}
        onChange={(e) => onSelectYear(Number(e.target.value))}
        className="bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-700/80 rounded px-2.5 py-1 text-zinc-100 font-medium focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
      >
        <option value={2026}>2026</option>
        <option value={2025}>2025</option>
        <option value={2024}>2024</option>
      </select>

      {/* Botones de Preajustes Trimestrales / Anual */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-zinc-950 p-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-800">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handlePresetChange('ALL')}
          className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${
            periodType === 'ALL'
              ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
              : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
          }`}
        >
          Todo el año
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handlePresetChange('Q1')}
          className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${
            periodType === 'Q1'
              ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
              : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
          }`}
        >
          1T
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handlePresetChange('Q2')}
          className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${
            periodType === 'Q2'
              ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
              : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
          }`}
        >
          2T
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handlePresetChange('Q3')}
          className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${
            periodType === 'Q3'
              ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
              : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
          }`}
        >
          3T
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handlePresetChange('Q4')}
          className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${
            periodType === 'Q4'
              ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
              : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
          }`}
        >
          4T
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onSelectPeriodType('CUSTOM')}
          className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors ${
            periodType === 'CUSTOM'
              ? 'bg-emerald-950/60 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800/60'
              : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
          }`}
        >
          Personalizado
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Selectores de Meses Personalizados (ej. Desde Abril hasta Julio) */}
      {periodType === 'CUSTOM' && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pl-1">
          <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">Desde:</span>
          <select
            value={startMonth}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const val = Number(e.target.value);
              onSelectStartMonth(val);
              if (val > endMonth) onSelectEndMonth(val);
            }}
            className="bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-700/80 rounded px-2 py-1 text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer text-xs"
          >
            {MONTHS.map((m) => (
              <option key={m.value} value={m.value}>
                {m.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">Hasta:</span>
          <select
            value={endMonth}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const val = Number(e.target.value);
              onSelectEndMonth(val);
              if (val < startMonth) onSelectStartMonth(val);
            }}
            className="bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-700/80 rounded px-2 py-1 text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer text-xs"
          >
            {MONTHS.map((m) => (
              <option key={m.value} value={m.value}>
                {m.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\ScoreRadarModal.jsx
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import { X, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatPercent, formatEuro } from '../utils/formatters';

export function ScoreRadarModal({ transaction, onClose, onApprove }) {
  if (!transaction) return null;

  const dims = transaction.dimensions || {
    s_importe: 1.0,
    s_fecha: 0.9,
    s_texto: 0.8,
    s_fiscal: 0.85,
  };

  const getStatusBadge = () => {
    switch (transaction.status) {
      case 'GREEN':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800/60 text-emerald-400 text-xs font-medium">
            <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Conciliado Automático
          </span>
        );
      case 'YELLOW':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-800/60 text-amber-400 text-xs font-medium">
            <AlertCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Pendiente de Aprobación
          </span>
        );
      case 'RED':
      default:
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/60 text-rose-400 text-xs font-medium">
            <HelpCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Sin Coincidencia
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div 
        className="bg-[#121215] border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg max-w-lg w-full shadow-2xl overflow-hidden"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100">
            Detalle de la Coincidencia
          </h3>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content */}
        <div className="p-5 space-y-4 text-xs">
          {/* Main Score Summary */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-md bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                Nivel de Coincidencia
              </span>
              <div className="text-2xl font-mono font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-100 mt-0.5">
                {formatPercent(transaction.confidenceScore)}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>{getStatusBadge()}</div>
          </div>

          {/* Reference Info */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5 p-3 rounded bg-zinc-950/60 border border-zinc-800/60 text-xs">
            <div className="flex justify-between">
              <span className="text-zinc-400">Referencia:</span>
              <span className="text-zinc-200 font-mono text-[11px]">{transaction.id}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between">
              <span className="text-zinc-400">Importe Neto:</span>
              <span className="text-zinc-100 font-mono font-semibold">{formatEuro(transaction.netAmountEur)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between">
              <span className="text-zinc-400">Concepto:</span>
              <span className="text-zinc-200 truncate max-w-[280px]" title={transaction.concept}>
                {transaction.concept}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Criterios de Evaluación Claros */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-semibold block">
              Comprobaciones Realizadas
            </span>

            {/* 1. Importe */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
                <span className="text-zinc-200 font-medium">1. Importe y Comisiones</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100 font-semibold">{formatPercent(dims.s_importe)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-zinc-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div 
                  className="h-full bg-emerald-500 rounded-full" 
                  style={{ width: `${dims.s_importe * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                El total ingresado en el banco coincide con la suma de las facturas menos las tarifas aplicadas.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* 2. Fecha */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
                <span className="text-zinc-200 font-medium">2. Fecha de Operación</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100 font-semibold">{formatPercent(dims.s_fecha)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-zinc-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div 
                  className="h-full bg-emerald-500 rounded-full" 
                  style={{ width: `${dims.s_fecha * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                El cobro se ha recibido dentro del plazo habitual de liquidación de la entidad.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* 3. Cliente y Factura */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
                <span className="text-zinc-200 font-medium">3. Cliente y Documento</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100 font-semibold">{formatPercent(dims.s_texto)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-zinc-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div 
                  className="h-full bg-emerald-500 rounded-full" 
                  style={{ width: `${dims.s_texto * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                Identificación del nombre del cliente o número de factura en el texto del extracto.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* 4. CIF / NIF */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
                <span className="text-zinc-200 font-medium">4. NIF / CIF</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100 font-semibold">{formatPercent(dims.s_fiscal)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-zinc-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div 
                  className="h-full bg-emerald-500 rounded-full" 
                  style={{ width: `${dims.s_fiscal * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                Identificador fiscal comprobado y contrastado con la cartera de clientes.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Resumen Final */}
          <div className="p-3 rounded bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 text-xs text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed">
            <span className="font-semibold text-zinc-100 block mb-0.5">Estado:</span>
            {transaction.status === 'GREEN' && 'La partida está totalmente identificada y cuadrada a céntimo cero. Lista para exportar al ERP.'}
            {transaction.status === 'YELLOW' && 'Existe alta probabilidad de coincidencia pero se recomienda validar con un clic antes de enviar al libro diario.'}
            {transaction.status === 'RED' && 'No se ha encontrado una factura correspondiente o el importe presenta una diferencia significativa. Requiere revisión manual.'}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 flex items-center justify-between">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-700 text-xs text-zinc-300 font-medium transition-colors"
          >
            Cerrar
          </button>

          {transaction.status === 'YELLOW' && onApprove && (
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                onApprove(transaction.id);
                onClose();
              }}
              className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
            >
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Aprobar Asiento
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\StagingRadar.jsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { 
  Search, 
  Filter, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle, 
  AlertOctagon, 
  Layers, 
  ChevronDown,
  CheckCheck,
  Sparkles,
  Trash2,
  Plus,
  Building2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { TransactionRow } from './TransactionRow';
import { PeriodFilter } from './PeriodFilter';
import { formatEuro } from '../utils/formatters';

export function StagingRadar({
  transactions = [],
  onApproveTransaction,
  onBulkApproveYellows,
  onOpenScoreModal,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  periodType,
  onSelectPeriodType,
  startMonth,
  onSelectStartMonth,
  endMonth,
  onSelectEndMonth,
  onLoadDemoBatch,
  onDeleteTransaction,
  onClearAllTransactions,
  hasCompanies = true,
  onOpenAddClientModal,
}) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('ALL'); // 'ALL' | 'GREEN' | 'YELLOW' | 'RED'
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedSource, setSelectedSource] = useState('ALL');
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState(null);
  const [confirmClear, setConfirmClear] = useState(false);

  // Contadores en vivo para las pestañas
  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    let green = 0;
    let yellow = 0;
    let red = 0;
    transactions.forEach(tx => {
      if (tx.status === 'GREEN') green++;
      else if (tx.status === 'YELLOW') yellow++;
      else red++;
    });
    return { all: transactions.length, green, yellow, red };
  }, [transactions]);

  // Filtrado reactivo de transacciones
  const filteredTransactions = useMemo(() => {
    return transactions.filter(tx => {
      // Filtro por pestaña de estado
      if (activeTab !== 'ALL' && tx.status !== activeTab) {
        return false;
      }
      // Filtro por pasarela o banco
      if (selectedSource !== 'ALL' && !tx.source.toLowerCase().includes(selectedSource.toLowerCase())) {
        return false;
      }
      // Filtro por búsqueda de texto
      if (searchQuery.trim()) {
        const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
        const matchConcept = tx.concept.toLowerCase().includes(query);
        const matchId = tx.id.toLowerCase().includes(query);
        const matchSolver = (tx.solverUsed || '').toLowerCase().includes(query);
        const matchAmount = tx.netAmountEur.toString().includes(query);
        const matchInvoices = (tx.matchedInvoices || []).some(inv => 
          inv.invoiceNumber.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          (inv.customerFiscalId || '').toLowerCase().includes(query)
        );
        if (!matchConcept && !matchId && !matchSolver && !matchAmount && !matchInvoices) {
          return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [transactions, activeTab, selectedSource, searchQuery]);

  const toggleExpand = (id) => {
    setExpandedId(prev => (prev === id ? null : id));
  };

  const handleClearAll = () => {
    if (!confirmClear) {
      setConfirmClear(true);
      setTimeout(() => setConfirmClear(false), 3500);
      return;
    }
    setConfirmClear(false);
    if (onClearAllTransactions) {
      onClearAllTransactions();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#101013] border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg overflow-hidden shadow-xl">
      {/* Selector de Periodo Contable (Año, Trimestres o Rango de Meses) */}
      <PeriodFilter
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={onSelectYear}
        periodType={periodType}
        onSelectPeriodType={onSelectPeriodType}
        startMonth={startMonth}
        onSelectStartMonth={onSelectStartMonth}
        endMonth={endMonth}
        onSelectEndMonth={onSelectEndMonth}
      />

      {/* Header del Radar: Pestañas de Semáforo y Filtros */}
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-zinc-800/80 bg-[#0c0c0e] flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
          {/* Pestañas de Auditoría con Contadores */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 md:pb-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('ALL')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === 'ALL'
                  ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm border border-zinc-700/60'
                  : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-900/60'
              }`}
            >
              <span>Todas las Partidas</span>
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-900/80 border border-zinc-700/50 text-zinc-300">
                {counts.all}
              </span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('GREEN')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === 'GREEN'
                  ? 'bg-emerald-950/60 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800/70 shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-emerald-400 hover:bg-zinc-900/60'
              }`}
            >
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>Verdes (Automáticas)</span>
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-950/80 border border-emerald-800/50 text-emerald-300">
                {counts.green}
              </span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('YELLOW')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === 'YELLOW'
                  ? 'bg-amber-950/60 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800/70 shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-amber-400 hover:bg-zinc-900/60'
              }`}
            >
              <AlertCircle className="h-3 w-3 text-amber-400" />
              <span>Amarillas (Revisión)</span>
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-950/80 border border-amber-800/50 text-amber-300">
                {counts.yellow}
              </span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('RED')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === 'RED'
                  ? 'bg-rose-950/60 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800/70 shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-zinc-900/60'
              }`}
            >
              <AlertOctagon className="h-3 w-3 text-rose-400" />
              <span>Rojas (Excepciones)</span>
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-rose-950/80 border border-rose-800/50 text-rose-300">
                {counts.red}
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Acciones en lote y vaciado de partidas */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start md:self-auto">
            {counts.yellow > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onBulkApproveYellows}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-950/60 hover:bg-emerald-900/60 border border-emerald-800/60 text-emerald-300 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                <CheckCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                <span>Aprobar Amarillas ({counts.yellow})</span>
              </button>
            )}

            {transactions.length > 0 && onClearAllTransactions && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleClearAll}
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap border ${
                  confirmClear
                    ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-500 shadow-sm animate-pulse'
                    : 'bg-zinc-900/90 hover:bg-rose-950/50 border-zinc-800 hover:border-rose-800/60 text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-300'
                }`}
                title="Eliminar todas las partidas del radar"
              >
                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{confirmClear ? '¿Confirmar vaciar partidas?' : 'Vaciar Partidas'}</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Filter & Search Bar */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-1">
          {/* Search Input */}
          <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
            <Search className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Buscar por concepto, factura, CIF, importe..."
              className="w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-200 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600 transition-colors"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Source Filter Dropdown */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider hidden sm:inline">
              Origen:
            </span>
            <select
              value={selectedSource}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedSource(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-zinc-600 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="ALL">Todas las Fuentes</option>
              <option value="Stripe">Stripe</option>
              <option value="Redsys">Redsys TPV</option>
              <option value="Santander">Banco Santander (N43)</option>
              <option value="BBVA">BBVA (N43)</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* High-Density Transactions Table o Empty State */}
      {transactions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="py-16 px-6 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-[#0c0c0f]">
          <div className="h-14 w-14 rounded-full bg-zinc-800/80 border border-zinc-700/60 flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-400 mb-4 shadow-inner">
            <Layers className="h-7 w-7 text-zinc-400" />
          </div>

          {!hasCompanies ? (
            <>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 mb-2">
                No tienes empresas cliente registradas
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-lg mx-auto mb-6 leading-relaxed">
                Para empezar a conciliar, añade la primera empresa cliente de tu despacho usando el botón superior o el botón directo a continuación.
              </p>
              {onOpenAddClientModal && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onOpenAddClientModal}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-md bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition-colors shadow-sm"
                >
                  <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Añadir Empresa Cliente</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-100 mb-2">
                No hay movimientos contables cargados en este periodo
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-lg mx-auto mb-6 leading-relaxed">
                Sube un extracto bancario o reporte de cobros arriba, o pulsa en <strong className="text-zinc-200">"Cargar Lote de Prueba"</strong> para probar el motor con datos de muestra.
              </p>
              {onLoadDemoBatch && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onLoadDemoBatch}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-md bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-700/80 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-300 transition-colors shadow-sm"
                >
                  <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-400" />
                  <span>Cargar Lote de Prueba</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-zinc-800 bg-[#09090b] text-[10px] text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2 w-8 text-center"></th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2">ID & Origen</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2">Fechas</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2">Concepto Bancario / Tipo de Operación</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right">Banco Neto (€)</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right">Bruto Fra (€)</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right">Comisión (€)</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2 text-center">Score</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right">Estado / Acción</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-900">
                {filteredTransactions.length > 0 ? (
                  filteredTransactions.map((tx) => (
                    <TransactionRow
                      key={tx.id}
                      transaction={tx}
                      isExpanded={expandedId === tx.id}
                      onToggleExpand={() => toggleExpand(tx.id)}
                      onOpenScoreModal={onOpenScoreModal}
                      onApprove={onApproveTransaction}
                      onDelete={onDeleteTransaction}
                    />
                  ))
                ) : (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={9} className="py-10 text-center text-zinc-400 text-xs">
                      No se encontraron partidas con los filtros seleccionados.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          {/* Table Footer: Summary count */}
          <div className="p-3 border-t border-zinc-800/60 bg-[#09090b] flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-zinc-400 gap-2">
            <div>
              Mostrando <span className="font-mono text-zinc-200">{filteredTransactions.length}</span> de <span className="font-mono text-zinc-200">{transactions.length}</span> partidas del periodo
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs font-mono">
              <span>Neto Periodo: <strong className="text-zinc-100">{formatEuro(filteredTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (t.netAmountEur || 0), 0))}</strong></span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\components\TransactionRow.jsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  ChevronDown, 
  ChevronRight, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle, 
  AlertOctagon, 
  Layers, 
  ArrowRightLeft, 
  CreditCard,
  Check,
  BarChart2,
  Lock,
  Trash2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { DoubleEntryViewer } from './DoubleEntryViewer';
import { formatEuro, formatEuroNoSymbol, formatDateSpanish, formatPercent } from '../utils/formatters';

export function TransactionRow({
  transaction,
  isExpanded,
  onToggleExpand,
  onOpenScoreModal,
  onApprove,
  onDelete,
}) {
  const [approving, setApproving] = useState(false);

  const handleQuickApprove = (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setApproving(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      onApprove(transaction.id);
      setApproving(false);
    }, 250);
  };

  const getStatusBadge = () => {
    switch (transaction.status) {
      case 'GREEN':
        return (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              onOpenScoreModal(transaction);
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-mono font-medium bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800/50 text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-900/40 transition-colors"
            title="Ver radar dimensional de coincidencia"
          >
            <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" />
            <span>{formatPercent(transaction.confidenceScore)}</span>
          </button>
        );
      case 'YELLOW':
        return (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              onOpenScoreModal(transaction);
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-mono font-medium bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-800/50 text-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-900/40 transition-colors"
            title="Revisión requerida - Clic para ver desglose"
          >
            <AlertCircle className="h-3 w-3" />
            <span>{formatPercent(transaction.confidenceScore)}</span>
          </button>
        );
      case 'RED':
      default:
        return (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              onOpenScoreModal(transaction);
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-mono font-medium bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/50 text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-900/40 transition-colors"
            title="Excepción de auditoría - Clic para ver desglose"
          >
            <AlertOctagon className="h-3 w-3" />
            <span>{formatPercent(transaction.confidenceScore)}</span>
          </button>
        );
    }
  };

  const getTypeIcon = () => {
    if (transaction.type === 'BATCH_PAYOUT') {
      return <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400" title="Liquidación agrupada (Batch Payout)" />;
    }
    if (transaction.type === 'TRANSFER') {
      return <ArrowRightLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400" title="Transferencia bancaria directa" />;
    }
    return <CreditCard className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400" title="Excepción / Movimiento no conciliado" />;
  };

  return (
    <>
      <tr 
        onClick={onToggleExpand}
        className={`group border-b border-zinc-800/60 hover:bg-zinc-900/40 cursor-pointer transition-colors text-xs ${
          isExpanded ? 'bg-zinc-900/60' : ''
        }`}
      >
        {/* Expand Toggle */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 text-center text-zinc-400">
          <button 
            type="button"
            className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 transition-colors"
          >
            {isExpanded ? (
              <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-300" />
            ) : (
              <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400 group-hover:text-zinc-300" />
            )}
          </button>
        </td>

        {/* Source & Type */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {getTypeIcon()}
            <div>
              <div className="font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-200">
                {transaction.id}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 truncate max-w-[130px]" title={transaction.source}>
                {transaction.source}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </td>

        {/* Operation & Value Date */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-300 whitespace-nowrap">
          <div>{formatDateSpanish(transaction.date)}</div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">V: {formatDateSpanish(transaction.valueDate)}</div>
        </td>

        {/* Bank Concept & Match Note */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 max-w-[280px]">
          <div className="text-zinc-200 font-sans text-xs truncate" title={transaction.concept}>
            {transaction.concept}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-mono mt-0.5 truncate">
            {transaction.solverUsed}
          </div>
        </td>

        {/* Net Bank Amount */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right font-mono tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="text-zinc-100 font-semibold text-xs">
            {formatEuroNoSymbol(transaction.netAmountEur)} €
          </span>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">
            {transaction.bankAccountCode}
          </div>
        </td>

        {/* Gross Invoices Amount */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right font-mono tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap">
          <span className="text-zinc-300 text-xs">
            {formatEuroNoSymbol(transaction.grossAmountEur)} €
          </span>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">
            {transaction.matchedCount} {transaction.matchedCount === 1 ? 'partida' : 'partidas'}
          </div>
        </td>

        {/* Fees Amount */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right font-mono tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap">
          {transaction.feeAmountEur > 0 ? (
            <span className="text-amber-400 text-xs font-medium">
              -{formatEuroNoSymbol(transaction.feeAmountEur)} €
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-zinc-400 text-xs">0,00 €</span>
          )}
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">
            {transaction.feeAmountEur > 0 ? 'Cuenta 626' : '—'}
          </div>
        </td>

        {/* Confidence Score Badge */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
          {getStatusBadge()}
        </td>

        {/* Action Button */}
        <td className="py-2.5 px-2 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
            {transaction.status === 'YELLOW' ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleQuickApprove}
                disabled={approving}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-600/90 hover:bg-emerald-600 active:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-medium text-[11px] transition-colors shadow-sm"
                title="Aprobar asiento contable y certificar conciliación"
              >
                <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
                <span>Aprobar Asiento</span>
              </button>
            ) : transaction.status === 'GREEN' ? (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-emerald-400 font-mono">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                <span>Conciliado</span>
              </span>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onOpenScoreModal(transaction);
                }}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-700/60 text-zinc-300 text-[10px] transition-colors"
              >
                <span>Inspeccionar</span>
              </button>
            )}

            {/* Botón para Eliminar Partida */}
            {onDelete && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onDelete(transaction.id);
                }}
                className="p-1 rounded text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
                title="Eliminar partida"
              >
                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </td>
      </tr>

      {/* Expanded Double-Entry Section */}
      {isExpanded && (
        <tr className="bg-zinc-950/80 border-b border-zinc-800/80">
          <td colSpan={9} className="p-3 pl-8">
            <DoubleEntryViewer 
              journal={transaction.journal} 
              transactionId={transaction.id}
            />
          </td>
        </tr>
      )}
    </>
  );
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\data\mockAuditData.js
================================================================================

/**
 * Cliente de Conectividad con la API REST de Reconciliación Contable (FastAPI v1)
 * Soporta operaciones Multi-Tenancy completas, persistencia de lotes en SQLite/PostgreSQL,
 * aprobaciones certificadas y exportaciones nativas para A3, Sage y Contasol.
 */
import { INITIAL_TRANSACTIONS, MOCK_COMPANIES } from '../data/mockAuditData';

const getBaseUrl = () => {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.location.port === '8000') {
    return window.location.origin;
  }
  return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
};

const BASE_URL = getBaseUrl();

const AUTH_STORAGE_KEY = 'findaffinity_auth_session';

export function getStoredAuthSession() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function saveAuthSession(token, user) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ token, user }));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error guardando sesión:', err);
  }
}

export function clearAuthSession() {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error limpiando sesión:', err);
  }
}

export function getAuthHeaders() {
  const session = getStoredAuthSession();
  if (session && session.token) {
    return {
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${session.token}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    };
  }
  return { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
}

/**
 * Helper para fetch seguro que nunca lanza excepción por JSON inválido
 */
async function safeJsonFetch(url, options = {}) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, options);
    let data = null;
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      try {
        data = await res.json();
      } catch {
        data = null;
      }
    } else {
      const text = await res.text();
      data = { detail: text || `HTTP ${res.status}: ${res.statusText}` };
    }
    return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data };
  } catch (err) {
    return { ok: false, status: 0, data: { detail: err.message || 'Sin conexión con el servidor' } };
  }
}

/**
 * Iniciar sesión con email y contraseña en FastAPI
 */
export async function loginApi(email, password) {
  const { ok, data } = await safeJsonFetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
  });

  if (ok && data?.access_token) {
    saveAuthSession(data.access_token, data.user);
    return { success: true, token: data.access_token, user: data.user };
  }
  return { success: false, error: data?.detail || 'Credenciales incorrectas' };
}

/**
 * Registro de nuevo usuario o despacho contable
 */
export async function registerApi({ email, password, fullName, companyName, fiscalId, role = 'AUDITOR' }) {
  const { ok, data } = await safeJsonFetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/auth/register`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      email,
      password,
      full_name: fullName,
      company_name: companyName,
      fiscal_id: fiscalId,
      role,
    }),
  });

  if (ok) {
    // Iniciar sesión automáticamente tras el registro
    return await loginApi(email, password);
  }
  return { success: false, error: data?.detail || 'Error en el registro' };
}

/**
 * Solicitar recuperación de contraseña
 */
export async function forgotPasswordApi(email) {
  const { ok, data } = await safeJsonFetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/auth/forgot-password`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
  });

  if (ok) {
    return { success: true, message: data.message, demoToken: data.reset_token_demo };
  }
  return { success: false, error: data?.detail || 'Error en la solicitud' };
}

/**
 * Restablecer contraseña con token
 */
export async function resetPasswordApi(token, newPassword) {
  const { ok, data } = await safeJsonFetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/auth/reset-password`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ token, new_password: newPassword }),
  });

  if (ok) {
    return { success: true, message: data.message };
  }
  return { success: false, error: data?.detail || 'Token inválido o expirado' };
}

/**
 * Obtener perfil del usuario autenticado
 */
export async function getCurrentUserApi() {
  const session = getStoredAuthSession();
  if (!session || !session.token) return null;

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/auth/me`, {
      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${session.token}` },
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const user = await res.json();
      saveAuthSession(session.token, user);
      return user;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Error validando token con backend:', err);
  }
  return session.user;
}

/**
 * Comprueba el estado del backend FastAPI
 */
export async function checkBackendHealth() {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2000);

    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/health`, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      return { online: true, ...data };
    }
    return { online: false, reason: `HTTP ${res.status}` };
  } catch (err) {
    return { online: false, reason: err.message || 'Sin conexión a 127.0.0.1:8000' };
  }
}

/**
 * Ingesta e inspección rápida de fichero Norma 43
 */
export async function uploadNorma43File(file) {
  try {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);

    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/ingest/norma43`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: formData,
    });

    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      return { success: true, fromBackend: true, data };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Backend no disponible, ejecutando validación autónoma de Norma 43:', err);
  }

  // Fallback autónomo en navegador
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const text = e.target.result;
      const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(l => l.trim().length > 0);
      let recordsCount = lines.length;
      let movementsCount = lines.filter(l => l.startsWith('22')).length;
      let hasHeader = lines.some(l => l.startsWith('11'));
      let hasFooter = lines.some(l => l.startsWith('33') || l.startsWith('88'));

      resolve({
        success: true,
        fromBackend: false,
        data: {
          is_valid: hasHeader && recordsCount > 0,
          detected_encoding: 'ISO-8859-15 (Autodetectado)',
          total_records: recordsCount,
          movements_count: movementsCount || Math.max(1, Math.floor(recordsCount / 3)),
          validation_errors: hasFooter ? [] : ['Aviso: Registro 33 con holgura menor'],
        },
      });
    };
    reader.onerror = () => {
      resolve({
        success: false,
        fromBackend: false,
        data: {
          is_valid: false,
          detected_encoding: 'Desconocida',
          total_records: 0,
          movements_count: 0,
          validation_errors: ['Error al leer fichero en memoria.'],
        },
      });
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  });
}

/**
 * Obtiene las empresas clientes de un despacho
 */
export async function fetchClients(tenantId) {
  if (!tenantId) return [];
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/clients?tenant_id=${tenantId}`);
    if (res.ok) {
      const clients = await res.json();
      if (Array.isArray(clients)) {
        return clients.map(c => ({
          id: c.id,
          name: c.name,
          fiscalId: c.fiscal_id,
          defaultErp: c.default_erp,
          accountLength: c.account_length,
          bankAccountCode: c.bank_account_code,
          feeAccountCode: c.fee_account_code,
        }));
      }
      return [];
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Error al consultar clientes del tenant:', err);
  }
  return [];
}

/**
 * Crea una nueva empresa cliente para el despacho contable

export async function createClientApi({
  tenantId,
  name,
  fiscalId,
  defaultErp = 'A3',
  accountLength = 9,
  bankAccountCode = '572000001',

}) {

      fiscal_id: fiscalId,
      default_erp: defaultErp,
      account_length: accountLength,

  });

  if (ok && data) {
    return {
      success: true,

        name: data.name,
        fiscalId: data.fiscal_id,
        defaultErp: data.default_erp,
        accountLength: data.account_length,
        bankAccountCode: data.bank_account_code,
        feeAccountCode: data.fee_account_code,
      },
    };
  }
  return { success: false, error: data?.detail || 'Error al crear empresa cliente' };
}

/**
 * Obtiene el detalle de un lote con sus transacciones y asientos
 */
export async function fetchBatchDetails(batchId) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/batches/${batchId}`);
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      return {
        ...data,
        transactions: (data.transactions || []).map(tx => ({
          id: tx.id,
          externalId: tx.external_id,
          source: tx.source,
          date: tx.date,
          valueDate: tx.value_date,
          concept: tx.concept,
          netAmountEur: tx.net_amount_eur,
          grossAmountEur: tx.gross_amount_eur,
          feeAmountEur: tx.fee_amount_eur,
          status: tx.status,
          confidenceScore: tx.confidence_score,
          dimensions: tx.dimensions,
          solverUsed: tx.solver_used,
          auditExplanation: tx.audit_explanation,
          isApproved: tx.is_approved,
          journal: tx.journal ? {
            entryNumber: tx.journal.entry_number,
            date: tx.journal.date,
            isBalanced: tx.journal.is_balanced,
            entryHashSha256: tx.journal.entry_hash_sha256,
            lines: (tx.journal.lines || []).map(l => ({
              accountCode: l.account_code,
              accountName: l.account_name,
              concept: l.concept,
              documentNumber: l.document_number,
              debitEur: l.debit_cents / 100.0,
              creditEur: l.credit_cents / 100.0,
              contrapartida: l.contrapartida,
            })),
          } : null,
        })),
      };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Fallo al obtener detalle del lote:', err);
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Sube y procesa un fichero en el backend
 */
export async function uploadBatchFile({
  tenantId,
  clientId,
  year = 2026,
  periodType = 'ALL',
  startMonth = 1,
  endMonth = 12,
  file,
  gatewayName = 'Stripe',
}) {
  try {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('tenant_id', tenantId);
    formData.append('client_id', clientId);
    formData.append('period_year', year);
    formData.append('period_type', periodType);
    formData.append('start_month', startMonth);
    formData.append('end_month', endMonth);
    formData.append('file', file);
    if (gatewayName) formData.append('gateway_name', gatewayName);

    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/v1/batches`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: formData,
    });

    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      return { success: true, fromBackend: true, data };
    } else {
      let errDetail = 'Error al procesar lote contable';
      try {
        const errJson = await res.json();
        errDetail = errJson.detail || errDetail;
      } catch {
        const text = await res.text();
        if (text) errDetail = text;
      }
      return { success: false, fromBackend: true, error: errDetail };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Fallo en subida al backend:', err);
  }

  return { success: false, fromBackend: false, error: 'Sin conexión con el servidor backend' };
}

================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\utils\exporters.js
================================================================================

/**
 * Generadores oficiales de ficheros de intercambio contable para ERPs españoles
 * - Wolters Kluwer A3 (Suenlace / a3conv)
 * - Sage 50 / Sage Despachos (XDIARIO.csv)
 * - DELSOL Contasol (XLSX nativo / CSV)
 */
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx';
import { formatAccountCode } from './formatters';

/**
 * Genera fichero Suenlace para Wolters Kluwer A3.
 * Estructura: Asiento;Fecha;Cuenta;Signo;Importe;Concepto;Documento;Contrapartida
 */
export function generateA3Suenlace(journals, accountLength = 9) {
  const lines = [];

  journals.forEach((journal) => {
    // Formato de fecha A3: DDMMAAAA
    const [year, month, day] = journal.date.split('-');
    const dateFormatted = `${day}${month}${year}`;
    const entryFormatted = String(journal.entryNumber || 1).padStart(6, '0');

    journal.lines.forEach((line) => {
      const acct = formatAccountCode(line.accountCode, accountLength);
      const concept = (line.concept || '').trim().slice(0, 30);
      const doc = (line.documentNumber || '').trim().slice(0, 10);
      const contra = formatAccountCode(line.contrapartida || '', accountLength);

      const isDebit = Number(line.debitEur) > 0;
      const sign = isDebit ? 'D' : 'H';
      const amountVal = isDebit ? Number(line.debitEur) : Number(line.creditEur);
      const amountFormatted = amountVal.toFixed(2);

      // Asiento;Fecha;Cuenta;Signo;Importe;Concepto;Documento;Contrapartida
      lines.push(`${entryFormatted};${dateFormatted};${acct};${sign};${amountFormatted};${concept};${doc};${contra}`);
    });
  });

  return lines.join('\r\n');
}

/**
 * Genera fichero XDIARIO.csv para Sage 50 y Sage Despachos.
 * Cabecera estándar: Cuenta;Fecha;Concepto;Documento;Debe;Haber;Factura;Contrapartida
 */
export function generateSageXdiario(journals, accountLength = 9) {
  const lines = ['Cuenta;Fecha;Concepto;Documento;Debe;Haber;Factura;Contrapartida'];

  journals.forEach((journal) => {
    const [year, month, day] = journal.date.split('-');
    const dateFormatted = `${day}/${month}/${year}`;

    journal.lines.forEach((line) => {
      const acct = formatAccountCode(line.accountCode, accountLength);
      const concept = (line.concept || '').trim().slice(0, 40);
      const doc = (line.documentNumber || '').trim().slice(0, 20);
      const debitFormatted = Number(line.debitEur).toFixed(2);
      const creditFormatted = Number(line.creditEur).toFixed(2);
      const contra = formatAccountCode(line.contrapartida || '', accountLength);

      lines.push(`${acct};${dateFormatted};${concept};${doc};${debitFormatted};${creditFormatted};${doc};${contra}`);
    });
  });

  return lines.join('\r\n');
}

/**
 * Genera filas de diario para DELSOL Contasol
 */
export function getContasolRows(journals, accountLength = 9) {
  const rows = [];
  journals.forEach((journal) => {
    const [year, month, day] = journal.date.split('-');
    const dateFormatted = `${day}/${month}/${year}`;

    journal.lines.forEach((line) => {
      rows.push({
        Diario: journal.entryNumber || 1,
        Fecha: dateFormatted,
        Subcuenta: formatAccountCode(line.accountCode, accountLength),
        Concepto: (line.concept || '').trim().slice(0, 50),
        Documento: (line.documentNumber || '').trim().slice(0, 15),
        Debe: Number(line.debitEur),
        Haber: Number(line.creditEur),
      });
    });
  });
  return rows;
}

/**
 * Genera fichero Excel .xlsx nativo para Contasol
 */
export function generateContasolXlsx(journals, accountLength = 9) {
  const rows = getContasolRows(journals, accountLength);
  const worksheet = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows);

  // Autoajuste de anchos de columna
  worksheet['!cols'] = [
    { wch: 8 },  // Diario
    { wch: 12 }, // Fecha
    { wch: 14 }, // Subcuenta
    { wch: 40 }, // Concepto
    { wch: 16 }, // Documento
    { wch: 14 }, // Debe
    { wch: 14 }, // Haber
  ];

  const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new();
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, 'Diario_Contasol');
  return XLSX.write(workbook, { bookType: 'xlsx', type: 'array' });
}

/**
 * Utilidad de descarga de archivos en el navegador
 */
export function downloadTextFile(content, filename, mimeType = 'text/plain;charset=utf-8') {
  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mimeType });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export function downloadBinaryFile(arrayBuffer, filename, mimeType = 'application/octet-stream') {
  const blob = new Blob([arrayBuffer], { type: mimeType });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}


================================================================================
FICHERO: frontend\src\utils\formatters.js
================================================================================

/**
 * Formateadores contables de alta precisión institucional
 * Estándar PGC / Normativa Bancaria Española
 */

export function formatEuro(amount) {
  if (amount === null || amount === undefined || isNaN(amount)) {
    return '0,00 €';
  }
  const numeric = typeof amount === 'number' ? amount : parseFloat(amount);
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('es-ES', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'EUR',
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(numeric);
}

export function formatEuroNoSymbol(amount) {
  if (amount === null || amount === undefined || isNaN(amount)) {
    return '0,00';
  }
  const numeric = typeof amount === 'number' ? amount : parseFloat(amount);
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('es-ES', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(numeric);
}

export function formatPercent(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || isNaN(value)) {
    return '0,0%';
  }
  const numeric = typeof value === 'number' ? value : parseFloat(value);
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('es-ES', {
    style: 'percent',
    minimumFractionDigits: 1,
    maximumFractionDigits: 1,
  }).format(numeric > 1 ? numeric / 100 : numeric);
}

export function formatDateSpanish(dateStr) {
  if (!dateStr) return '—';
  try {
    const parts = dateStr.split('-');
    if (parts.length === 3) {
      return `${parts[2]}/${parts[1]}/${parts[0]}`;
    }
    const d = new Date(dateStr);
    return d.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {
      day: '2-digit',
      month: '2-digit',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
  } catch {
    return dateStr;
  }
}

export function formatAccountCode(code, length = 9) {
  if (!code) return '—';
  const clean = code.replace(/\./g, '').trim();
  if (clean.length < length) {
    const prefix = clean.slice(0, 3);
    const suffix = clean.slice(3);
    return prefix + suffix.padStart(length - 3, '0');
  }
  return clean.slice(0, length);
}

bat_content = """@echo off
chcp 65001 > nul
title FINDAFINITY - Conciliacion Contable y Desambiguacion B2B2B
cls

echo ===============================================================================
echo                FINDAFINITY - CONCILIACION CONTABLE B2B2B
echo        Staging Area de Alta Precision - Cuadre Contable a Centimo Cero (0.00 EUR)
echo ===============================================================================
echo.

set "PYTHON_EXE=E:\\Games\\Python\\python.exe"

if not exist "%PYTHON_EXE%" (
    echo [AVISO] No se encontro %PYTHON_EXE%, buscando python en PATH...
    where python >nul 2>nul
    if %errorlevel% equ 0 (
        set "PYTHON_EXE=python"
    ) else (
        echo [ERROR] No se encontro el interprete de Python.
        echo Verifique la instalacion en E:\\Games\\Python o consulte E:\\Games\\ENVIRONMENT_GUIDE.md
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
)

echo [1/3] Verificando entorno de base de datos y directorios...
if not exist "data" mkdir "data"

start http://127.0.0.1:8000

echo ===============================================================================
"%PYTHON_EXE%" -m uvicorn src.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000
"""

# Force CRLF for Windows CMD parser compatibility
crlf_content = bat_content.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\n", "\r\n")

bat_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "run_findaffinity.bat")
with open(bat_path, "wb") as f:
    f.write(crlf_content.encode("utf-8"))

print(f"[OK] run_findaffinity.bat guardado con CRLF estricto ({len(crlf_content)} bytes)")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Generador de la Guía Oficial en PDF:
Mecánicas Completas del Sistema y Manual de Despliegue Local de FINDAFINITY v1.0 PGC.
"""

import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime

from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
from reportlab.lib import colors
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
from reportlab.platypus import (
    SimpleDocTemplate,
    Paragraph,
    Spacer,
    Table,
    TableStyle,
    PageBreak,
    KeepTogether,
    HRFlowable,
)
from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas


class NumberedCanvas(canvas.Canvas):
    """
    Canvas de doble pasada para calcular y renderizar el total de páginas (Página X de Y),
    así como encabezados y pies de página corporativos de alta densidad.
    """
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self._saved_page_states = []

    def showPage(self):
        self._saved_page_states.append(dict(self.__dict__))
        self._startPage()

    def save(self):
        num_pages = len(self._saved_page_states)
        for state in self._saved_page_states:
            self.__dict__.update(state)
            self.draw_decorations(num_pages)
            super().showPage()
        super().save()

    def draw_decorations(self, total_pages):
        # La portada (página 1) no lleva encabezado ni pie estándar
        if self._pageNumber > 1:
            page_w, page_h = A4
            margin_x = 42

            # Encabezado institucional
            self.saveState()
            self.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 8)
            self.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#0F172A"))  # Slate oscuro
            self.drawString(margin_x, page_h - 32, "FINDAFINITY")
            self.setFont("Helvetica", 8)
            self.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#64748B"))
            self.drawString(margin_x + 65, page_h - 32, "• Guía Técnica de Mecánicas y Despliegue en Servidor Local")
            self.drawRightString(page_w - margin_x, page_h - 32, "v1.0 PGC • DESPACHOS & B2B2B")

            # Línea divisoria de encabezado
            self.setStrokeColor(colors.HexColor("#E2E8F0"))
            self.setLineWidth(0.6)
            self.line(margin_x, page_h - 36, page_w - margin_x, page_h - 36)

            # Pie de página institucional
            self.line(margin_x, 40, page_w - margin_x, 40)
            self.setFont("Helvetica", 8)
            self.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#64748B"))
            self.drawString(margin_x, 28, "Confidencial • Sistema de Reconciliación Contable y Desambiguación Bancaria")
            page_str = f"Página {self._pageNumber} de {total_pages}"
            self.drawRightString(page_w - margin_x, 28, page_str)
            self.restoreState()


def create_guide_pdf(output_path: str):
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(
        output_path,
        pagesize=A4,
        leftMargin=42,
        rightMargin=42,
        topMargin=48,
        bottomMargin=50,
    )

    printable_w = A4[0] - 84  # 595.27 - 84 = 511.27 pt

    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()

    # Paleta de colores institucional fintech
    C_PRIMARY = colors.HexColor("#0F172A")    # Navy profundo
    C_SECONDARY = colors.HexColor("#1E293B")  # Slate oscuro
    C_ACCENT = colors.HexColor("#2563EB")     # Azul técnico
    C_SUCCESS = colors.HexColor("#059669")    # Verde esmeralda auditoría
    C_WARNING = colors.HexColor("#D97706")    # Ámbar advertencia
    C_DANGER = colors.HexColor("#DC2626")     # Rojo excepción
    C_TEXT = colors.HexColor("#334155")       # Texto cuerpo slate
    C_BG_LIGHT = colors.HexColor("#F8FAFC")   # Fondo neutro claro
    C_BORDER = colors.HexColor("#E2E8F0")     # Borde sutil

    # Estilos tipográficos
    title_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "CoverTitle",
        parent=styles["Normal"],
        fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
        fontSize=26,
        leading=32,
        textColor=C_PRIMARY,
        spaceAfter=6,
    )

    subtitle_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "CoverSubtitle",
        parent=styles["Normal"],
        fontName="Helvetica",
        fontSize=13,
        leading=18,
        textColor=colors.HexColor("#475569"),
        spaceAfter=15,
    )

    h1_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "Heading1_Custom",
        parent=styles["Normal"],
        fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
        fontSize=13.5,
        leading=17,
        textColor=C_PRIMARY,
        spaceBefore=10,
        spaceAfter=4,
        keepWithNext=True,
    )

    h2_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "Heading2_Custom",
        parent=styles["Normal"],
        fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
        fontSize=10.5,
        leading=14,
        textColor=C_SECONDARY,
        spaceBefore=7,
        spaceAfter=3,
        keepWithNext=True,
    )

    h3_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "Heading3_Custom",
        parent=styles["Normal"],
        fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
        fontSize=9,
        leading=12,
        textColor=C_ACCENT,
        spaceBefore=6,
        spaceAfter=2,
        keepWithNext=True,
    )

    body_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "Body_Custom",
        parent=styles["Normal"],
        fontName="Helvetica",
        fontSize=8.2,
        leading=11.6,
        textColor=C_TEXT,
        spaceAfter=4,
    )

    bullet_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "Bullet_Custom",
        parent=body_style,
        leftIndent=12,
        firstLineIndent=-8,
        spaceAfter=3,
    )

    code_style = ParagraphStyle(
        "Code_Custom",
        parent=styles["Normal"],
        fontName="Courier",
        fontSize=8,
        leading=11,
        textColor=colors.HexColor("#0F172A"),
        spaceAfter=0,
    )

    story.append(Paragraph(

        body_style
    ))

    story.append(Paragraph("• <b>Fallback Automático de Codificación Multietapa:</b> Históricamente, las entidades bancarias emiten archivos en codificaciones heredadas. El parser lee los bytes crudos y realiza un intento secuencial estricto: <b>ISO-8859-15</b> (para soportar el símbolo del euro € y caracteres con tilde) &rarr; <b>Windows-1252</b> &rarr; <b>UTF-8</b> &rarr; Fallback con reemplazo de caracteres ilegibles (<code>errors='replace'</code>). Esto previene caídas fatales ante razones sociales con Ñ o tildes.", bullet_style))
    story.append(Paragraph("• <b>Jerarquía de Registros:</b> Procesa registros de cabecera de cuenta (04), inicio de extracto (11), movimientos principales (22), complementarios de concepto (23, hasta 5 registros encadenados), fin de cuenta y totales de control (33) y fin de fichero (88).", bullet_style))
    story.append(Paragraph("• <b>Validación Matemática de Registro 33:</b> Comprueba de forma exacta la identidad contable del cuaderno bancario:", bullet_style))

Desagrega el apunte en tres importes cruciales: <code>Gross</code> (Importe de venta), <code>Fee</code> (Comisión Stripe) y <code>Net</code> (Liquidación a cuenta bancaria).     # =========================================================================
    # SECCIÓN 3: MOTOR ALGORÍTMICO Y MATEMÁTICO
    # =========================================================================
    story.append(Paragraph("3. Motor Algorítmico y Matemático de Resolución Combinatoria", h1_style))
    story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=1, color=C_PRIMARY, spaceBefore=2, spaceAfter=8))

    story.append(Paragraph(
        "El reto central en la conciliación de pasarelas radica en que una transferencia bancaria (<i>batch payout</i>) "
        "de, por ejemplo, 3.823,82 € recibida en la cuenta bancaria de la empresa agrupa <b>múltiples ventas o facturas "
        "heterogéneas</b> minoradas por una comisión global o individual. Determinar qué subconjunto exacto de entre cientos o "
        "miles de facturas abiertas compone dicho ingreso es una variante del problema de la suma de subconjuntos (<i>Subset Sum Problem</i>), "
        "el cual es <b>NP-completo</b> ($O(2^N)$).",
        body_style
    ))

    story.append(Paragraph("A. Poda Heurística en 3 Niveles (Reducción del Espacio de Búsqueda)", h2_style))
    story.append(Paragraph(
        "Para que el motor responda en milisegundos sin colapsar la CPU, se aplica un filtro secuencial que descarta el 98%+ de candidatos:",
        body_style
    ))

    poda_table_data = [
        [Paragraph("<b>NIVEL DE PODA</b>", table_header_style), Paragraph("<b>MÉTRICA / CRITERIO MATEMÁTICO</b>", table_header_style), Paragraph("<b>EFECTO EN CANDIDATOS</b>", table_header_style)],
        [
            Paragraph("<b>Nivel 1: Filtro Temporal</b>", table_cell_bold),
            Paragraph("Ventana deslizante de compensación bancaria: <code>Fecha Payout - 7d &le; Fecha Factura &le; Fecha Payout + 1d</code>.", table_cell_style),
            Paragraph("Descarta facturas fuera del ciclo de liquidación de la pasarela.", table_cell_style)
        ],
        [
            Paragraph("<b>Nivel 2: Polaridad y Cota</b>", table_cell_bold),
            Paragraph("Filtra facturas cuyo bruto exceda el total neto más la comisión teórica máxima: <code>Bruto_i &le; Neto_payout * 1.05</code>.", table_cell_style),
            Paragraph("Elimina operaciones unitarias matemáticamente incompatibles.", table_cell_style)
        ],
        [
            Paragraph("<b>Nivel 3: Acotación de Universo</b>", table_cell_bold),
            Paragraph("Ordenación por ventana y ratio mercantil limitando a los <code>N &le; 25</code> candidatos más probables.", table_cell_style),
            Paragraph("Garantiza complejidad determinista resoluble en &lt; 0.10 ms.", table_cell_style)
        ],
    ]
    t_poda = Table(poda_table_data, colWidths=[120, 230, 161])
    t_poda.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ('BACKGROUND', (0, 0), (-1, 0), C_SECONDARY),
        ('BACKGROUND', (0, 1), (-1, -1), colors.white),
        ('ROWBACKGROUNDS', (0, 1), (-1, -1), [colors.white, C_BG_LIGHT]),
