"""
Parser de Norma 43 (Cuaderno 43 de la Asociación Española de Banca)
Incluye detección resiliente y fallback de codificación (ISO-8859-15, Windows-1252, UTF-8),
extracción estricta de registros de longitud fija (80 caracteres) y validación matemática de integridad.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple, Union

from src.parsers.base import CanonicalBankTransaction, TransactionType


class Norma43ValidationError(Exception):
    """Error emitido cuando el fichero Norma 43 incumple reglas estructurales o de balance matemático."""
    pass


@dataclass
class Norma43AccountHeader:
    bank_code: str
    branch_code: str
    account_number: str
    start_date: date
    end_date: date
    initial_balance_cents: int
    initial_balance_type: str  # '1': Deudor (Negativo), '2': Acreedor (Positivo)
    currency: str
    account_name: str


@dataclass
class Norma43AccountFooter:
    bank_code: str
    branch_code: str
    account_number: str
    num_debits: int
    total_debits_cents: int
    num_credits: int
    total_credits_cents: int
    final_balance_cents: int
    final_balance_type: str    # '1': Deudor (Negativo), '2': Acreedor (Positivo)
    currency: str


@dataclass
class Norma43MovementItem:
    movement_id: str
    branch_code: str
    operation_date: date
    value_date: date
    common_code: str           # '04' transferencias, '17' comisiones, etc.
    own_code: str
    debit_credit: str          # '1' Debe/Cargo, '2' Haber/Abono
    amount_cents: int
    document_number: str
    reference_1: str
    reference_2: str
    complementary_lines: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def full_concept(self) -> str:
        all_parts = [self.reference_1.strip(), self.reference_2.strip()]
        all_parts.extend([line.strip() for line in self.complementary_lines if line.strip()])
        return " - ".join([p for p in all_parts if p]).strip()


@dataclass
class Norma43AccountBlock:
    header: Norma43AccountHeader
    movements: List[Norma43MovementItem]
    footer: Optional[Norma43AccountFooter] = None
    is_balanced: bool = False
    balance_discrepancy_cents: int = 0


@dataclass
class Norma43ParseResult:
    detected_encoding: str
    total_records: int
    accounts: List[Norma43AccountBlock] = field(default_factory=list)
    canonical_transactions: List[CanonicalBankTransaction] = field(default_factory=list)
    is_valid: bool = False
    validation_errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


class Norma43Parser:
    SUPPORTED_ENCODINGS = ["iso-8859-15", "windows-1252", "iso-8859-1", "utf-8", "utf-8-sig"]

    @classmethod
    def detect_and_decode(cls, raw_data: bytes) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """
        Detecta la codificación de un fichero bancario español probando primero ISO-8859-15
        y Windows-1252 (estándares habituales de la banca española con Ñ, tildes y €)
        y aplicando fallback controlado.
        """
        # Prioridad de prueba:
        # En la banca española los extractos con 'ñ', 'á', '€' suelen codificarse en ISO-8859-15 o CP1252.
        # Si un archivo contiene bytes como 0xF1 ('ñ') o 0xA4 ('€' en ISO-8859-15),
        # probar UTF-8 primero podría fallar con UnicodeDecodeError.
        for enc in cls.SUPPORTED_ENCODINGS:
            try:
                decoded = raw_data.decode(enc)
                # Validar que no contenga el carácter nulo o patrones corruptos
                if "\x00" not in decoded:
                    return decoded, enc
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                continue

        # Fallback de emergencia reemplazando bytes no representables
        return raw_data.decode("latin1", errors="replace"), "latin1-replace"

    @classmethod
    def parse_file(cls, file_path_or_bytes: Union[str, Path, bytes]) -> Norma43ParseResult:
        if isinstance(file_path_or_bytes, (str, Path)):
            with open(file_path_or_bytes, "rb") as f:
                raw_bytes = f.read()
        else:
            raw_bytes = file_path_or_bytes

        text_content, detected_enc = cls.detect_and_decode(raw_bytes)
        return cls.parse_text(text_content, detected_encoding=detected_enc)

    @classmethod
    def parse_text(cls, text: str, detected_encoding: str = "utf-8") -> Norma43ParseResult:
        lines = [line.rstrip("\r\n") for line in text.splitlines() if line.strip()]
        result = Norma43ParseResult(detected_encoding=detected_encoding, total_records=len(lines))

        current_block: Optional[Norma43AccountBlock] = None
        current_movement: Optional[Norma43MovementItem] = None
        global_mov_counter = 0

        for line_idx, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
            if len(line) < 80:
                # Los registros AEB exigen longitud fija de 80 caracteres. Rellenar si tiene padding recortado
                line = line.ljust(80)

            rec_type = line[0:2]

            try:
                if rec_type == "11":
                    # Cabecera de cuenta
                    bank_code = line[2:6]
                    branch_code = line[6:10]
                    account_num = line[10:20]
                    start_date = cls._parse_date_yymmdd(line[20:26])
                    end_date = cls._parse_date_yymmdd(line[26:32])
                    init_bal_type = line[32]
                    init_bal_cents = int(line[33:47])
                    currency = cls._parse_currency(line[47:50])
                    acct_name = line[51:77].strip()

                    header = Norma43AccountHeader(
                        bank_code=bank_code,
                        branch_code=branch_code,
                        account_number=account_num,
                        start_date=start_date,
                        end_date=end_date,
                        initial_balance_cents=init_bal_cents,
                        initial_balance_type=init_bal_type,
                        currency=currency,
                        account_name=acct_name,
                    )
                    current_block = Norma43AccountBlock(header=header, movements=[])
                    result.accounts.append(current_block)
                    current_movement = None

                elif rec_type == "22":
                    if not current_block:
                        result.validation_errors.append(f"Línea {line_idx}: Movimiento (22) sin cabecera previa (11).")
                        continue

                    global_mov_counter += 1
                    branch_code = line[6:10]
                    op_date = cls._parse_date_yymmdd(line[10:16])
                    val_date = cls._parse_date_yymmdd(line[16:22])
                    common_code = line[22:24]
                    own_code = line[24:27]
                    deb_cred = line[27]  # '1'=Debe, '2'=Haber
                    amount_cents = int(line[28:42])
                    doc_num = line[42:52].strip()
                    ref_1 = line[52:64].strip()
                    ref_2 = line[64:80].strip()

                    mov_id = f"N43-{current_block.header.bank_code}-{current_block.header.account_number}-{global_mov_counter}"
                    current_movement = Norma43MovementItem(
                        movement_id=mov_id,
                        branch_code=branch_code,
                        operation_date=op_date,
                        value_date=val_date,
                        common_code=common_code,
                        own_code=own_code,
                        debit_credit=deb_cred,
                        amount_cents=amount_cents,
                        document_number=doc_num,
                        reference_1=ref_1,
                        reference_2=ref_2,
                    )
                    current_block.movements.append(current_movement)

                elif rec_type == "23":
                    if not current_movement:
                        result.validation_errors.append(f"Línea {line_idx}: Concepto complementario (23) sin apunte principal (22).")
                        continue
                    concept_line = line[4:80].strip()
                    if concept_line:
                        current_movement.complementary_lines.append(concept_line)

                elif rec_type == "33":
                    if not current_block:
                        result.validation_errors.append(f"Línea {line_idx}: Fin de cuenta (33) sin cabecera previa (11).")
                        continue

                    bank_code = line[2:6]
                    branch_code = line[6:10]
                    account_num = line[10:20]
                    num_debits = int(line[20:25])
                    tot_debits = int(line[25:39])
                    num_credits = int(line[39:44])
                    tot_credits = int(line[44:58])
                    fin_bal_type = line[58]
                    fin_bal_cents = int(line[59:73])
                    currency = cls._parse_currency(line[73:76])

                    footer = Norma43AccountFooter(
                        bank_code=bank_code,
                        branch_code=branch_code,
                        account_number=account_num,
                        num_debits=num_debits,
                        total_debits_cents=tot_debits,
                        num_credits=num_credits,
                        total_credits_cents=tot_credits,
                        final_balance_cents=fin_bal_cents,
                        final_balance_type=fin_bal_type,
                        currency=currency,
                    )
                    current_block.footer = footer
                    cls._verify_block_balance(current_block, result)
                    current_movement = None

                elif rec_type == "88":
                    # Registro final de fichero
                    break

                else:
                    result.validation_errors.append(f"Línea {line_idx}: Tipo de registro desconocido o inválido '{rec_type}'.")

            except Exception as e:
                result.validation_errors.append(f"Error procesando línea {line_idx} (Tipo {rec_type}): {str(e)}")

        # Validar cierre de cuentas (detección de fichero truncado por EOF prematuro)
        for block in result.accounts:
            if not block.footer:
                result.validation_errors.append(
                    f"Cuenta {block.header.account_number}: Fichero truncado, falta registro de fin de cuenta (33)."
                )

        if not result.accounts and not result.validation_errors:
            result.validation_errors.append("Fichero sin registros de cuenta válidos (11).")

        # Convertir a entidades canónicas si es válido
        if not result.validation_errors and result.accounts:
            result.is_valid = True
            for block in result.accounts:
                iban_synth = f"ES00{block.header.bank_code}{block.header.branch_code}00{block.header.account_number}"
                for mov in block.movements:
                    tx_type = TransactionType.DEPOSIT if mov.debit_credit == "2" else TransactionType.WITHDRAWAL
                    full_desc = mov.full_concept

                    canonical_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
                        transaction_id=mov.movement_id,
                        account_iban=iban_synth,
                        operation_date=mov.operation_date,
                        value_date=mov.value_date,
                        transaction_type=tx_type,
                        amount_cents=mov.amount_cents,
                        currency=block.header.currency,
                        common_code=mov.common_code,
                        concept=full_desc,
                        payer_or_payee=mov.reference_1 or full_desc,
                        raw_reference=mov.reference_2 or mov.document_number,
                    )
                    result.canonical_transactions.append(canonical_tx)

        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_date_yymmdd(str_val: str) -> date:
        # AAMMDD: año 00-49 -> 2000-2049, 50-99 -> 1950-1999
        yy = int(str_val[0:2])
        mm = int(str_val[2:4])
        dd = int(str_val[4:6])
        full_year = 2000 + yy if yy < 50 else 1900 + yy
        return date(full_year, mm, dd)

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_currency(code_str: str) -> str:
        # En norma 43 '978' o 'EUR' representan euros
        clean = code_str.strip()
        if clean in ("978", "EUR", ""):
            return "EUR"
        if clean in ("840", "USD"):
            return "USD"
        return clean

    @staticmethod
    def _verify_block_balance(block: Norma43AccountBlock, result: Norma43ParseResult) -> None:
        """
        Verifica matemáticamente que el saldo inicial, abonos, cargos y saldo final
        satisfagan la ecuación de cierre del extracto contable:
        Saldo Inicial con signo + Total Abonos - Total Cargos = Saldo Final con signo
        """
        if not block.footer:
            return

        header = block.header
        footer = block.footer

        # Clave 1 = Deudor (negativo), Clave 2 = Acreedor (positivo)
        signed_init = header.initial_balance_cents if header.initial_balance_type == "2" else -header.initial_balance_cents
        signed_final = footer.final_balance_cents if footer.final_balance_type == "2" else -footer.final_balance_cents

        expected_final = signed_init + footer.total_credits_cents - footer.total_debits_cents
        discrepancy = signed_final - expected_final

        # Comprobar también el sumatorio de apuntes leídos
        actual_debits = sum(m.amount_cents for m in block.movements if m.debit_credit == "1")
        actual_credits = sum(m.amount_cents for m in block.movements if m.debit_credit == "2")

        if actual_debits != footer.total_debits_cents:
            result.validation_errors.append(
                f"Cuenta {header.account_number}: El sumatorio de cargos ({actual_debits} céntimos) "
                f"no coincide con el registro 33 ({footer.total_debits_cents} céntimos)."
            )
        if actual_credits != footer.total_credits_cents:
            result.validation_errors.append(
                f"Cuenta {header.account_number}: El sumatorio de abonos ({actual_credits} céntimos) "
                f"no coincide con el registro 33 ({footer.total_credits_cents} céntimos)."
            )

        actual_num_debits = sum(1 for m in block.movements if m.debit_credit == "1")
        actual_num_credits = sum(1 for m in block.movements if m.debit_credit == "2")

        if actual_num_debits != footer.num_debits:
            result.validation_errors.append(
                f"Cuenta {header.account_number}: El número de cargos ({actual_num_debits}) "
                f"no coincide con el registro 33 ({footer.num_debits})."
            )
        if actual_num_credits != footer.num_credits:
            result.validation_errors.append(
                f"Cuenta {header.account_number}: El número de abonos ({actual_num_credits}) "
                f"no coincide con el registro 33 ({footer.num_credits})."
            )

        if discrepancy != 0:
            block.is_balanced = False
            block.balance_discrepancy_cents = discrepancy
            result.validation_errors.append(
                f"Cuenta {header.account_number}: Descuadre matemático de extracto. "
                f"Saldo final esperado: {expected_final} céntimos, registrado: {signed_final} céntimos (Diferencia: {discrepancy} céntimos)."
            )
        else:
            block.is_balanced = True
            block.balance_discrepancy_cents = 0


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\parsers\base.py
================================================================================

"""
Modelos de Datos Canónicos para Ingesta Multiformato
Desacopla los diferentes orígenes bancarios y de pasarelas en estructuras estandarizadas.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional


class TransactionType(str, Enum):
    DEPOSIT = "DEPOSIT"          # Abono / Haber (incremento en cuenta)
    WITHDRAWAL = "WITHDRAWAL"    # Cargo / Debe (salida de dinero)


@dataclass
class CanonicalBankTransaction:
    """Representa un apunte bancario unificado independientemente de su origen (Norma 43, CAMT, CSV)."""
    transaction_id: str
    account_iban: str
    operation_date: date
    value_date: date
    transaction_type: TransactionType
    amount_cents: int                  # En céntimos enteros (positivo siempre)
    currency: str = "EUR"
    common_code: Optional[str] = None  # Código concepto AEB (04 transf, 17 comisiones, etc.)
    concept: str = ""                  # Descripción o concepto consolidado
    payer_or_payee: str = ""           # Nombre ordenante / beneficiario
    fiscal_id: Optional[str] = None    # NIF/CIF extraído si está presente
    raw_reference: Optional[str] = None

    @property
    def signed_amount_cents(self) -> int:
        return self.amount_cents if self.transaction_type == TransactionType.DEPOSIT else -self.amount_cents

    @property
    def amount_eur(self) -> Decimal:
        return Decimal(self.amount_cents) / Decimal(100)


@dataclass
class CanonicalInvoice:
    """Factura viva / abierta en el sistema contable."""
    invoice_id: str
    invoice_number: str
    issue_date: date
    due_date: date
    customer_id: str
    customer_name: str
    gross_amount_cents: int = 0        # Total a cobrar en céntimos
    customer_fiscal_id: Optional[str] = None
    concept: str = ""
    gateway_id: Optional[str] = None   # Pasarela asignada (p.ej. 'stripe', 'redsys_tpv1')
    currency: str = "EUR"

    @property
    def gross_eur(self) -> Decimal:
        return Decimal(self.gross_amount_cents) / Decimal(100)


@dataclass
class CanonicalGatewayTransaction:
    """Transacción individual procesada por pasarela (cobro, devolución o comisión)."""
    transaction_id: str
    gateway_id: str                    # 'stripe', 'redsys', 'paypal', 'sumup'
    created_at: datetime
    type: str                          # 'charge', 'refund', 'fee', 'adjustment'
    gross_cents: int                   # Importe bruto
    fee_cents: int                     # Comisión real aplicada
    net_cents: int                     # Importe neto liquidable
    currency: str = "EUR"
    order_id: Optional[str] = None
    customer_email: Optional[str] = None
    customer_name: Optional[str] = None
    payout_id: Optional[str] = None    # ID de liquidación si ya viene asignado


@dataclass
class CanonicalBatchPayout:
    """Liquidación agrupada transferida a la cuenta bancaria."""
    payout_id: str
    gateway_id: str
    payout_date: date
    net_amount_cents: int              # Total abonado en banco en céntimos
    currency: str = "EUR"
    status: str = "paid"
    transactions: List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction] = field(default_factory=list)


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\parsers\gateways\redsys.py
================================================================================

"""
Parser de Liquidaciones y Operaciones de Redsys (TPV Virtual y Físico)
Soporta volcados tabulares de Redsys en CSV / Excel exportados desde el portal de administración del banco.
"""
import csv
import io
import math
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union

from src.parsers.base import CanonicalBatchPayout, CanonicalGatewayTransaction


class RedsysParser:
    @staticmethod
    def parse_operations_csv(csv_content: Union[str, bytes], delimiter: str = ";") -> List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction]:
        """
        Parsea el volcado estándar de operaciones de Redsys TPV (Canalweb / Comercios).
        Campos típicos en España:
        - Terminal / TPV
        - Fecha / Hora
        - Número Pedido / Referencia
        - Tipo Operación (0000 / SIS0000 = Autorización/Venta, 0900 = Devolución)
        - Importe / Importe Bruto
        - Tasa de Descuento / Comisión
        - Importe Líquido / Neto
        - Número Autorización
        """
        if isinstance(csv_content, bytes):
            # Redsys a veces descarga en windows-1252 o latin-1
            for enc in ["utf-8", "windows-1252", "iso-8859-15"]:
                try:
                    text = csv_content.decode(enc)
                    break
                except UnicodeDecodeError:
                    continue
            else:
                text = csv_content.decode("latin1", errors="replace")
        else:
            text = csv_content

        # Auto-detectar delimitador si no es ';'
        sample_line = text.splitlines()[0] if text.splitlines() else ""
        if ";" not in sample_line and "," in sample_line:
            delimiter = ","

        reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(text), delimiter=delimiter)
        transactions: List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction] = []

        for row in reader:
            # Normalizar nombres de columnas eliminando espacios y mayúsculas
            norm_row = {
                k.strip().lower(): (v.strip() if v is not None else "")
                for k, v in row.items()
                if k and isinstance(k, str)
            }

            order_id = (
                norm_row.get("número pedido")
                or norm_row.get("num. pedido")
                or norm_row.get("pedido")
                or norm_row.get("referencia")
                or norm_row.get("orden")
                or ""
            )
            auth_code = norm_row.get("autorización") or norm_row.get("autorizacion") or norm_row.get("código aut.") or ""
            tx_id = f"RED-{order_id}-{auth_code}" if order_id or auth_code else f"RED-{len(transactions)+1}"

            op_type_raw = norm_row.get("tipo operación") or norm_row.get("tipo operacion") or norm_row.get("operación") or "0000"
            is_refund = "0900" in op_type_raw or "devolución" in op_type_raw.lower() or "devolucion" in op_type_raw.lower()
            tx_type = "refund" if is_refund else "charge"

            gross_str = (
                norm_row.get("importe bruto")
                or norm_row.get("importe")
                or norm_row.get("cantidad")
                or "0"
            )
            fee_str = (
                norm_row.get("tasa descuento")
                or norm_row.get("comisión")
                or norm_row.get("comision")
                or "0"
            )
            net_str = (
                norm_row.get("importe líquido")
                or norm_row.get("importe liquido")
                or norm_row.get("neto")
                or ""
            )

            gross_cents = RedsysParser._to_cents(gross_str)
            fee_cents = RedsysParser._to_cents(fee_str)

            if net_str:
                net_cents = RedsysParser._to_cents(net_str)
            else:
                net_cents = gross_cents - fee_cents

            # Si es devolución, ajustar signo si no viene negativo
            if is_refund:
                if gross_cents > 0:
                    gross_cents = -gross_cents
                if net_cents > 0:
                    net_cents = -net_cents

            date_str = norm_row.get("fecha") or ""
            time_str = norm_row.get("hora") or "00:00:00"
            created_dt = RedsysParser._parse_datetime(f"{date_str} {time_str}".strip())

            tx = CanonicalGatewayTransaction(
                transaction_id=tx_id,
                gateway_id="redsys",
                created_at=created_dt,
                type=tx_type,
                gross_cents=gross_cents,
                fee_cents=fee_cents,
                net_cents=net_cents,
                currency="EUR",
                order_id=order_id,
                customer_name=f"TPV Auth: {auth_code}",
            )
            transactions.append(tx)

        return transactions

    @staticmethod
    def _to_cents(val_str: Optional[str]) -> int:
        if not val_str or not isinstance(val_str, str):
            return 0
        cleaned = val_str.replace("€", "").replace(" ", "").strip()
        if cleaned.lower() in ("nan", "null", "none", "n/a", "--", "inf", "-inf", "infinity", "-infinity"):
            return 0

        # Manejar múltiples puntos o comas
        if "," in cleaned and "." in cleaned:
            if cleaned.rfind(",") > cleaned.rfind("."):
                # Formato europeo: 1.234.567,89
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(".", "").replace(",", ".")
            else:
                # Formato americano: 1,234,567.89
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", "")
        elif "," in cleaned:
            if cleaned.count(",") > 1:
                # Múltiples comas anómalas como 12,34,56 -> quitar separadores intermedios salvo el último
                last_comma = cleaned.rfind(",")
                cleaned = cleaned[:last_comma].replace(",", "") + "." + cleaned[last_comma + 1:]
            else:
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", ".")
        elif "." in cleaned and cleaned.count(".") > 1:
            # Múltiples puntos anómalos como 1.234.567
            last_dot = cleaned.rfind(".")
            cleaned = cleaned[:last_dot].replace(".", "") + "." + cleaned[last_dot + 1:]

        try:
            val = float(cleaned)
            if math.isnan(val) or math.isinf(val):
                return 0
            return int(round(val * 100))
        except (ValueError, OverflowError):
            return 0

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_datetime(dt_str: str) -> datetime:
        formats = [
            "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S",
            "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M",
            "%d-%m-%Y %H:%M:%S",
            "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
            "%d/%m/%Y",
            "%Y-%m-%d",
        ]
        for fmt in formats:
            try:
                return datetime.strptime(dt_str.strip(), fmt)
            except ValueError:
                continue
        return datetime.now()


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\parsers\gateways\stripe.py
================================================================================

"""
Parser de Liquidaciones y Transacciones de Stripe
Procesa informes oficiales de 'Balance Transactions' y liquidaciones (Payouts) de Stripe.
"""
import csv
import io
import math
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union

from src.parsers.base import CanonicalBatchPayout, CanonicalGatewayTransaction


class StripeParser:
    @staticmethod
    def parse_balance_transactions_csv(csv_content: Union[str, bytes]) -> List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction]:
        """
        Procesa el fichero CSV oficial de transacciones de saldo de Stripe
        (campos: id, Type, Gross, Fee, Net, Created (UTC), Description, Source, etc.).
        """
        if isinstance(csv_content, bytes):
            # Stripe usa UTF-8
            text = csv_content.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        else:
            text = csv_content

        reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(text))
        transactions: List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction] = []

        for row in reader:
            tx_id = row.get("id") or row.get("Balance transaction ID") or ""
            if not tx_id:
                continue

            tx_type = row.get("Type") or row.get("Reporting Category") or "charge"
            tx_type = tx_type.lower()

            # Extraer importes y convertir a céntimos enteros
            gross_val = StripeParser._parse_currency_to_cents(row.get("Gross") or row.get("Amount") or "0")
            fee_val = StripeParser._parse_currency_to_cents(row.get("Fee") or "0")
            net_val = StripeParser._parse_currency_to_cents(row.get("Net") or "0")

            currency = (row.get("Currency") or "eur").upper()
            created_str = row.get("Created (UTC)") or row.get("Created") or ""
            created_dt = StripeParser._parse_datetime(created_str)

            order_id = row.get("Order ID") or row.get("Invoice ID") or row.get("Order") or row.get("Source") or row.get("Payment ID") or row.get("Customer ID") or ""
            desc = row.get("Description") or ""
            payout_id = row.get("Payout ID") or row.get("Automatic payout ID") or None

            tx = CanonicalGatewayTransaction(
                transaction_id=tx_id,
                gateway_id="stripe",
                created_at=created_dt,
                type=tx_type,
                gross_cents=gross_val,
                fee_cents=fee_val,
                net_cents=net_val,
                currency=currency,
                order_id=order_id,
                customer_name=desc,
                payout_id=payout_id,
            )
            transactions.append(tx)

        return transactions

    @staticmethod
    def group_by_payout(transactions: List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction]) -> Dict[str, CanonicalBatchPayout]:
        """Agrupa transacciones individuales bajo sus respectivos payouts si vienen informados."""
        payouts: Dict[str, CanonicalBatchPayout] = {}
        for tx in transactions:
            pid = tx.payout_id or "unassigned"
            if pid not in payouts:
                payouts[pid] = CanonicalBatchPayout(
                    payout_id=pid,
                    gateway_id="stripe",
                    payout_date=tx.created_at.date(),
                    net_amount_cents=0,
                    currency=tx.currency,
                    transactions=[],
                )
            payouts[pid].transactions.append(tx)
            # Solo sumar cobros y deducciones (no el propio apunte payout que suele ser negativo)
            if tx.type != "payout":
                payouts[pid].net_amount_cents += tx.net_cents

        return payouts

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_currency_to_cents(val_str: Optional[str]) -> int:
        if not val_str or not isinstance(val_str, str):
            return 0
        cleaned = val_str.strip().replace("€", "").replace("$", "").replace(" ", "")
        if cleaned.lower() in ("nan", "null", "none", "n/a", "--", "inf", "-inf", "infinity", "-infinity"):
            return 0
        # Si tiene coma como separador decimal
        if "," in cleaned and "." not in cleaned:
            cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", ".")
        elif "," in cleaned and "." in cleaned:
            # ej. 1,234.56 o 1.234,56
            if cleaned.rfind(".") > cleaned.rfind(","):
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", "")
            else:
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(".", "").replace(",", ".")
        try:
            val = float(cleaned)
            if math.isnan(val) or math.isinf(val):
                return 0
            return int(round(val * 100))
        except (ValueError, OverflowError):
            return 0

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_datetime(dt_str: str) -> datetime:
        formats = [
            "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
            "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M",
            "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ",
            "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z",
            "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S",
            "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M",
        ]
        for fmt in formats:
            try:
                return datetime.strptime(dt_str.strip(), fmt)
            except ValueError:
                continue
        return datetime.now()


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\parsers\mapper.py
================================================================================

"""
Autodescubridor y Mapeador Inteligente de Ficheros de Clientes
Detecta automáticamente encabezados y estructuras heterogéneas enviadas por micropymes
(Hojas de cálculo de facturación, extractos CSV no estructurados).
"""
import csv
import io
import math
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Union

from src.parsers.base import CanonicalInvoice


class ColumnMapper:
    """Mapeo difuso de sinónimos habituales en facturas emitidas por pymes españolas."""
    SYNONYMS = {
        "invoice_number": ["número", "numero", "nº factura", "num_factura", "factura", "doc", "invoice_no", "invoice_id"],
        "issue_date": ["fecha", "fecha emisión", "fecha_emision", "date", "f.factura", "fecha_fra"],
        "due_date": ["vencimiento", "fecha vencimiento", "fecha_vto", "f.vto", "due_date"],
        "customer_name": ["cliente", "nombre", "razón social", "razon_social", "destinatario", "customer"],
        "customer_fiscal_id": ["cif", "nif", "cif/nif", "nif/cif", "dni", "vat_number", "id_fiscal"],
        "gross_amount": ["total", "importe", "total factura", "bruto", "amount", "total_fra", "importe_total"],
        "concept": ["concepto", "descripción", "descripcion", "observaciones", "artículos", "lineas"],
        "gateway_id": ["pasarela", "forma de pago", "forma_pago", "medio_pago", "gateway", "canal_cobro"],
    }

    @classmethod
    def detect_mapping(cls, headers: List[Optional[str]]) -> Dict[str, str]:
        detected = {}
        if not headers:
            return detected

        cleaned_headers = [
            (h.strip().lower() if h and isinstance(h, str) else "")
            for h in headers
        ]

        for target_field, candidates in cls.SYNONYMS.items():
            for idx, raw_h in enumerate(cleaned_headers):
                if not raw_h:
                    continue
                orig_h = headers[idx]
                if orig_h is None:
                    continue
                if raw_h in candidates:
                    detected[target_field] = orig_h
                    break
                # Búsqueda por contención
                elif any(cand in raw_h for cand in candidates):
                    detected[target_field] = orig_h
                    break

        return detected

    @classmethod
    def parse_invoices_csv(cls, csv_content: Union[str, bytes]) -> List[CanonicalInvoice]:
        if isinstance(csv_content, bytes):
            for enc in ["utf-8", "windows-1252", "iso-8859-15"]:
                try:
                    text = csv_content.decode(enc)
                    break
                except UnicodeDecodeError:
                    continue
            else:
                text = csv_content.decode("latin1", errors="replace")
        else:
            text = csv_content

        # Auto-detectar delimitador
        first_line = text.splitlines()[0] if text.splitlines() else ""
        delim = ";" if ";" in first_line else ","

        reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(text), delimiter=delim)
        if not reader.fieldnames:
            return []

        mapping = cls.detect_mapping(reader.fieldnames)
        invoices: List[CanonicalInvoice] = []

        for row in reader:
            if not row:
                continue

            def _safe_get(field_key: str, default: str = "") -> str:
                mapped_col = mapping.get(field_key)
                if not mapped_col:
                    return default
                val = row.get(mapped_col)
                return val.strip() if val and isinstance(val, str) else default

            inv_num = _safe_get("invoice_number", "")
            cust_name = _safe_get("customer_name", "")
            gross_str = _safe_get("gross_amount", "0")

            if not inv_num and not cust_name:
                continue

            gross_cents = cls._to_cents(gross_str)
            issue_date = cls._parse_date(_safe_get("issue_date", ""))
            due_date = cls._parse_date(_safe_get("due_date", "")) or issue_date
            fiscal_id = _safe_get("customer_fiscal_id", "") or None
            concept = _safe_get("concept", "")
            gateway_raw = _safe_get("gateway_id", "")
            gateway = gateway_raw.lower() if gateway_raw else None

            inv = CanonicalInvoice(
                invoice_id=f"INV-{inv_num or len(invoices)+1}",
                invoice_number=inv_num,
                issue_date=issue_date,
                due_date=due_date,
                customer_id=fiscal_id or f"CUST-{len(invoices)+1}",
                customer_name=cust_name,
                customer_fiscal_id=fiscal_id,
                gross_amount_cents=gross_cents,
                concept=concept,
                gateway_id=gateway,
            )
            invoices.append(inv)

        return invoices

    @staticmethod
    def _to_cents(val_str: Optional[str]) -> int:
        if not val_str or not isinstance(val_str, str):
            return 0
        cleaned = val_str.replace("€", "").replace("$", "").replace(" ", "").strip()
        if cleaned.lower() in ("nan", "null", "none", "n/a", "--", "inf", "-inf", "infinity", "-infinity"):
            return 0
        if "," in cleaned and "." in cleaned:
            if cleaned.rfind(",") > cleaned.rfind("."):
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(".", "").replace(",", ".")
            else:
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", "")
        elif "," in cleaned:
            if cleaned.count(",") > 1:
                last_comma = cleaned.rfind(",")
                cleaned = cleaned[:last_comma].replace(",", "") + "." + cleaned[last_comma + 1:]
            else:
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", ".")
        elif "." in cleaned and cleaned.count(".") > 1:
            last_dot = cleaned.rfind(".")
            cleaned = cleaned[:last_dot].replace(".", "") + "." + cleaned[last_dot + 1:]
        try:
            val = float(cleaned)
            if math.isnan(val) or math.isinf(val):
                return 0
            return int(round(val * 100))
        except (ValueError, OverflowError):
            return 0

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_date(dt_str: Optional[str]) -> date:
        if not dt_str or not isinstance(dt_str, str):
            return date.today()
        dt_str = dt_str.strip()
        for fmt in ["%d/%m/%Y", "%Y-%m-%d", "%d-%m-%Y", "%d/%m/%y"]:
            try:
                return datetime.strptime(dt_str, fmt).date()
            except ValueError:
                continue
        return date.today()


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\reconciliation\engine.py
================================================================================

"""
Orquestador de Conciliación Algorítmica de Liquidaciones Agrupadas (Batch Payouts)
Integra:
1. Poda Heurística en 3 niveles
2. Selección automática entre Horowitz-Sahni (Meet-in-the-Middle) y HiGHS MILP
3. Formulación rigurosa con función objetivo contra subconjuntos espurios
4. Generación automática del asiento cuadrado en partida doble (572 / 626 / 430)
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from src.parsers.base import CanonicalBankTransaction, CanonicalInvoice, CanonicalGatewayTransaction
from src.reconciliation.pruning import ReconciliationPruner
from src.reconciliation.meet_in_the_middle import MeetInTheMiddleSolver, SubsetSumCandidate
from src.reconciliation.milp_solver import HighsMILPSolver, MILPInvoiceCandidate, MILPRefundCandidate
from src.accounting.double_entry import AccountingEntryBuilder, DoubleEntryJournal


@dataclass
class ReconciliationReport:
    is_reconciled: bool
    payout_id: str
    target_net_cents: int
    matched_invoice_ids: List[str]
    matched_refund_ids: List[str]
    matched_invoices: List[CanonicalInvoice] = field(default_factory=list)
    total_gross_cents: int = 0
    total_fees_cents: int = 0
    total_net_cents: int = 0
    discrepancy_cents: float = 0.0
    solver_used: str = ""
    journal_entry: Optional[DoubleEntryJournal] = None
    pruning_stats: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


class BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine:
    def __init__(
        self,
        default_alpha_rate: float = 0.015,       # 1.5% comisión variable estimada
        default_beta_cents: int = 25,             # 0.25 € comisión fija
        tolerance_cents: int = 2,
    ):
        self.default_alpha_rate = default_alpha_rate
        self.default_beta_cents = default_beta_cents
        self.tolerance_cents = tolerance_cents

    def reconcile_payout(
        self,
        bank_tx: CanonicalBankTransaction,
        open_invoices: List[CanonicalInvoice],
        gateway_transactions: Optional[List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction]] = None,
        gateway_id: Optional[str] = "stripe",
        bank_account_code: str = "572000001",
        fee_account_code: str = "626000001",
        entry_number: int = 1,
    ) -> ReconciliationReport:
        """
        Concilia un apunte bancario correspondiente al cobro neto de una liquidación
        frente al catálogo de facturas pendientes de cobro.
        """
        target_net = bank_tx.amount_cents
        payout_date = bank_tx.operation_date

        # 1. Poda Heurística
        pruned_res = ReconciliationPruner.prune(
            target_net_cents=target_net,
            payout_date=payout_date,
            candidates=open_invoices,
            gateway_id=gateway_id,
        )
        candidates = pruned_res.invoices
        stats = pruned_res.pruning_stats

        if not candidates:
            return ReconciliationReport(
                is_reconciled=False,
                payout_id=bank_tx.transaction_id,
                target_net_cents=target_net,
                matched_invoice_ids=[],
                matched_refund_ids=[],
                pruning_stats=stats,
            )

        # Mapeo de transacciones de pasarela si se han aportado
        gw_fee_map: Dict[str, int] = {}
        if gateway_transactions:
            for gtx in gateway_transactions:
                if gtx.order_id:
                    gw_fee_map[gtx.order_id] = gtx.fee_cents
                    gw_fee_map[gtx.order_id.replace("-", "").replace(" ", "").upper()] = gtx.fee_cents
                if gtx.transaction_id:
                    gw_fee_map[gtx.transaction_id] = gtx.fee_cents

        # Preparar candidatos para los solvers
        milp_candidates: List[MILPInvoiceCandidate] = []
        mitm_candidates: List[SubsetSumCandidate] = []

        for inv in candidates:
            gross = inv.gross_amount_cents
            # Comisión teórica esperada c_i = alpha * A_i + beta
            theo_fee = int(round(gross * self.default_alpha_rate + self.default_beta_cents))
            
            # Comisión observada (si viene del reporte de pasarela, si no usar la teórica)
            clean_num = inv.invoice_number.replace("-", "").replace(" ", "").upper()
            obs_fee = (
                gw_fee_map.get(inv.invoice_number)
                or gw_fee_map.get(clean_num)
                or gw_fee_map.get(inv.invoice_id)
                or theo_fee
            )
            net_val = gross - obs_fee

            milp_candidates.append(
                MILPInvoiceCandidate(
                    invoice_id=inv.invoice_id,
                    gross_cents=gross,
                    observed_fee_cents=obs_fee,
                    theoretical_fee_cents=theo_fee,
                    net_cents=net_val,
                )
            )

            mitm_candidates.append(
                SubsetSumCandidate(
                    item_id=inv.invoice_id,
                    net_cents=net_val,
                    gross_cents=gross,
                    fee_cents=obs_fee,
                )
            )

        solver_name = ""
        matched_inv_ids: List[str] = []
        delta_val = 0.0

        # Si n <= 24 y no hay discrepancia en comisiones observadas vs teóricas, probar Meet-in-the-Middle
        can_use_mitm = len(mitm_candidates) <= 24 and all(
            m.observed_fee_cents == m.theoretical_fee_cents for m in milp_candidates
        )

        if can_use_mitm:
            mitm_res = MeetInTheMiddleSolver.solve(
                target_net_cents=target_net,
                candidates=mitm_candidates,
                tolerance_cents=self.tolerance_cents,
            )
            if mitm_res:
                matched_inv_ids = mitm_res.matched_ids
                delta_val = float(mitm_res.delta_cents)
                solver_name = "Meet-in-the-Middle (Horowitz-Sahni)"

        # Si MITM no aplicaba o no encontró solución exacta, ejecutar HiGHS MILP con la función objetivo optimizada
        if not matched_inv_ids:
            milp_res = HighsMILPSolver.solve(
                target_net_cents=target_net,
                invoices=milp_candidates,
                tolerance_cents=self.tolerance_cents,
                lambda_1=100.0,
                lambda_2=1.0,
            )
            if milp_res.is_success and milp_res.matched_invoice_ids:
                matched_inv_ids = milp_res.matched_invoice_ids
                delta_val = milp_res.delta_cents
                solver_name = "HiGHS MILP (scipy.optimize.milp)"

        if not matched_inv_ids:
            return ReconciliationReport(
                is_reconciled=False,
                payout_id=bank_tx.transaction_id,
                target_net_cents=target_net,
                matched_invoice_ids=[],
                matched_refund_ids=[],
                pruning_stats=stats,
            )

        # Construir resultado enriquecido
        inv_by_id = {inv.invoice_id: inv for inv in candidates}
        matched_invoices_objs = [inv_by_id[iid] for iid in matched_inv_ids]

        milp_cand_by_id = {m.invoice_id: m for m in milp_candidates}
        total_gross = sum(inv.gross_amount_cents for inv in matched_invoices_objs)
        total_fees = sum(milp_cand_by_id[iid].observed_fee_cents for iid in matched_inv_ids)
        total_net = total_gross - total_fees

        # Preparar datos para el asiento contable
        invoice_rows_for_entry = [
            {
                "account_code": "430000001",
                "invoice_number": inv.invoice_number,
                "gross_cents": inv.gross_amount_cents,
                "fee_cents": milp_cand_by_id[inv.invoice_id].observed_fee_cents,
                "customer_name": inv.customer_name,
            }
            for inv in matched_invoices_objs
        ]

        journal_entry = AccountingEntryBuilder.build_payout_reconciliation_entry(
            entry_number=entry_number,
            entry_date=payout_date,
            bank_account_code=bank_account_code,
            fee_account_code=fee_account_code,
            payout_net_cents=target_net,
            matched_invoices=invoice_rows_for_entry,
            gateway_name=gateway_id.capitalize() if gateway_id else "Pasarela",
        )

        return ReconciliationReport(
            is_reconciled=True,
            payout_id=bank_tx.transaction_id,
            target_net_cents=target_net,
            matched_invoice_ids=matched_inv_ids,
            matched_refund_ids=[],
            matched_invoices=matched_invoices_objs,
            total_gross_cents=total_gross,
            total_fees_cents=total_fees,
            total_net_cents=total_net,
            discrepancy_cents=delta_val,
            solver_used=solver_name,
            journal_entry=journal_entry,
            pruning_stats=stats,
        )


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\reconciliation\meet_in_the_middle.py
================================================================================

"""
Algoritmo de Partición Meet-in-the-Middle (Horowitz-Sahni)
Resuelve el Subset Sum Problem para liquidaciones deterministas con n <= 26
Precomputando las sumas en dos mitades de tamaño n/2 y aplicando búsqueda binaria.
Complejidad O(2^(n/2) * (n/2)), pasando 2^26 (~6.7x10^7) a < 7x10^4 operaciones elementales.
"""
import bisect
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple


@dataclass
class SubsetSumCandidate:
    item_id: str
    net_cents: int
    gross_cents: int
    fee_cents: int


@dataclass
class MeetInTheMiddleResult:
    matched_ids: List[str]
    total_net_cents: int
    delta_cents: int
    is_exact: bool


class MeetInTheMiddleSolver:
    @staticmethod
    def solve(
        target_net_cents: int,
        candidates: List[SubsetSumCandidate],
        tolerance_cents: int = 2
    ) -> Optional[MeetInTheMiddleResult]:
        n = len(candidates)
        if n == 0:
            return None

        # Si n es muy grande para Meet-in-the-Middle (p.ej. > 26), se debe derivar al solver MILP
        if n > 26:
            return None

        mid = n // 2
        left_candidates = candidates[:mid]
        right_candidates = candidates[mid:]

        # Generar todas las combinaciones de la mitad izquierda
        # Lista de tuplas: (suma_neta, mascara_bits)
        left_subsets = MeetInTheMiddleSolver._generate_subsets(left_candidates)
        # Ordenar por suma para búsqueda binaria
        left_subsets.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        left_sums = [s[0] for s in left_subsets]

        # Generar combinaciones de la mitad derecha
        right_subsets = MeetInTheMiddleSolver._generate_subsets(right_candidates)

        best_delta = float("inf")
        best_left_mask = 0
        best_right_mask = 0
        best_total = 0

        # Para cada subconjunto de la derecha, buscar el complemento en la izquierda
        for r_sum, r_mask in right_subsets:
            needed = target_net_cents - r_sum

            # Búsqueda binaria del valor más cercano en la izquierda
            idx = bisect.bisect_left(left_sums, needed)

            # Evaluar vecinos (idx y idx - 1)
            candidates_idx = []
            if idx < len(left_sums):
                candidates_idx.append(idx)
            if idx > 0:
                candidates_idx.append(idx - 1)
            if idx + 1 < len(left_sums):
                candidates_idx.append(idx + 1)

            for c_idx in candidates_idx:
                l_sum, l_mask = left_subsets[c_idx]
                total = l_sum + r_sum
                delta = abs(total - target_net_cents)

                # Si es un match exacto (delta == 0), retornar inmediatamente
                if delta == 0:
                    matched_ids = MeetInTheMiddleSolver._extract_ids(
                        left_candidates, l_mask, right_candidates, r_mask
                    )
                    return MeetInTheMiddleResult(
                        matched_ids=matched_ids,
                        total_net_cents=total,
                        delta_cents=0,
                        is_exact=True,
                    )

                if delta < best_delta:
                    best_delta = delta
                    best_left_mask = l_mask
                    best_right_mask = r_mask
                    best_total = total

        # Si el mejor delta entra dentro de la tolerancia admitida (<= 2 céntimos)
        if best_delta <= tolerance_cents and (best_left_mask > 0 or best_right_mask > 0):
            matched_ids = MeetInTheMiddleSolver._extract_ids(
                left_candidates, best_left_mask, right_candidates, best_right_mask
            )
            return MeetInTheMiddleResult(
                matched_ids=matched_ids,
                total_net_cents=best_total,
                delta_cents=int(best_delta),
                is_exact=False,
            )

        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _generate_subsets(candidates: List[SubsetSumCandidate]) -> List[Tuple[int, int]]:
        k = len(candidates)
        subsets = []
        num_combinations = 1 << k
        for mask in range(num_combinations):
            curr_sum = 0
            for i in range(k):
                if (mask >> i) & 1:
                    curr_sum += candidates[i].net_cents
            subsets.append((curr_sum, mask))
        return subsets

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_ids(
        left_candidates: List[SubsetSumCandidate],
        left_mask: int,
        right_candidates: List[SubsetSumCandidate],
        right_mask: int,
    ) -> List[str]:
        ids = []
        for i in range(len(left_candidates)):
            if (left_mask >> i) & 1:
                ids.append(left_candidates[i].item_id)
        for j in range(len(right_candidates)):
            if (right_mask >> j) & 1:
                ids.append(right_candidates[j].item_id)
        return ids


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\reconciliation\milp_solver.py
================================================================================

"""
Solver Combinatorio MILP con HiGHS (scipy.optimize.milp)
Implementa la formulación rigurosa con función objetivo parametrizada:
min ( lambda_1 * delta + lambda_2 * sum(|c_i - c_i^teorico| * x_i) + lambda_3 * sum(|c'_j - c'_j^teorico| * y_j) )
Sujeto a:
  -delta <= sum((A_i - c_i)*x_i) - sum((R_j + c'_j)*y_j) - P_net <= delta
  0 <= delta <= tolerancia
  x_i, y_j in {0, 1}
Elimina la factibilidad trivial (c=0) y previene la selección de combinaciones espurias.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import LinearConstraint, Bounds, milp


@dataclass
class MILPInvoiceCandidate:
    invoice_id: str
    gross_cents: int
    observed_fee_cents: int
    theoretical_fee_cents: int
    net_cents: int


@dataclass
class MILPRefundCandidate:
    refund_id: str
    gross_cents: int             # Positivo
    reversal_cost_cents: int     # Coste de reversión
    net_deduction_cents: int     # gross + cost


@dataclass
class MILPSolverResult:
    is_success: bool
    matched_invoice_ids: List[str]
    matched_refund_ids: List[str]
    delta_cents: float
    total_invoices_net_cents: int
    total_refunds_net_cents: int
    total_fee_deviation_cents: float
    objective_value: float


class HighsMILPSolver:
    @staticmethod
    def solve(
        target_net_cents: int,
        invoices: List[MILPInvoiceCandidate],
        refunds: Optional[List[MILPRefundCandidate]] = None,
        tolerance_cents: int = 2,
        lambda_1: float = 100.0,   # Peso de penalización por desviación respecto al neto bancario (delta)
        lambda_2: float = 1.0,     # Peso de penalización por desvío de comisiones en ventas
        lambda_3: float = 1.0,     # Peso de penalización por desvío de costes en devoluciones
    ) -> MILPSolverResult:
        refunds = refunds or []
        n = len(invoices)
        m = len(refunds)

        if n == 0 and m == 0:
            return MILPSolverResult(
                is_success=False,
                matched_invoice_ids=[],
                matched_refund_ids=[],
                delta_cents=0.0,
                total_invoices_net_cents=0,
                total_refunds_net_cents=0,
                total_fee_deviation_cents=0.0,
                objective_value=0.0,
            )

        # Variables:
        # [0 .. n-1]: x_i (binarias facturas)
        # [n .. n+m-1]: y_j (binarias devoluciones)
        # [n+m]: delta (holgura continua)
        total_vars = n + m + 1
        delta_idx = n + m

        # 1. Vector de costes de la función objetivo:
        # c^T * v = lambda_2 * sum(|c_i - c_i^teorico| * x_i) + lambda_3 * sum(costes_y * y_j) + lambda_1 * delta
        c_obj = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=np.float64)

        for i, inv in enumerate(invoices):
            fee_dev = abs(inv.observed_fee_cents - inv.theoretical_fee_cents)
            # Para romper empates en soluciones factibles idénticas, añadimos un término minúsculo (-1e-4)
            # que favorece incluir facturas legítimas frente a dejar el sistema ocioso
            c_obj[i] = lambda_2 * fee_dev - 1e-4

        for j, ref in enumerate(refunds):
            c_obj[n + j] = lambda_3 * ref.reversal_cost_cents

        c_obj[delta_idx] = lambda_1

        # 2. Vector de integralidad:
        # 1 para variables binarias/enteras (x_i, y_j), 0 para continua (delta)
        integrality = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=int)
        integrality[: n + m] = 1  # Binarias

        # 3. Límites (Bounds):
        # x_i in [0, 1], y_j in [0, 1], delta in [0, tolerance_cents]
        lb = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=np.float64)
        ub = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=np.float64)
        ub[delta_idx] = float(tolerance_cents)

        bounds = Bounds(lb=lb, ub=ub)

        # 4. Restricciones lineales:
        # S = sum(N_i * x_i) - sum((R_j + c'_j) * y_j)
        # R1: S - delta <= P_net
        # R2: S + delta >= P_net  <=>  -S - delta <= -P_net
        # Formato scipy: lb <= A @ v <= ub
        
        # Coeficientes para S:
        s_coeffs = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=np.float64)
        for i, inv in enumerate(invoices):
            s_coeffs[i] = float(inv.net_cents)
        for j, ref in enumerate(refunds):
            s_coeffs[n + j] = -float(ref.net_deduction_cents)

        # Fila 1: S - delta <= P_net  -->  -inf <= S - delta <= P_net
        row1 = np.copy(s_coeffs)
        row1[delta_idx] = -1.0

        # Fila 2: S + delta >= P_net  -->  P_net <= S + delta <= inf
        row2 = np.copy(s_coeffs)
        row2[delta_idx] = 1.0

        A = np.vstack([row1, row2])
        constraint_lb = np.array([-np.inf, float(target_net_cents)], dtype=np.float64)
        constraint_ub = np.array([float(target_net_cents), np.inf], dtype=np.float64)

        constraints = LinearConstraint(A, lb=constraint_lb, ub=constraint_ub)

        # Resolver mediante HiGHS
        res = milp(c=c_obj, integrality=integrality, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds)

        if not res.success:
            return MILPSolverResult(
                is_success=False,
                matched_invoice_ids=[],
                matched_refund_ids=[],
                delta_cents=0.0,
                total_invoices_net_cents=0,
                total_refunds_net_cents=0,
                total_fee_deviation_cents=0.0,
                objective_value=0.0,
            )

        sol = res.x
        x_sol = np.round(sol[:n]).astype(int)
        y_sol = np.round(sol[n : n + m]).astype(int)
        delta_val = float(sol[delta_idx])

        matched_inv_ids = [invoices[i].invoice_id for i in range(n) if x_sol[i] == 1]
        matched_ref_ids = [refunds[j].refund_id for j in range(m) if y_sol[j] == 1]

        # Verificar que se seleccionó al menos un elemento si el target > 0
        if target_net_cents > 0 and len(matched_inv_ids) == 0:
            return MILPSolverResult(
                is_success=False,
                matched_invoice_ids=[],
                matched_refund_ids=[],
                delta_cents=0.0,
                total_invoices_net_cents=0,
                total_refunds_net_cents=0,
                total_fee_deviation_cents=0.0,
                objective_value=0.0,
            )

        tot_inv_net = sum(invoices[i].net_cents for i in range(n) if x_sol[i] == 1)
        tot_ref_net = sum(refunds[j].net_deduction_cents for j in range(m) if y_sol[j] == 1)
        tot_fee_dev = sum(
            abs(invoices[i].observed_fee_cents - invoices[i].theoretical_fee_cents)
            for i in range(n)
            if x_sol[i] == 1
        )

        return MILPSolverResult(
            is_success=True,
            matched_invoice_ids=matched_inv_ids,
            matched_refund_ids=matched_ref_ids,
            delta_cents=round(delta_val, 2),
            total_invoices_net_cents=tot_inv_net,
            total_refunds_net_cents=tot_ref_net,
            total_fee_deviation_cents=float(tot_fee_dev),
            objective_value=float(res.fun),
        )


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\reconciliation\pruning.py
================================================================================

"""
Motor de Poda Heurística en 3 Niveles para Mitigación de Complejidad Combinatoria
Reduce el espacio de búsqueda de O(2^N) a O(2^k) con k <= 25 antes de invocar los solvers.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, timedelta
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple

from src.parsers.base import CanonicalInvoice


@dataclass
class PrunedCandidates:
    invoices: List[CanonicalInvoice]
    pruning_stats: dict


class ReconciliationPruner:
    @staticmethod
    def prune(
        target_net_cents: int,
        payout_date: date,
        candidates: List[CanonicalInvoice],
        gateway_id: Optional[str] = None,
        max_lookback_days: int = 14,
        alpha_max: float = 0.05,    # Comisión porcentual máxima de pasarela (p.ej. 5%)
        beta_max_cents: int = 50,    # Comisión fija máxima por transacción en céntimos (0.50 €)
    ) -> PrunedCandidates:
        """
        Aplica los 3 niveles de poda descritos en el estudio de viabilidad:
        1. Ventana Temporal Dinámica: [t_payout - max_lookback_days, t_payout]
        2. Partición por Metadatos de Pasarela (Gateway ID)
        3. Acotación por Horquilla de Comisiones (Bounding Box)
        """
        initial_count = len(candidates)
        stats = {
            "initial_count": initial_count,
            "after_temporal": 0,
            "after_gateway": 0,
            "after_bounding": 0,
            "reduction_percentage": 0.0,
        }

        # Nivel 1: Ventana Temporal Desplazada
        # Una liquidación no puede incluir facturas emitidas después del abono, ni de fecha excesivamente antigua
        min_date = payout_date - timedelta(days=max_lookback_days)
        temporal_filtered = [
            inv for inv in candidates
            if min_date <= inv.issue_date <= payout_date
        ]
        stats["after_temporal"] = len(temporal_filtered)

        # Nivel 2: Partición por Pasarela (Gateway ID)
        # Si la liquidación proviene de Stripe, las facturas pagadas por Redsys o PayPal quedan excluidas
        if gateway_id:
            gw_clean = gateway_id.lower().strip()
            gateway_filtered = [
                inv for inv in temporal_filtered
                if not inv.gateway_id or inv.gateway_id.lower().strip() == gw_clean
            ]
        else:
            gateway_filtered = temporal_filtered
        stats["after_gateway"] = len(gateway_filtered)

        # Nivel 3: Acotación por Bounding Box de Comisiones
        # El importe bruto de una factura individual no puede ser inferior a un umbral mínimo
        # ni exceder sustancialmente el neto del payout bancario
        max_possible_gross = int(round((target_net_cents + beta_max_cents) / (1.0 - alpha_max)))
        bounding_filtered = [
            inv for inv in gateway_filtered
            if 0 < inv.gross_amount_cents <= max_possible_gross
        ]
        stats["after_bounding"] = len(bounding_filtered)

        reduction = (1.0 - (len(bounding_filtered) / initial_count)) * 100.0 if initial_count > 0 else 0.0
        stats["reduction_percentage"] = round(reduction, 2)

        return PrunedCandidates(invoices=bounding_filtered, pruning_stats=stats)
