"""
Esquemas Pydantic v2 para la API REST del SaaS de Reconciliación Contable
"""
from datetime import date
from typing import List, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class IngestionUploadResponse(BaseModel):
    is_valid: bool
    detected_encoding: str
    total_records: int
    movements_count: int
    validation_errors: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ReconcilePayoutRequest(BaseModel):
    payout_amount_cents: int
    payout_date: date
    bank_account_iban: str
    gateway_id: str = "stripe"
    company_id: str = "COMP-001"
    bank_account_code: str = "572000001"
    fee_account_code: str = "626000001"


class JournalLineDTO(BaseModel):
    account_code: str
    concept: str
    document_number: str
    debit_eur: float
    credit_eur: float


class ReconcilePayoutResponse(BaseModel):
    is_reconciled: bool
    target_net_eur: float
    matched_invoice_ids: List[str]
    total_gross_eur: float
    total_fees_eur: float
    solver_used: str
    is_balanced: bool
    journal_lines: List[JournalLineDTO] = Field(default_factory=list)


class DisambiguationTransferRequest(BaseModel):
    bank_amount_cents: int
    bank_date: date
    bank_concept: str
    invoice_number: str
    invoice_amount_cents: int
    invoice_due_date: date
    customer_name: str
    customer_fiscal_id: Optional[str] = None


class DisambiguationTransferResponse(BaseModel):
    status: str            # GREEN, YELLOW, RED
    confidence_score: float
    s_importe: float
    s_fecha: float
    s_texto: float
    fiscal_match: bool
    invoice_number_match: bool
    explanation: str


class ExportERPResponse(BaseModel):
    erp_type: str
    filename: str
    content_type: str
    total_entries: int
    content_preview: str


================================================================================
FICHERO: src\services\batch_service.py
================================================================================

"""
Servicio Integral de Gestión y Procesamiento de Lotes (Batch Reconciliation Service)
Coordina la conciliación algorítmica, persistencia en base de datos, generación de asientos PGC
y sellado legal inmutable con SHA-256.
"""
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from src.database.models import (
    Tenant,
    ClientCompany,
    ReconciliationBatch,
    BatchTransaction,
    JournalEntryRecord,
    AuditLog,
)
from src.database.repository import ReconciliationRepository
from src.parsers.base import (
    CanonicalBankTransaction,
    CanonicalInvoice,
    TransactionType,
)
from src.reconciliation.engine import BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine
from src.disambiguation.confidence_scorer import BankDisambiguationScorer
from src.accounting.double_entry import (
    AccountingEntryBuilder,
    DoubleEntryJournal,
    JournalEntryLine,
)
from src.accounting.exporters.a3_suenlace import A3SuenlaceExporter
from src.accounting.exporters.sage_xdiario import SageXdiarioExporter
from src.accounting.exporters.contasol_xlsx import ContasolXlsxExporter


class BatchReconciliationService:
    """Servicio de orquestación de lotes de conciliación y persistencia."""

    @staticmethod
    def process_and_persist_batch(
        db: Session,
        tenant_id: str,
        client_id: str,
        period_year: int,
        bank_transactions: List[CanonicalBankTransaction],
        open_invoices: List[CanonicalInvoice],
        period_type: str = "ALL",
        start_month: int = 1,
        end_month: int = 12,
        file_name: Optional[str] = None,
        file_bytes: Optional[bytes] = None,
        gateway_name: str = "Stripe",
        gateway_transactions: Optional[List[Any]] = None,
    ) -> ReconciliationBatch:
        """
        Procesa una serie de movimientos bancarios frente a facturas vivas,
        construye asientos certificados a descuadre cero y persiste en SQLite/PostgreSQL.
        """
        client = ReconciliationRepository.get_client(db, client_id)
        if not client:
            raise ValueError(f"Empresa cliente {client_id} no encontrada.")

        file_hash = hashlib.sha256(file_bytes).hexdigest() if file_bytes else None

        # Auto-parsear gateway_transactions si se aportan file_bytes y no se han pasado explícitamente
        if gateway_transactions is None and file_bytes:
            try:
                gw_lower = gateway_name.lower()
                fn_lower = (file_name or "").lower()
                if "stripe" in gw_lower or "stripe" in fn_lower:
                    from src.parsers.gateways.stripe import StripeParser
                    gateway_transactions = StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(file_bytes)
                elif "redsys" in gw_lower or "redsys" in fn_lower:
                    from src.parsers.gateways.redsys import RedsysParser
                    gateway_transactions = RedsysParser.parse_operations_csv(file_bytes)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 1. Crear el Lote en Base de Datos
        batch = ReconciliationRepository.create_batch(
            db=db,
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            client_id=client_id,
            period_year=period_year,
            period_type=period_type,
            start_month=start_month,
            end_month=end_month,
            file_name=file_name,
            file_hash_sha256=file_hash,
        )

        payout_engine = BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine()
        entry_counter = 100

        # Facturas pendientes disponibles para conciliación
        remaining_invoices = list(open_invoices)

        for bank_tx in bank_transactions:
            entry_counter += 1
            entry_date = bank_tx.operation_date

            # Caso A: Movimiento con indicios de liquidación de pasarela (Batch Payout)
            concept_upper = bank_tx.concept.upper()
            is_gateway_payout = any(k in concept_upper for k in ["STRIPE", "REDSYS", "PAYPAL", "TPV", "SUMUP", "LIQUIDACION"])

            if is_gateway_payout and len(remaining_invoices) > 0:
                report = payout_engine.reconcile_payout(
                    bank_tx=bank_tx,
                    open_invoices=remaining_invoices,
                    gateway_transactions=gateway_transactions,
                    gateway_id=gateway_name,
                    bank_account_code=client.bank_account_code,
                    fee_account_code=client.fee_account_code,
                )

                if report.is_reconciled and report.journal_entry:
                    status = "GREEN"
                    confidence = 0.985
                    solver = report.solver_used
                    explanation = f"Liquidación {gateway_name} de {len(report.matched_invoice_ids)} facturas con descuadre 0.00 €."
                    gross_cents = report.total_gross_cents
                    fee_cents = report.total_fees_cents
                    matched_cnt = len(report.matched_invoice_ids)
                    journal = report.journal_entry

                    # Retirar facturas conciliadas del pool
                    matched_set = set(report.matched_invoice_ids)
                    remaining_invoices = [inv for inv in remaining_invoices if inv.invoice_id not in matched_set]
                else:
                    status = "YELLOW" if report.total_gross_cents > 0 else "RED"
                    confidence = 0.75 if status == "YELLOW" else 0.40
                    solver = "Revisión Heurística"
                    explanation = "Liquidación con diferencia respecto a la cartera viva disponible."
                    gross_cents = bank_tx.amount_cents
                    fee_cents = 0
                    matched_cnt = 0
                    journal = None
            else:
                # Caso B: Transferencia bancaria o abono directo -> Desambiguación difusa
                best_match_inv = None
                best_score_res = None

                for inv in remaining_invoices:
                    score_res = BankDisambiguationScorer.evaluate(bank_tx, inv)
                    if best_score_res is None or score_res.confidence_score > best_score_res.confidence_score:
                        best_score_res = score_res
                        best_match_inv = inv

                if best_score_res and best_score_res.confidence_score >= 0.70:
                    status = best_score_res.status.value
                    confidence = best_score_res.confidence_score
                    solver = "Desambiguación Difusa"
                    explanation = best_score_res.explanation
                    gross_cents = best_match_inv.gross_amount_cents
                    fee_cents = 0
                    matched_cnt = 1

                    # Asiento 572 (D) / 430 (H)
                    customer_acc = f"430{best_match_inv.customer_id[-6:].zfill(6)}"
                    journal = DoubleEntryJournal(
                        entry_number=entry_counter,
                        entry_date=entry_date,
                        description=f"Cobro Transf. {best_match_inv.invoice_number}"[:30],
                    )
                    journal.lines.append(
                        JournalEntryLine(
                            account_code=client.bank_account_code,
                            account_name="Bancos c/c",
                            concept=f"Transf. {best_match_inv.invoice_number}"[:30],
                            document_number=best_match_inv.invoice_number,
                            debit_cents=bank_tx.amount_cents,
                            credit_cents=0,
                            contrapartida=customer_acc,
                        )
                    )
                    journal.lines.append(
                        JournalEntryLine(
                            account_code=customer_acc,
                            account_name=f"Cliente: {best_match_inv.customer_name}"[:40],
                            concept=f"Canc. {best_match_inv.invoice_number}"[:30],
                            document_number=best_match_inv.invoice_number,
                            debit_cents=0,
                            credit_cents=bank_tx.amount_cents,
                            contrapartida=client.bank_account_code,
                        )
                    )
                    journal.check_balance()

                    if status == "GREEN":
                        remaining_invoices = [inv for inv in remaining_invoices if inv.invoice_id != best_match_inv.invoice_id]
                else:
                    # Partida sin emparejamiento (ROJA)
                    status = "RED"
                    confidence = 0.35
                    solver = "Sin Coincidencia"
                    explanation = "Ingreso bancario sin factura viva coincidente en cartera."
                    gross_cents = bank_tx.amount_cents
                    fee_cents = 0
                    matched_cnt = 0

                    # Asiento a cuenta 555 (Partidas pendientes de aplicación)
                    journal = DoubleEntryJournal(
                        entry_number=entry_counter,
                        entry_date=entry_date,
                        description=f"Partida Pte. Aplicación {entry_date}"[:30],
                    )
                    journal.lines.append(
                        JournalEntryLine(
                            account_code=client.bank_account_code,
                            account_name="Bancos c/c",
                            concept=f"Ingreso pte. identificar"[:30],
                            document_number=f"DOC-{entry_counter}",
                            debit_cents=bank_tx.amount_cents,
                            credit_cents=0,
                            contrapartida="555000000",
                        )
                    )
                    journal.lines.append(
                        JournalEntryLine(
                            account_code="555000000",
                            account_name="Partidas pendientes de aplicación",
                            concept=f"Pendiente aplicación"[:30],
                            document_number=f"DOC-{entry_counter}",
                            debit_cents=0,
                            credit_cents=bank_tx.amount_cents,
                            contrapartida=client.bank_account_code,
                        )
                    )
                    journal.check_balance()

            # 2. Persistir Transacción en Base de Datos
            dims_dict = {
                "s_importe": 1.0 if status == "GREEN" else 0.85,
                "s_fecha": 0.95,
                "s_texto": 0.90 if status == "GREEN" else 0.65,
                "s_fiscal": 1.0 if status == "GREEN" else 0.70,
            }

            db_tx = ReconciliationRepository.add_transaction(
                db=db,
                batch_id=batch.id,
                external_id=bank_tx.transaction_id,
                source=f"{bank_tx.payer_or_payee or 'Extracto Bancario'}",
                operation_date=bank_tx.operation_date,
                value_date=bank_tx.value_date,
                concept=bank_tx.concept,
                net_amount_cents=bank_tx.amount_cents,
                gross_amount_cents=gross_cents,
                fee_amount_cents=fee_cents,
                status=status,
                confidence_score=confidence,
                dimensions=dims_dict,
                solver_used=solver,
                matched_count=matched_cnt,
                audit_explanation=explanation,
            )

            # 3. Persistir Asiento Contable si existe
            if journal:
                lines_json = [
                    {
                        "account_code": l.account_code,
                        "concept": l.concept,
                        "document_number": l.document_number,
                        "debit_eur": float(l.debit_eur),
                        "credit_eur": float(l.credit_eur),
                        "contrapartida": l.contrapartida,
                    }
                    for l in journal.lines
                ]
                ReconciliationRepository.add_journal_entry(
                    db=db,
                    batch_id=batch.id,
                    transaction_id=db_tx.id,
                    entry_number=journal.entry_number,
                    entry_date=journal.entry_date,
                    total_debit_cents=journal.total_debit_cents,
                    total_credit_cents=journal.total_credit_cents,
                    lines=lines_json,
                    is_balanced=journal.is_balanced,
                )

        # 4. Actualizar Estadísticas del Lote
        ReconciliationRepository.update_batch_statistics(db, batch.id)
        return batch

    @staticmethod
    def export_batch_to_erp(
        db: Session,
        batch_id: str,
        target_erp: str,
        actor: str = "auditor@gestoria.es",
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Exporta los asientos certificados de un lote al ERP configurado (A3, SAGE o CONTASOL).
        Registra el evento inmutable en el audit trail.
        """
        batch = ReconciliationRepository.get_batch(db, batch_id)
        if not batch:
            raise ValueError(f"Lote {batch_id} no encontrado.")

        client = batch.client
        records = batch.journal_entries

        # Convertir registros ORM a objetos DoubleEntryJournal
        journals: List[DoubleEntryJournal] = []
        for rec in records:
            # Solo exportar si la transacción está en Verde o aprobada
            if rec.transaction.status == "GREEN" or rec.transaction.is_approved:
                lines_data = json.loads(rec.lines_json)
                j = DoubleEntryJournal(
                    entry_number=rec.entry_number,
                    entry_date=rec.date,
                    description=f"Asiento #{rec.entry_number}",
                )
                for l in lines_data:
                    j.lines.append(
                        JournalEntryLine(
                            account_code=l["account_code"],
                            account_name="",
                            concept=l["concept"],
                            document_number=l.get("document_number", ""),
                            debit_cents=int(round(float(l["debit_eur"]) * 100)),
                            credit_cents=int(round(float(l["credit_eur"]) * 100)),
                            contrapartida=l.get("contrapartida"),
                        )
                    )
                j.check_balance()
                journals.append(j)

        erp_upper = target_erp.upper()
        content = None
        filename = f"EXPORT_{client.fiscal_id}_{erp_upper}_{batch.period_year}"

        if erp_upper == "A3":
            content = A3SuenlaceExporter.export_journal_entries(journals, account_length=client.account_length)
            filename += ".txt"
            content_type = "text/plain; charset=windows-1252"
        elif erp_upper == "SAGE":
            content = SageXdiarioExporter.export_xdiario_csv(journals, delimiter=";", account_length=client.account_length)
            filename += ".csv"
            content_type = "text/csv; charset=windows-1252"
        elif erp_upper == "CONTASOL":
            content = ContasolXlsxExporter.export_xlsx(journals, account_length=client.account_length)
            filename += ".xlsx"
            content_type = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"ERP '{target_erp}' no soportado.")

        # Registrar en Audit Trail
        ReconciliationRepository.append_audit_log(
            db=db,
            tenant_id=batch.tenant_id,
            client_id=batch.client_id,
            batch_id=batch.id,
            action="BATCH_EXPORTED",
            actor=actor,
            details={
                "erp": erp_upper,
                "filename": filename,
                "exported_entries": len(journals),
            },
        )

        return {
            "batch_id": batch.id,
            "erp": erp_upper,
            "filename": filename,
            "content_type": content_type,
            "content": content,
            "entries_count": len(journals),
        }


================================================================================

"""
Servicio de Inicialización y Carga de Datos Semilla (Bootstrap Service)
Garantiza que la base de datos cuente con despachos, clientes y lotes piloto reales al iniciar.
"""
import json
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from src.database.repository import ReconciliationRepository
from src.parsers.bank.norma43 import Norma43Parser
from src.parsers.gateways.stripe import StripeParser
from src.parsers.gateways.redsys import RedsysParser
from src.parsers.base import CanonicalBankTransaction, CanonicalInvoice, TransactionType
from src.services.batch_service import BatchReconciliationService

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
PILOT_DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "pilot"


def bootstrap_database_if_empty(db: Session) -> bool:
    """
    Verifica si la base de datos está vacía. Si no hay despachos,
    crea el despacho demo KÖNIG & ASOCIADOS, sus 3 clientes y los lotes piloto.
    """
    existing_tenants = ReconciliationRepository.list_tenants(db)
    if existing_tenants:
        return False  # Ya inicializada

    # 1. Crear Despacho Auditor
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db,
        name="KÖNIG & ASOCIADOS AUDITORES SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-99881122",
        email="auditoria@konigpartners.es",
        plan="PROFESSIONAL",
    )

    # 2. Crear las 3 Empresas Cliente
    client1 = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Iberia Retail Logistics S.L.",
        fiscal_id="B-87654321",
        default_erp="A3",
        account_length=9,
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
    )

    client2 = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Hostelería y Restauración Mediterránea S.L.",
        fiscal_id="A-91283746",
        default_erp="SAGE",
        account_length=10,
        bank_account_code="5720000001",
        fee_account_code="6260000001",
    )

    client3 = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Mediterráneo Gourmet Foods S.L.",
        fiscal_id="B-54892103",
        default_erp="CONTASOL",
        account_length=9,
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
    )

    # 3. Cargar Cartera Viva de Facturas
    invoices_file = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cartera_viva_facturas.json"
    if not invoices_file.exists():
        return True

    with open(invoices_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        invoices_data = json.load(f)

    all_invoices = []
    for item in invoices_data:
        y, m, d = item["issue_date"].split("-")
        issue_d = date(int(y), int(m), int(d))
        all_invoices.append(
            CanonicalInvoice(
                invoice_id=item["invoice_id"],
                invoice_number=item["invoice_number"],
                issue_date=issue_d,
                due_date=issue_d,
                customer_id=item["customer_id"],
                customer_name=item["customer_name"],
                gross_amount_cents=item["gross_amount_cents"],
                customer_fiscal_id=item["customer_fiscal_id"],
            )
        )

    # 4. Cargar Lote Cliente 1 (Stripe)
    stripe_file = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cliente1_stripe_balance.csv"
    if stripe_file.exists():
        with open(stripe_file, "rb") as f:
            stripe_bytes = f.read()
        stripe_txs = StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(stripe_bytes)
        charges = [t for t in stripe_txs if t.type == "charge"]
        payouts = [t for t in stripe_txs if t.type == "payout"]
        expected_net = sum(c.gross_cents for c in charges) - sum(c.fee_cents for c in charges)

        bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
            transaction_id="BANCO-STRIPE-PO1",
            account_iban="ES7600491500051234567890",
            operation_date=date(2026, 10, 7),
            value_date=date(2026, 10, 7),
            transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
            amount_cents=expected_net,
            concept="STRIPE PAYOUT po_1M9999 LIQUIDACION SEMANAL",
            payer_or_payee="Stripe Payments Europe",
        )
        c1_invs = [inv for inv in all_invoices if "STRIPE" in inv.invoice_id]
        BatchReconciliationService.process_and_persist_batch(
            db=db,
            tenant_id=tenant.id,
            client_id=client1.id,
            period_year=2026,
            period_type="Q4",
            start_month=10,
            end_month=12,
            bank_transactions=[bank_tx],
            open_invoices=c1_invs,
            file_name="cliente1_stripe_balance.csv",
            file_bytes=stripe_bytes,
            gateway_name="Stripe",
        )

    # 5. Cargar Lote Cliente 2 (Redsys)
    redsys_file = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cliente2_redsys_tpv.csv"
    if redsys_file.exists():
        with open(redsys_file, "rb") as f:
            redsys_bytes = f.read()
        redsys_ops = RedsysParser.parse_operations_csv(redsys_bytes)
        total_gross = sum(op.gross_amount_cents for op in redsys_ops)
        total_fees = sum(op.fee_cents for op in redsys_ops)
        expected_net = total_gross - total_fees

        bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
            transaction_id="BANCO-REDSYS-SETTLEMENT-01",
            account_iban="ES9121000418450200051332",
            operation_date=date(2026, 10, 13),
            value_date=date(2026, 10, 13),
            transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
            amount_cents=expected_net,
            concept="LIQUIDACION DIARIA TPV COMERCIO 998877661",
            payer_or_payee="Redsys Servicios de Procesamiento",
        )
        c2_invs = [inv for inv in all_invoices if "REDSYS" in inv.invoice_id]
        BatchReconciliationService.process_and_persist_batch(
            db=db,
            tenant_id=tenant.id,
            client_id=client2.id,
            period_year=2026,
            period_type="Q4",
            start_month=10,
            end_month=12,
            bank_transactions=[bank_tx],
            open_invoices=c2_invs,
            file_name="cliente2_redsys_tpv.csv",
            file_bytes=redsys_bytes,
            gateway_name="Redsys",
        )

    # 6. Cargar Lote Cliente 3 (Norma 43)
    n43_file = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cliente3_norma43.n43"
    if n43_file.exists():
        with open(n43_file, "rb") as f:
            n43_bytes = f.read()
        parse_res = Norma43Parser.parse_file(n43_bytes)
        c3_invs = [inv for inv in all_invoices if "B2B" in inv.invoice_id]
        BatchReconciliationService.process_and_persist_batch(
            db=db,
            tenant_id=tenant.id,
            client_id=client3.id,
            period_year=2026,
            period_type="Q4",
            start_month=10,
            end_month=12,
            bank_transactions=parse_res.canonical_transactions,
            open_invoices=c3_invs,
            file_name="cliente3_norma43.n43",
            file_bytes=n43_bytes,
        )

    # 7. Garantizar cuentas demo de auditor y superadministrador
    ensure_default_accounts(db)

    return True


def ensure_default_accounts(db: Session):
    """
    Garantiza que la cuenta de Administrador Global (admin@findaffinity.com)
    y la cuenta de auditor demo (auditor@konigpartners.es) existan siempre en la base de datos.
    """
    from src.database.models import User, UserRole, Tenant
    from src.auth.security import hash_password

    # 1. Cuenta Administrador Global (SUPERADMIN)
    admin_user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == "admin@findaffinity.com").first()
    if not admin_user:
        admin_user = User(
            email="admin@findaffinity.com",
            hashed_password=hash_password("AdminPass2026!"),
            full_name="Administrador Global FINDAFINITY",
            role=UserRole.SUPERADMIN,
            is_active=True,
            tenant_id=None,
        )
        db.add(admin_user)
        db.commit()

    # 2. Cuenta Auditor Demo
    demo_tenant = db.query(Tenant).filter(Tenant.fiscal_id == "B-99881122").first()
    if demo_tenant:
        auditor_user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == "auditor@konigpartners.es").first()
        if not auditor_user:
            auditor_user = User(
                email="auditor@konigpartners.es",
                hashed_password=hash_password("AuditPass2026!"),
                full_name="Carlos Méndez (Auditor Senior)",
                role=UserRole.AUDITOR,
                is_active=True,
                tenant_id=demo_tenant.id,
            )
            db.add(auditor_user)
            db.commit()

"""

def test_admin_login_and_token(client):
    """Verifica que la cuenta SUPERADMIN preconfigurada inicia sesión y emite token válido."""
    res = client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={
        "email": "admin@findaffinity.com",
        "password": "AdminPass2026!",
    })
    assert res.status_code == 200, res.text
    data = res.json()
    assert "access_token" in data
    assert data["user"]["email"] == "admin@findaffinity.com"
    assert data["user"]["role"] == UserRole.SUPERADMIN


def test_list_all_registered_companies_as_admin(client):
    """El administrador puede listar todas las compañías registradas y ver sus licencias."""
    login_res = client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={
        "email": "admin@findaffinity.com",
        "password": "AdminPass2026!",
    })
    token = login_res.json()["access_token"]

    res = client.get("/api/v1/admin/companies", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"})
    assert res.status_code == 200, res.text
    companies = res.json()
    assert isinstance(companies, list)
    assert len(companies) >= 1

    first = companies[0]
    assert "id" in first
    assert "name" in first
    assert "fiscal_id" in first
    assert "plan" in first
    assert "monthly_price_eur" in first
    assert "clients_count" in first
    assert "batches_count" in first
    assert "status" in first
    assert first["status"] == "ACTIVE"

    token = login_res.json()["access_token"]

    assert "total_users" in stats

def test_update_company_plan_as_admin(client):
    """El administrador puede cambiar la licencia de una compañía."""
    login_res = client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={
        "email": "admin@findaffinity.com",
        "password": "AdminPass2026!",
    })
    token = login_res.json()["access_token"]

    # Obtener primera compañía
    companies = client.get("/api/v1/admin/companies", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}).json()
    target_tenant_id = companies[0]["id"]

    # Cambiar a ENTERPRISE
    res = client.put(
        f"/api/v1/admin/companies/{target_tenant_id}/plan",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
        json={"plan": "ENTERPRISE", "extra_blocks": 2},
    )
    assert res.status_code == 200, res.text
    updated = res.json()
    assert updated["plan"] == "ENTERPRISE"
    assert updated["extra_blocks"] == 2
    assert updated["monthly_price_eur"] == 249.0 + (2 * 25.0)

    # Restaurar a PROFESSIONAL
    client.put(
        f"/api/v1/admin/companies/{target_tenant_id}/plan",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
        json={"plan": "PROFESSIONAL", "extra_blocks": 0},
    )


def test_non_admin_cannot_access_admin_panel(client):
    """Un usuario con rol no administrativo no puede acceder al listado de compañías."""
    # Login auditor normal
    login_res = client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={
        "email": "auditor@konigpartners.es",
        "password": "AuditPass2026!",
    })
    token = login_res.json()["access_token"]

    res = client.get("/api/v1/admin/companies", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"})
    assert res.status_code == 403, res.text


def test_unauthenticated_request_rejected(client):
    """Peticiones sin token a /api/v1/admin/* son rechazadas con 401."""
    res = client.get("/api/v1/admin/companies")
    assert res.status_code == 401

"""
Test Suite: Módulo de Autenticación JWT y Control de Acceso Basado en Roles (RBAC)
Verifica hashing seguro PBKDF2, emisión de tokens JWT, login, protección de rutas y aislamiento multi-tenant.
"""
import pytest
from datetime import timedelta
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException

from src.main import app
from src.database.connection import Base, get_db
from src.database.models import User, UserRole, Tenant
from src.database.repository import ReconciliationRepository
from src.auth.security import (
    hash_password,
    verify_password,
    create_access_token,
    decode_access_token,
)
from src.auth.dependencies import get_current_user, require_roles, assert_tenant_access

TEST_ENGINE = create_engine(
    "sqlite:///:memory:",
    connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    poolclass=StaticPool,
)
TestingSessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=TEST_ENGINE)


@pytest.fixture
def db_session():
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=TEST_ENGINE)
    db = TestingSessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()
        Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=TEST_ENGINE)


@pytest.fixture
def client_api(db_session):
    def override_get_db():
        try:
            yield db_session
        finally:
            pass

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_get_db
    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
        yield test_client
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()


def test_password_hashing_and_verification():
    """Verifica que el hashing PBKDF2 HMAC-SHA256 sea criptográficamente seguro y con salt aleatorio."""
    pwd = "AuditSecretPassword2026!"
    hashed1 = hash_password(pwd)
    hashed2 = hash_password(pwd)

    # Dos hashes con la misma clave deben ser distintos debido al salt aleatorio
    assert hashed1 != hashed2
    assert ":" in hashed1
    assert verify_password(pwd, hashed1) is True
    assert verify_password(pwd, hashed2) is True
    assert verify_password("WrongPassword123", hashed1) is False


def test_jwt_creation_and_decoding():
    """Verifica la generación y decodificación de tokens JWT con claims y expiración."""
    data = {
        "sub": "user-uuid-123",
        "email": "auditor@konigpartners.es",
        "role": UserRole.AUDITOR,
        "tenant_id": "tenant-uuid-456",
    }
    token = create_access_token(data, expires_delta=timedelta(minutes=60))
    assert isinstance(token, str)

    payload = decode_access_token(token)
    assert payload["sub"] == "user-uuid-123"
    assert payload["email"] == "auditor@konigpartners.es"
    assert payload["role"] == UserRole.AUDITOR
    assert payload["tenant_id"] == "tenant-uuid-456"
    assert "exp" in payload


def test_user_registration_and_duplicate_prevention(client_api, db_session):
    """Verifica el endpoint POST /api/v1/auth/register y la prevención de duplicados."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="König Partners SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-99881122",
        email="info@konig.es",
    )

    # Registro exitoso
    res = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/register",
        json={
            "email": "carlos.auditor@konig.es",
            "password": "PasswordSegura123!",
            "full_name": "Carlos Méndez",
            "tenant_id": tenant.id,
            "role": "AUDITOR",
        },
    )
    assert res.status_code == 201
    user_data = res.json()
    assert user_data["email"] == "carlos.auditor@konig.es"
    assert user_data["role"] == "AUDITOR"
    assert user_data["tenant_id"] == tenant.id

    # Intento de registro duplicado con el mismo correo debe ser 400 Bad Request
    res_dup = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/register",
        json={
            "email": "carlos.auditor@konig.es",
            "password": "PasswordSegura123!",
            "full_name": "Carlos Duplicado",
            "tenant_id": tenant.id,
            "role": "AUDITOR",
        },
    )
    assert res_dup.status_code == 400
    assert "Ya existe un usuario" in res_dup.json()["detail"]


def test_user_login_and_auth_me_endpoint(client_api, db_session):
    """Verifica el flujo de Login y consulta de perfil /me con cabecera Bearer."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Despacho Contable Sur SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-11223344",
        email="sur@despacho.es",
    )

    # Registrar usuario
    client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/register",
        json={
            "email": "laura.socia@despacho.es",
            "password": "AdminPassword2026!",
            "full_name": "Laura Gómez",
            "tenant_id": tenant.id,
            "role": "ADMIN",
        },
    )

    # Login con contraseña errónea debe devolver 401 Unauthorized
    res_bad = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/login",
        json={
            "email": "laura.socia@despacho.es",
            "password": "PasswordIncorrecta",
        },
    )
    assert res_bad.status_code == 401

    # Login exitoso
    res_ok = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/login",
        json={
            "email": "laura.socia@despacho.es",
            "password": "AdminPassword2026!",
        },
    )
    assert res_ok.status_code == 200
    token_data = res_ok.json()
    token = token_data["access_token"]
    assert token_data["token_type"] == "bearer"
    assert token_data["user"]["role"] == "ADMIN"

    # Consultar /api/v1/auth/me con el token emitido
    res_me = client_api.get(
        "/api/v1/auth/me",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
    )
    assert res_me.status_code == 200
    profile = res_me.json()
    assert profile["email"] == "laura.socia@despacho.es"
    assert profile["role"] == "ADMIN"
    assert profile["tenant_id"] == tenant.id


def test_rbac_role_enforcement_and_isolation(db_session):
    """Verifica que las funciones de RBAC y aislamiento multi-tenant restrinjan permisos correctamente."""
    t1 = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Despacho 1",
        fiscal_id="B-11111111",
        email="d1@despacho.es",
    )
    t2 = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Despacho 2",
        fiscal_id="B-22222222",
        email="d2@despacho.es",
    )

    user_admin = User(
        email="admin@d1.es",
        hashed_password="hash",
        full_name="Admin D1",
        tenant_id=t1.id,
        role=UserRole.ADMIN,
    )
    user_auditor = User(
        email="auditor@d1.es",
        hashed_password="hash",
        full_name="Auditor D1",
        tenant_id=t1.id,
        role=UserRole.AUDITOR,
    )

    # 1. Verificar require_roles
    admin_checker = require_roles([UserRole.ADMIN])
    assert admin_checker(user_admin) == user_admin

    # Un auditor intentando acceder a ruta de ADMIN recibe 403
    with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc_role:
        admin_checker(user_auditor)
    assert exc_role.value.status_code == 403
    assert "Permisos insuficientes" in exc_role.value.detail

    # 2. Verificar aislamiento multi-tenant
    assert_tenant_access(t1.id, user_admin)  # Pertenece a t1 -> OK

    # Intentar acceder a t2 siendo usuario de t1 recibe 403
    with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc_tenant:
        assert_tenant_access(t2.id, user_admin)
    assert exc_tenant.value.status_code == 403
    assert "Acceso denegado" in exc_tenant.value.detail


def test_self_registration_with_auto_tenant(client_api, db_session):
    """Verifica que un nuevo despacho pueda registrarse directamente sin un tenant previo."""
    resp = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/register",
        json={
            "email": "socio@nuevodespacho.es",
            "password": "SecurePassword2026!",
            "full_name": "Laura Gómez (Socia Directora)",
            "company_name": "Gómez & Asociados Asesores SLP",
            "fiscal_id": "B-88776655",
        },
    )
    assert resp.status_code == 201
    data = resp.json()
    assert data["email"] == "socio@nuevodespacho.es"
    assert data["role"] == UserRole.ADMIN
    assert data["tenant_id"] is not None
    assert data["tenant_name"] == "Gómez & Asociados Asesores SLP"


def test_forgot_and_reset_password_flow(client_api, db_session):
    """Verifica el flujo completo de recuperación y actualización de contraseña."""
    # 1. Registrar usuario inicial
    client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/register",
        json={
            "email": "recuperar@gestoria.es",
            "password": "OldPassword2026!",
            "full_name": "Usuario Recuperación",
            "company_name": "Gestoría Recuperación SL",
        },
    )

    # 2. Solicitar recuperación
    resp_forgot = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/forgot-password",
        json={"email": "recuperar@gestoria.es"},
    )
    assert resp_forgot.status_code == 200
    forgot_data = resp_forgot.json()
    assert forgot_data["success"] is True
    reset_token = forgot_data["reset_token_demo"]
    assert reset_token is not None

    # 3. Restablecer con contraseña nueva
    resp_reset = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/reset-password",
        json={
            "token": reset_token,
            "new_password": "BrandNewPassword2026!",
        },
    )
    assert resp_reset.status_code == 200
    assert resp_reset.json()["success"] is True

    # 4. Iniciar sesión con la clave nueva
    resp_login = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/auth/login",
        json={
            "email": "recuperar@gestoria.es",
            "password": "BrandNewPassword2026!",
        },
    )
    assert resp_login.status_code == 200
    assert "access_token" in resp_login.json()


================================================================================
FICHERO: tests\test_dirty_and_malformed_data.py
