"""
Test Suite de Estrés y Resiliencia con Datos Sucios, Truncados y Malformados
(Dirty Data Stress Test & Fuzzing Resilience Auditor)

Verifica que el sistema maneje limpiamente y sin excepciones no controladas (Unhandled Exceptions):
1. Norma 43 Truncada y Corrupta:
   - Archivo cortado a mitad de registro (EOF inesperado).
   - Líneas con menos de 80 y más de 120 caracteres.
   - Tipos de registro inexistentes o desconocidos ('99', 'AA').
   - Registros 23 huérfanos sin registro 22 previo.
   - Registros 33 con sumas descuadradas o incompletas.
   - Bytes binarios corruptos / codificación mixta ilegible.
2. Identificadores Fiscales y Textos Caóticos:
   - NIFs, CIFs y NIEs truncados, caracteres prohibidos o letras de control erróneas.
   - Conceptos bancarios vacíos, solo espacios, solo emojis o caracteres de control.
   - Cadenas con 500+ caracteres de ruido bancario y palabras desordenadas.
3. Pasarelas (Stripe y Redsys) con Formatos Malformados:
   - CSVs de Stripe sin cabeceras 'id' o 'Gross', valores numéricos corruptos ("N/A", "NaN", "null", "--", "Infinity").
   - CSVs de Redsys con importes con formatos numéricos anómalos o múltiples separadores decimales.
   - Importes negativos absurdos o fees mayores que el bruto.
4. Autodescubridor de Columnas en Condiciones Extremas:
   - Tablas CSV de clientes sin encabezados reconocibles, vacíos o con None.
   - Filas truncadas con valores nulos y formatos numéricos desbordados.
5. Tolerancia y Fallback en Motores de Conciliación y Doble Partida.
"""
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
import pytest

from src.parsers.bank.norma43 import Norma43Parser, Norma43ParseResult
from src.parsers.gateways.stripe import StripeParser
from src.parsers.gateways.redsys import RedsysParser
from src.parsers.mapper import ColumnMapper
from src.parsers.base import (
    CanonicalBankTransaction,
    CanonicalInvoice,
    CanonicalGatewayTransaction,
    TransactionType,
)
from src.disambiguation.fiscal_validators import SpanishFiscalValidator
from src.disambiguation.normalizer import SpanishBankTextNormalizer
from src.disambiguation.phonetic import SpanishPhoneticEncoder
from src.disambiguation.fuzzy_matcher import SpanishFuzzyMatcher
from src.disambiguation.confidence_scorer import BankDisambiguationScorer, DisambiguationStatus
from src.accounting.double_entry import AccountingEntryBuilder, DoubleEntryJournal
from src.reconciliation.engine import BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine


# ==============================================================================
# SECCIÓN 1: NORMA 43 TRUNCADA Y CORRUPTA
# ==============================================================================

def test_norma43_unexpected_eof_truncated_mid_record():
    """Verifica rechazo limpio cuando el fichero se corta abruptamente a mitad de registro (EOF inesperado)."""
    # 1. Cortado a mitad de registro 11 (25 caracteres)
    truncated_11 = "1100491234123456789026100"
    res1 = Norma43Parser.parse_text(truncated_11)
    assert not res1.is_valid
    assert len(res1.validation_errors) > 0
    assert any("Error procesando" in err or "truncado" in err for err in res1.validation_errors)

    # 2. Cortado a mitad de registro 22 (35 caracteres)
    line_11 = "110049123412345678902610012610152000000010000009783CLIENTE PRUEBA SL           "
    truncated_22 = f"{line_11}\n22004912342610102610100400020000000"
    res2 = Norma43Parser.parse_text(truncated_22)
    assert not res2.is_valid
    assert len(res2.validation_errors) > 0

    # 3. Registros 11 y 22 completos, pero EOF antes del registro 33 (cuenta abierta sin cierre)
    line_22 = "220049123426101026101004000200000000145025          STRIPE PAYOUT             "
    eof_no_33 = f"{line_11}\n{line_22}"
    res3 = Norma43Parser.parse_text(eof_no_33)
    assert not res3.is_valid
    assert any("truncado" in err.lower() or "falta registro de fin" in err.lower() for err in res3.validation_errors)
    assert len(res3.canonical_transactions) == 0  # No genera transacciones canónicas de un fichero truncado


def test_norma43_lines_with_less_than_80_and_more_than_120_chars():
    """Verifica tolerancia y manejo limpio de líneas con longitud anómala (< 80 y > 120 caracteres)."""
    # Registro 23 con solo 45 caracteres (menos de 80)
    line_11 = "110049123412345678902610012610152000000010000009783CLIENTE PRUEBA SL           "
    line_22 = "220049123426101026101004000200000000145025          STRIPE PAYOUT             "
    short_23 = "2301CONCEPTO CORTO CON MENOS DE 80 CARACTERES"
    # Registro 33 exacto: 0 debits (0 céntimos), 1 credit (145025 céntimos), final balance = 1145025
    valid_33 = "3300491234123456789000000000000000000000000100000000145025200000001145025978    "
    line_88 = "889999999999999999000004                                                        "
    file_short_line = "\n".join([line_11, line_22, short_23, valid_33, line_88])

    res_short = Norma43Parser.parse_text(file_short_line)
    # Debe procesar con relleno espacial (padding) sin crash y ser válido
    assert res_short.is_valid, f"Errores inesperados: {res_short.validation_errors}"
    assert len(res_short.accounts) == 1
    assert "CONCEPTO CORTO" in res_short.accounts[0].movements[0].full_concept

    # Línea 23 con 180 caracteres (desborde excesivo de texto bancario)
    long_23 = "2301" + "RUIDO BANCARIO EXTRA " * 15
    assert len(long_23) > 120
    file_long_line = "\n".join([line_11, line_22, long_23, valid_33, line_88])

    res_long = Norma43Parser.parse_text(file_long_line)
    assert res_long.is_valid
    assert len(res_long.canonical_transactions) == 1


def test_norma43_unknown_record_types_99_and_AA():
    """Verifica que registros desconocidos ('99', 'AA') se rechacen limpiamente y de forma estructurada."""
    line_11 = "110049123412345678902610012610152000000010000009783CLIENTE PRUEBA SL           "
    unknown_99 = "9900491234REGISTRO INVENTADO QUE NO EXISTE EN LA NORMA 43 AEB                 "
    unknown_AA = "AA00491234REGISTRO ALFANUMERICO ANOMALO NO CONTEMPLADO                         "
    valid_33 = "3300491234123456789000000000000000000000000000000000000000200000001000000978    "
    line_88 = "889999999999999999000004                                                        "
    raw_content = "\n".join([line_11, unknown_99, unknown_AA, valid_33, line_88])

    res = Norma43Parser.parse_text(raw_content)
    assert not res.is_valid
    assert any("desconocido o inválido '99'" in err for err in res.validation_errors)
    assert any("desconocido o inválido 'AA'" in err for err in res.validation_errors)


def test_norma43_orphan_record_23_without_record_22():
    """Verifica detección de registros complementarios 23 huérfanos sin registro 22 previo."""
    line_11 = "110049123412345678902610012610152000000010000009783CLIENTE PRUEBA SL           "
    orphan_23 = "2301CONCEPTO HUERFANO SIN APUNTE PRINCIPAL PREVIO 22                            "
    valid_33 = "3300491234123456789000000000000000000000000000000000000000200000001000000978    "
    raw_content = "\n".join([line_11, orphan_23, valid_33])

    res = Norma43Parser.parse_text(raw_content)
    assert not res.is_valid
    assert any("sin apunte principal (22)" in err for err in res.validation_errors)

    # 23 que aparece antes de cualquier registro 11
    res_pre_11 = Norma43Parser.parse_text(orphan_23)
    assert not res_pre_11.is_valid
    assert any("sin apunte principal (22)" in err for err in res_pre_11.validation_errors)


def test_norma43_record_33_mismatched_and_incomplete_sums():
    """Verifica que sumas descuadradas o recuentos erróneos en el registro 33 se identifiquen matemáticamente."""
    line_11 = "110049123412345678902610012610152000000010000009783CLIENTE PRUEBA SL           "
    line_22 = "220049123426101026101004000200000000145025          STRIPE PAYOUT             "
    # Saldo esperado: 1000000 + 145025 = 1145025. Ponemos saldo final erróneo 1145000 (25 céntimos de descuadre)
    # y recuento de abonos erróneo (dice 5 pero hay 1 apunte)
    corrupt_33 = "3300491234123456789000000000000000000000000500000000145025200000001145000978    "
    line_88 = "889999999999999999000003                                                        "
    file_corrupt_33 = "\n".join([line_11, line_22, corrupt_33, line_88])

    res = Norma43Parser.parse_text(file_corrupt_33)
    assert not res.is_valid
    assert len(res.validation_errors) >= 2
    assert any("número de abonos" in err for err in res.validation_errors)
    assert any("Descuadre matemático" in err for err in res.validation_errors)
    assert not res.accounts[0].is_balanced
    assert res.accounts[0].balance_discrepancy_cents == -25


def test_norma43_corrupt_binary_bytes_and_mixed_encodings():
    """Verifica que secuencias binarias aleatorias o codificaciones corruptas no provoquen crashes."""
    # 1. Bytes binarios no legibles (nulos, cabeceras ELF/PE, bytes corruptos)
    corrupt_bin = b"\x7fELF\x02\x01\x01\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x03\x00\x3e\x00\xff\xfe\xaa\xbb"
    res_bin = Norma43Parser.parse_file(corrupt_bin)
    assert not res_bin.is_valid
    assert len(res_bin.canonical_transactions) == 0
    assert len(res_bin.validation_errors) > 0

    # 2. Fichero vacío
    res_empty = Norma43Parser.parse_file(b"")
    assert not res_empty.is_valid
    assert any("sin registros" in err.lower() for err in res_empty.validation_errors)

    # 3. Codificación mixta: texto en español con bytes no UTF-8 intercalados
    text_prefix = "110049123412345678902610012610152000000010000009783CLIENTE PRUEBA SL           \n"
    mixed_bytes = text_prefix.encode("utf-8") + b"\xff\xfe\x80\x90\x00\x01\x02\x03"
    res_mixed = Norma43Parser.parse_file(mixed_bytes)
    assert not res_mixed.is_valid or len(res_mixed.accounts) >= 0  # No debe lanzar Unhandled Exception


# ==============================================================================
# SECCIÓN 2: IDENTIFICADORES FISCALES Y TEXTOS CAÓTICOS
# ==============================================================================

def test_fiscal_identifiers_truncated_prohibited_chars_and_wrong_checksum():
    """Verifica validación estricta de NIFs, NIEs y CIFs ante valores nulos, truncados y corruptos."""
    validator = SpanishFiscalValidator

    # Casos NIF
    assert not validator.validate_nif("")
    assert not validator.validate_nif(None)
    assert not validator.validate_nif("1234567")          # Truncado (7 dígitos)
    assert not validator.validate_nif("1234567890")       # Excedido (10 caracteres)
    assert not validator.validate_nif("12345678@")        # Carácter prohibido
    assert not validator.validate_nif("123456789")        # Sin letra
    assert not validator.validate_nif("12345678A")        # Letra errónea (para 12345678 es Z)
    assert validator.validate_nif("12345678Z")            # Válido

    # Casos NIE
    assert not validator.validate_nie("")
    assert not validator.validate_nie(None)
    assert not validator.validate_nie("A1234567Z")        # Letra inicial prohibida (solo X, Y, Z)
    assert not validator.validate_nie("X123456")          # Truncado
    assert not validator.validate_nie("X1234567@")        # Carácter no alfabético
    assert not validator.validate_nie("X1234567A")        # Letra de control errónea (X1234567 es L)
    assert validator.validate_nie("X1234567L")            # Válido

    # Casos CIF
    assert not validator.validate_cif("")
    assert not validator.validate_cif(None)
    assert not validator.validate_cif("Z1234567A")        # Letra inicial no contemplada
    assert not validator.validate_cif("B123456")          # Truncado
    assert not validator.validate_cif("B1234567@")        # Carácter especial
    assert not validator.validate_cif("B12345678")        # Control erróneo (para B1234567 el dígito es 4)
    assert validator.validate_cif("B12345674")            # Válido

    # Entradas no cadena (tipos anómalos)
    assert not validator.validate_nif(12345678)
    assert not validator.validate_nie([1, 2, 3])
    assert not validator.validate_cif({"cif": "B12345674"})


def test_fiscal_extraction_from_chaotic_text():
    """Verifica extracción resiliente de NIF/CIF o número de factura desde textos caóticos."""
    validator = SpanishFiscalValidator

    # Textos vacíos, solo espacios o tipos erróneos
    assert validator.extract_fiscal_id(None) is None
    assert validator.extract_fiscal_id("") is None
    assert validator.extract_fiscal_id("     ") is None
    assert validator.extract_invoice_number(None) is None
    assert validator.extract_invoice_number("") is None

    # Solo emojis o caracteres de control
    assert validator.extract_fiscal_id("🔥🔥🔥 🚀🚀🚀 💩💩💩 \x00\x01\x1f") is None
    assert validator.extract_invoice_number("🔥🔥🔥 🚀🚀🚀") is None

    # Cadena con 600 caracteres de ruido bancario y un NIF válido incrustado
    noise = "TRANSFERENCIA SEPA ORDENANTE PEPE PAGO REF 99999 " * 12
    dirty_text = f"{noise} NIF 12345678Z FACTURA INV-2026-9901 {noise}"
    extracted = validator.extract_fiscal_id(dirty_text)
    assert extracted is not None
    assert extracted == ("NIF", "12345678Z")

    extracted_inv = validator.extract_invoice_number(dirty_text)
    assert extracted_inv is not None
    assert "2026-9901" in extracted_inv or "INV" in extracted_inv


def test_bank_text_normalizer_empty_spaces_emojis_and_control_chars():
    """Verifica que el normalizador bancario tolere emojis, caracteres de control y cadenas vacías."""
    normalizer = SpanishBankTextNormalizer

    # Nulo y tipos inválidos
    assert normalizer.clean(None) == ""
    assert normalizer.clean(12345) == ""
    assert normalizer.clean("") == ""
    assert normalizer.clean("   \t\r\n   ") == ""

    # Solo caracteres de control
    assert normalizer.clean("\x00\x01\x02\x07\x0b\x1f") == ""

    # Mezcla de ruido bancario, control y emojis
    raw = "\x00 TRANSF. SEPA DE: JUAN CARLOS GARCIA-LOPEZ / REF: 9988 // CONCEPTO: PAGO FRA. 🔥"
    cleaned = normalizer.clean(raw)
    assert "JUAN CARLOS GARCIA LOPEZ" in cleaned
    assert "9988" in cleaned
    assert "TRANSF" not in cleaned
    assert "CONCEPTO" not in cleaned


def test_chaotic_bank_text_500_plus_chars_disambiguation_pipeline():
    """Somete a prueba el pipeline completo de desambiguación ante un concepto de 600+ caracteres de ruido."""
    long_chaotic_concept = (
        "TRANSF. EMITIDA ORDENANTE INDITEX ESPAÑA S.A. REF SEPA 1234567890 "
        "DETALLE OPERACION BANCARIA LIQUIDACION COMPLEJA DE SERVICIOS INFORMATICOS "
        "Y LOGISTICOS EN TERRITORIO NACIONAL CORRESPONDIENTE AL TERCER TRIMESTRE 2026 "
        "CON DEDUCCION DE RETENCIONES FISCALES Y TASAS DE GESTION ADMINISTRATIVA. "
        "CLIENTE DE REFERENCIA INTERNA CIF: B12345674 FACTURA FRA-2026-0044 "
    ) * 2
    assert len(long_chaotic_concept) > 600

    bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-CHAOTIC-001",
        account_iban="ES0000491234001234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=150000,
        concept=long_chaotic_concept,
        payer_or_payee="INDITEX ESPAÑA S.A.",
    )

    invoice = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-0044",
        invoice_number="FRA-2026-0044",
        issue_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        due_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        customer_id="CUST-INDITEX",
        customer_name="INDITEX ESPAÑA S.A.",
        gross_amount_cents=150000,
        customer_fiscal_id="B12345674",
    )

    # Debe procesar sin fallar en menos de 10ms y validar por CIF / número de factura
    result = BankDisambiguationScorer.evaluate(bank_tx, invoice)
    assert result.status == DisambiguationStatus.GREEN
    assert result.confidence_score >= 0.95
    assert result.fiscal_match is True
    assert result.invoice_number_match is True


# ==============================================================================
# SECCIÓN 3: PASARELAS (STRIPE Y REDSYS) CON FORMATOS MALFORMADOS
# ==============================================================================

def test_stripe_csv_missing_headers_and_empty_files():
    """Verifica que ficheros CSV de Stripe sin cabeceras mínimas se rechacen sin excepción."""
    # 1. Sin cabecera 'id'
    csv_no_id = """Gross,Fee,Net,Currency,Created (UTC)
100.00,2.15,97.85,eur,2026-10-10 10:15:00
50.00,1.20,48.80,eur,2026-10-10 11:20:00
"""
    txs_no_id = StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(csv_no_id)
    assert len(txs_no_id) == 0  # Filas sin ID se descartan limpiamente

    # 2. Sin cabecera 'Gross' (se asume 0 céntimos por defecto)
    csv_no_gross = """id,Type,Fee,Net,Currency
txn_01,charge,2.15,0.00,eur
"""
    txs_no_gross = StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(csv_no_gross)
    assert len(txs_no_gross) == 1
    assert txs_no_gross[0].gross_cents == 0

    # 3. Fichero completamente vacío
    assert len(StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv("")) == 0
    assert len(StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(b"")) == 0


def test_stripe_csv_corrupt_numeric_values():
    """Verifica conversión segura de valores numéricos corruptos en Stripe ("N/A", "NaN", "null", "--", "Infinity")."""
    corrupt_csv = """id,Type,Gross,Fee,Net,Currency
txn_nan,charge,NaN,null,--,eur
txn_inf,charge,Infinity,-Infinity,N/A,eur
txn_mixed,charge,100.50 €,None,98.00 $,eur
txn_spaces,charge,  50.00  ,  1.50  ,  48.50  ,eur
"""
    txs = StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(corrupt_csv)
    assert len(txs) == 4

    # txn_nan: todos 0 céntimos sin OverflowError ni ValueError
    assert txs[0].gross_cents == 0
    assert txs[0].fee_cents == 0
    assert txs[0].net_cents == 0

    # txn_inf: infinito amortiguado a 0
    assert txs[1].gross_cents == 0
    assert txs[1].fee_cents == 0
    assert txs[1].net_cents == 0

    # txn_mixed: 100.50 € -> 10050 céntimos
    assert txs[2].gross_cents == 10050
    assert txs[2].fee_cents == 0
    assert txs[2].net_cents == 9800

    # txn_spaces: espacios recortados
    assert txs[3].gross_cents == 5000
    assert txs[3].fee_cents == 150
    assert txs[3].net_cents == 4850


def test_redsys_csv_anomalous_formats_and_multiple_decimal_separators():
    """Verifica soporte para formatos españoles, múltiples separadores decimales y columnas truncadas en Redsys."""
    sample_csv = """Terminal;Fecha;Número Pedido;Tipo Operación;Importe Bruto;Comisión;Importe Líquido;Autorización
TPV01;12/10/2026;PED-101;0000;1.234,56 €;15,50 €;1.219,06 €;AUTH01
TPV01;12/10/2026;PED-102;0000;12,34,56;1,00;11,00;AUTH02
TPV01;12/10/2026;PED-103;0000;Infinity;NaN;null;AUTH03
TPV01;12/10/2026;PED-104;0000;--
"""
    txs = RedsysParser.parse_operations_csv(sample_csv)
    assert len(txs) == 4

    # PED-101: 1.234,56 € -> 123456 céntimos
    assert txs[0].gross_cents == 123456
    assert txs[0].fee_cents == 1550
    assert txs[0].net_cents == 121906

    # PED-102: Múltiples comas anómalas (12,34,56)
    assert isinstance(txs[1].gross_cents, int)

    # PED-103: Infinity / NaN amortiguados a 0
    assert txs[2].gross_cents == 0
    assert txs[2].fee_cents == 0

    # PED-104: Fila truncada (menos valores que cabeceras)
    assert txs[3].gross_cents == 0


def test_gateway_absurdly_negative_amounts_and_excessive_fees():
    """Verifica que transacciones con fees superiores al bruto o importes absurdos se procesen en asientos balanceados."""
    # Transacción donde la pasarela cobró más comisión que el bruto (saldo neto negativo)
    # Bruto = 10.00 € (1000 céntimos), Fee = 15.00 € (1500 céntimos), Neto = -5.00 € (-500 céntimos)
    matched_invoices = [
        {
            "account_code": "430000001",
            "invoice_number": "INV-SMALL-01",
            "gross_cents": 1000,
            "fee_cents": 1500,
            "customer_name": "Cliente Micro",
        }
    ]

    # Construir asiento
    journal = AccountingEntryBuilder.build_payout_reconciliation_entry(
        entry_number=1,
        entry_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
        payout_net_cents=-500,
        matched_invoices=matched_invoices,
        gateway_name="Stripe",
    )

    # DEBE = -500 (Banco) + 1500 (Comisiones) = 1000 céntimos
    # HABER = 1000 (Cliente)
    # Cuadre estricto: Debe == Haber
    assert journal.total_debit_cents == 1000
    assert journal.total_credit_cents == 1000
    assert journal.check_balance() is True
    assert journal.imbalance_cents == 0


# ==============================================================================
# SECCIÓN 4: AUTODESCUBRIDOR DE COLUMNAS EN CONDICIONES EXTREMAS
# ==============================================================================

def test_column_mapper_unrecognized_or_empty_headers():
    """Verifica que ColumnMapper gestione cabeceras no identificables, cabeceras None y filas vacías."""
    mapper = ColumnMapper

    # 1. Cabeceras None o vacías en detect_mapping
    mapping_none = mapper.detect_mapping(["Col1", None, "", "   "])
    assert isinstance(mapping_none, dict)
    assert len(mapping_none) == 0

    # 2. CSV sin ninguna cabecera reconocible
    unrecognized_csv = """col_alpha,col_beta,col_gamma,col_delta
val1,val2,val3,val4
"""
    invoices = mapper.parse_invoices_csv(unrecognized_csv)
    assert len(invoices) == 0  # No reconoce factura ni cliente -> no genera facturas espurias

    # 3. CSV con valores None en filas (filas con menos campos que columnas)
    truncated_row_csv = """número,cliente,total
FRA-001,ACME S.L.
FRA-002
"""
    invoices_trunc = mapper.parse_invoices_csv(truncated_row_csv)
    assert len(invoices_trunc) == 2
    assert invoices_trunc[0].invoice_number == "FRA-001"
    assert invoices_trunc[0].customer_name == "ACME S.L."
    assert invoices_trunc[0].gross_amount_cents == 0  # Campo total ausente asumido 0 sin crash
    assert invoices_trunc[1].invoice_number == "FRA-002"

    # 4. CSV con importes absurdos / desbordados
    overflow_csv = """factura,cliente,importe
FRA-999,CLIENTE VIP,Infinity
FRA-888,CLIENTE 2,NaN
FRA-777,CLIENTE 3,123.456,78 €
"""
    invoices_overflow = mapper.parse_invoices_csv(overflow_csv)
    assert len(invoices_overflow) == 3
    assert invoices_overflow[0].gross_amount_cents == 0
    assert invoices_overflow[1].gross_amount_cents == 0
    assert isinstance(invoices_overflow[2].gross_amount_cents, int)


# ==============================================================================
# SECCIÓN 5: RESILIENCIA EN MOTOR DE CONCILIACIÓN
# ==============================================================================

def test_reconciliation_engine_with_dirty_and_extreme_inputs():
    """Verifica que el orquestador algorítmico rechace o maneje con gracia datos numéricos y fechas extremas."""
    engine = BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine()

    # Apunte bancario con importe cero
    bank_tx_zero = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-ZERO",
        account_iban="ES0000491234001234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=0,
    )

    report_zero = engine.reconcile_payout(bank_tx_zero, open_invoices=[])
    assert report_zero.is_reconciled is False
    assert len(report_zero.matched_invoice_ids) == 0

    # Facturas con importes desorbitados o fechas en el futuro
    extreme_invoice = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-EXTREME",
        invoice_number="FRA-EXTREME",
        issue_date=date(2026, 11, 1),  # Fecha posterior al payout (debe ser podada)
        due_date=date(2026, 11, 15),
        customer_id="CUST-EXTREME",
        customer_name="EXTREMO SL",
        gross_amount_cents=9999999999,  # 100 millones de euros
    )

    report_extreme = engine.reconcile_payout(bank_tx_zero, open_invoices=[extreme_invoice])
    assert report_extreme.is_reconciled is False


================================================================================
FICHERO: tests\test_end_to_end_user_journey.py
================================================================================

"""
Test End-to-End del Recorrido de Usuario Completo (User Journey & Security Boundary)
Valida:
1. Auto-registro de nuevo despacho profesional -> Rol ADMIN de su propio inquilino.
2. Login inmediato del nuevo despacho emitiendo JWT con rol ADMIN.
3. Aislamiento de permisos: El nuevo ADMIN recibe 403 Forbidden al intentar acceder al panel global (/api/v1/admin/companies y /stats).
4. Acceso SUPERADMIN (admin@findaffinity.com): Puede auditar todas las compañías, viendo la nueva empresa y su plan.
5. Estado limpio y libre de documentos fantasma: La nueva empresa cliente tiene 0 lotes y 0 transacciones.
6. Ingesta y conciliación aislada: La nueva empresa sube un extracto bancario/pasarela y genera asientos sin contaminar a otros inquilinos.
"""
import uuid
import pytest
from io import BytesIO
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from src.main import app
from src.database.connection import SessionLocal
from src.database.models import User, UserRole, Tenant, ClientCompany
from src.services.bootstrap_service import ensure_default_accounts, bootstrap_database_if_empty


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def api_client():
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        with SessionLocal() as db:
            bootstrap_database_if_empty(db)
            ensure_default_accounts(db)
        yield client


def test_complete_user_journey_and_security_isolation(api_client):
    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]
    test_email = f"laura_{suffix}@gomez-auditores.es"
    test_fiscal = f"B{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}"
    test_company = f"Gómez & Asociados Auditores {suffix.upper()} SLP"

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 1: Registro de un nuevo despacho profesional
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    new_user_payload = {
        "email": test_email,
        "password": "PasswordGomez2026!",
        "full_name": f"Laura Gómez {suffix}",
        "company_name": test_company,
        "fiscal_id": test_fiscal,
    }
    reg_res = api_client.post("/api/v1/auth/register", json=new_user_payload)
    assert reg_res.status_code == 201, f"Fallo al registrar usuario: {reg_res.text}"
    reg_data = reg_res.json()
    assert reg_data["email"] == test_email
    assert reg_data["role"] == UserRole.ADMIN
    assert reg_data["tenant_id"] is not None

    gomez_tenant_id = reg_data["tenant_id"]

    # Login inmediato del usuario registrado
    login_res = api_client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={
        "email": test_email,
        "password": "PasswordGomez2026!",
    })
    assert login_res.status_code == 200, f"Fallo al iniciar sesión: {login_res.text}"
    login_data = login_res.json()
    assert "access_token" in login_data
    assert login_data["user"]["role"] == UserRole.ADMIN

    gomez_token = login_data["access_token"]
    gomez_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {gomez_token}"}

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 2: Intento del nuevo ADMIN de acceder al panel global SUPERADMIN
    # Debe recibir estrictamente 403 Forbidden
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    admin_probe = api_client.get("/api/v1/admin/companies", headers=gomez_headers)
    assert admin_probe.status_code == 403, (
        f"Un Tenant ADMIN no debe tener acceso a /api/v1/admin/companies: {admin_probe.status_code}"
    )

    stats_probe = api_client.get("/api/v1/admin/stats", headers=gomez_headers)
    assert stats_probe.status_code == 403, (
        f"Un Tenant ADMIN no debe tener acceso a /api/v1/admin/stats: {stats_probe.status_code}"
    )

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 3: Login del SUPERADMIN oficial de FINDAFINITY
    # Accede al panel global y ve la nueva empresa registrada
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    sa_login = api_client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={
        "email": "admin@findaffinity.com",
        "password": "AdminPass2026!",
    })
    assert sa_login.status_code == 200, sa_login.text
    sa_token = sa_login.json()["access_token"]
    sa_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {sa_token}"}

    sa_companies = api_client.get("/api/v1/admin/companies", headers=sa_headers)
    assert sa_companies.status_code == 200, sa_companies.text
    company_list = sa_companies.json()
    assert isinstance(company_list, list)

    gomez_in_list = next((c for c in company_list if c["id"] == gomez_tenant_id), None)
    assert gomez_in_list is not None, "El despacho recién registrado debe figurar en el panel de SUPERADMIN"
    assert gomez_in_list["name"] == test_company
    assert gomez_in_list["fiscal_id"] == test_fiscal
    assert gomez_in_list["plan"] == "STARTER"

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 4: Consulta de clientes del nuevo despacho
    # Verificación de CERO empresas cliente fantasma tras registrarse
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    existing_clients_res = api_client.get(
        f"/api/v1/clients?tenant_id={gomez_tenant_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert existing_clients_res.status_code == 200, existing_clients_res.text
    existing_clients = existing_clients_res.json()
    assert len(existing_clients) == 0, "Un despacho recién registrado debe iniciar con exactamente 0 empresas cliente fantasma"

    # El despacho crea explícitamente su primera Empresa Cliente vía modal/API
    client_payload = {
        "tenant_id": gomez_tenant_id,
        "name": f"Restaurante El Faro {suffix.upper()} SL",
        "fiscal_id": f"B{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",
        "default_erp": "A3",
        "account_length": 9,
        "bank_account_code": "572000001",
        "fee_account_code": "626000001",
    }
    create_client_res = api_client.post(
        "/api/v1/clients",
        json=client_payload,
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert create_client_res.status_code == 201, create_client_res.text
    created_client = create_client_res.json()
    client_id = created_client["id"]
    assert created_client["name"] == client_payload["name"]

    # Consulta de lotes existentes para este nuevo cliente (debe ser lista vacía [])
    batches_res = api_client.get(
        f"/api/v1/batches?client_id={client_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert batches_res.status_code == 200, batches_res.text
    batches = batches_res.json()
    assert batches == [], "Un cliente recién creado debe tener exactamente 0 lotes (cero documentos fantasma)"

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 5: Subida de fichero real por el nuevo despacho y conciliación aislada
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    stripe_csv_content = (
        "id,Type,Gross,Fee,Net,Created (UTC),Description\n"
        f"ch_{suffix}_001,charge,120.00,3.50,116.50,2026-05-10 12:00:00,Cobro Degustacion La Bahia\n"
        f"ch_{suffix}_002,charge,80.00,2.50,77.50,2026-05-10 14:30:00,Cobro Terraza La Bahia\n"
    ).encode("utf-8")

    upload_res = api_client.post(
        "/api/v1/batches",
        headers=gomez_headers,
        data={
            "tenant_id": gomez_tenant_id,
            "client_id": client_id,
            "period_year": 2026,
            "period_type": "MONTH",
            "start_month": 5,
            "end_month": 5,
        },
        files={
            "file": (f"stripe_{suffix}.csv", BytesIO(stripe_csv_content), "text/csv")
        }
    )
    assert upload_res.status_code == 201, upload_res.text
    batch_data = upload_res.json()
    batch_id = batch_data["id"]
    assert batch_data["total_movements"] >= 1
    assert batch_data["tenant_id"] == gomez_tenant_id
    assert batch_data["client_id"] == client_id

    # Comprobar que los lotes ahora contienen este lote
    batches_after = api_client.get(
        f"/api/v1/batches?client_id={client_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    ).json()
    assert len(batches_after) == 1
    assert batches_after[0]["id"] == batch_id

    # Comprobar detalle del lote
    batch_detail = api_client.get(
        f"/api/v1/batches/{batch_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert batch_detail.status_code == 200
    txs = batch_detail.json()["transactions"]
    assert len(txs) >= 1
    for tx in txs:
        assert tx["status"] in ["GREEN", "YELLOW", "RED"]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 6: Eliminación granular de partidas y vaciado de lote
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    target_tx = txs[0]
    initial_movements = len(txs)

    # Eliminar una partida individual
    del_tx_res = api_client.delete(
        f"/api/v1/batches/{batch_id}/transactions/{target_tx['id']}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert del_tx_res.status_code == 200, del_tx_res.text
    assert del_tx_res.json()["success"] is True

    # Comprobar que la partida ya no está en el lote
    batch_detail_after = api_client.get(
        f"/api/v1/batches/{batch_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    ).json()
    remaining_txs = batch_detail_after["transactions"]
    assert len(remaining_txs) == initial_movements - 1
    assert not any(t["id"] == target_tx["id"] for t in remaining_txs)

    # Vaciar/eliminar el lote completo
    del_batch_res = api_client.delete(
        f"/api/v1/batches/{batch_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert del_batch_res.status_code == 200, del_batch_res.text
    assert del_batch_res.json()["success"] is True

    # Verificar que el cliente vuelve a tener 0 lotes
    batches_empty = api_client.get(
        f"/api/v1/batches?client_id={client_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    ).json()
    assert len(batches_empty) == 0

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 7: Eliminación de empresa cliente
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    del_client_res = api_client.delete(
        f"/api/v1/clients/{client_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert del_client_res.status_code == 200, del_client_res.text
    assert del_client_res.json()["success"] is True

    clients_after_del = api_client.get(
        f"/api/v1/clients?tenant_id={gomez_tenant_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    ).json()
    assert len(clients_after_del) == 0

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # PASO 8: Eliminación de compañía en panel ADMIN con seguridad
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # Un ADMIN corriente no puede borrar compañías (403)
    unauthorized_del = api_client.delete(
        f"/api/v1/admin/companies/{gomez_tenant_id}",
        headers=gomez_headers,
    )
    assert unauthorized_del.status_code == 403

    # SUPERADMIN no puede borrar la cuenta maestra (400)
    master_comp = next((c for c in company_list if c["email"] == "admin@findaffinity.com"), None)
    if master_comp:
        master_del = api_client.delete(
            f"/api/v1/admin/companies/{master_comp['id']}",
            headers=sa_headers,
        )
        assert master_del.status_code == 400

    # SUPERADMIN borra definitivamente el tenant Gómez
    sa_del_res = api_client.delete(
        f"/api/v1/admin/companies/{gomez_tenant_id}",
        headers=sa_headers,
    )
    assert sa_del_res.status_code == 200, sa_del_res.text
    assert sa_del_res.json()["success"] is True

    # Verificar que ya no figura en la lista global de SUPERADMIN
    sa_companies_final = api_client.get("/api/v1/admin/companies", headers=sa_headers).json()
    assert not any(c["id"] == gomez_tenant_id for c in sa_companies_final)


def test_deletion_routes_and_slash_variants_never_return_405(api_client):
    """
    Verifica que ninguna variante de ruta DELETE (con o sin barra final, o con query param)
    genere 405 Method Not Allowed.
    """
    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]
    # Crear tenant temporal
    reg_res = api_client.post("/api/v1/auth/register", json={
        "email": f"delete_test_{suffix}@test.es",
        "password": "PasswordTest2026!",
        "full_name": "Test Delete",
        "company_name": f"Test Delete {suffix} SL",
        "fiscal_id": f"B{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",
    })
    tenant_id = reg_res.json()["tenant_id"]

    # Crear cliente 1 para probar DELETE sin slash
    c1_res = api_client.post("/api/v1/clients", json={
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "name": "Cliente Slash 1",
        "fiscal_id": f"B{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",
    })
    c1_id = c1_res.json()["id"]

    # Crear cliente 2 para probar DELETE con slash
    c2_res = api_client.post("/api/v1/clients", json={
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "name": "Cliente Slash 2",
        "fiscal_id": f"B{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",
    })
    c2_id = c2_res.json()["id"]

    # Crear cliente 3 para probar DELETE con query param
    c3_res = api_client.post("/api/v1/clients", json={
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "name": "Cliente Query 3",
        "fiscal_id": f"B{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",
    })
    c3_id = c3_res.json()["id"]

    # 1. DELETE sin barra
    del1 = api_client.delete(f"/api/v1/clients/{c1_id}")
    assert del1.status_code == 200, f"DELETE sin barra falló: {del1.status_code}"
    assert del1.status_code != 405

    # 2. DELETE con barra
    del2 = api_client.delete(f"/api/v1/clients/{c2_id}/")
    assert del2.status_code == 200, f"DELETE con barra falló: {del2.status_code}"
    assert del2.status_code != 405

    # 3. DELETE con query parameter
    del3 = api_client.delete(f"/api/v1/clients?client_id={c3_id}")
    assert del3.status_code == 200, f"DELETE con query param falló: {del3.status_code}"
    assert del3.status_code != 405



================================================================================
FICHERO: tests\test_phase1_ingestion.py
================================================================================

"""
Test Suite para la Fase 1: Ingesta Multiformato y Parsers Resilientes
Verifica:
1. Resiliencia de codificación en Norma 43 (ISO-8859-15, Windows-1252, UTF-8) con caracteres especiales ('Ñ', 'ó', '€').
2. Integridad matemática del Registro 33 frente a los registros 11 y 22.
3. Ingesta de Stripe Balance Transactions y agrupación de liquidaciones.
4. Ingesta de extractos de TPV Redsys.
5. Autodescubrimiento y mapeo de columnas para facturación heterogénea de pymes.
"""
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
import pytest

from src.parsers.bank.norma43 import Norma43Parser, Norma43ParseResult
from src.parsers.gateways.stripe import StripeParser
from src.parsers.gateways.redsys import RedsysParser
from src.parsers.mapper import ColumnMapper
from src.parsers.base import TransactionType


def build_sample_norma43_text(include_special_chars: bool = True) -> str:
    """
    Construye un extracto sintético con formato exacto de 80 columnas de la AEB.
    - Registro 11: Saldo inicial = 10.000,00 € (1000000 céntimos, acreedor '2')
    - Registro 22 (1): Abono Stripe liquidación = 1.450,25 € (145025 céntimos, clave '2')
    - Registro 23 (1): Detalle con 'LIQUIDACIÓN STRIPE ESPAÑA S.L. - MAÑANA'
    - Registro 22 (2): Cargo comisión cuenta = 15,00 € (1500 céntimos, clave '1')
    - Registro 23 (2): Detalle 'COMISIÓN MANTENIMIENTO CUENTA'
    - Registro 33: Total Debe = 15,00 € (1500 céntimos), Total Haber = 1.450,25 € (145025 céntimos)
                   Saldo final = 1000000 + 145025 - 1500 = 1143525 céntimos (11.435,25 €)
    - Registro 88: Fin de fichero
    """
    # 80 caracteres exactos por línea
    line_11 = "110049123412345678902610012610152000000010000009783CLIENTE PRUEBA SL           "
    line_22_1 = "220049123426101026101004000200000000145025          STRIPE PAYOUT             "
    special_txt = "LIQUIDACIÓN STRIPE ESPAÑA S.L. - COMPAÑÍA" if include_special_chars else "LIQUIDACION STRIPE ESPANA S.L. - COMPANIA"
    line_23_1 = f"2301{special_txt:<76}"
    line_22_2 = "220049123426101226101217000100000000001500          COMISION BANCO            "
    line_23_2 = f"2301{'COMISIÓN MANTENIMIENTO TRIMESTRAL':<76}"
    # Registro 33: debits count = 1, debits total = 1500 (14d), credits count = 1, credits total = 145025 (14d), final bal = 1143525 (14d)
    line_33 = "3300491234123456789000001000000000015000000100000000145025200000001143525978    "
    line_88 = "889999999999999999000006                                                        "

    return "\n".join([line_11, line_22_1, line_23_1, line_22_2, line_23_2, line_33, line_88])


def test_norma43_iso8859_15_encoding():
    """Prueba que el parser decodifica sin errores bytes codificados en ISO-8859-15 con Ñ y tildes."""
    text_content = build_sample_norma43_text(include_special_chars=True)
    raw_bytes = text_content.encode("iso-8859-15")

    result = Norma43Parser.parse_file(raw_bytes)

    assert result.is_valid, f"Errores: {result.validation_errors}"
    assert result.detected_encoding in ("iso-8859-15", "windows-1252", "iso-8859-1")
    assert len(result.canonical_transactions) == 2

    # Verificar que los caracteres especiales se mantuvieron intactos
    stripe_tx = result.canonical_transactions[0]
    assert "ESPAÑA" in stripe_tx.concept or "ESPA" in stripe_tx.concept
    assert stripe_tx.transaction_type == TransactionType.DEPOSIT
    assert stripe_tx.amount_cents == 145025
    assert stripe_tx.amount_eur == Decimal("1450.25")

    fee_tx = result.canonical_transactions[1]
    assert fee_tx.transaction_type == TransactionType.WITHDRAWAL
    assert fee_tx.amount_cents == 1500
    assert fee_tx.amount_eur == Decimal("15.00")


def test_norma43_windows1252_encoding():
    """Prueba decodificación nativa Windows-1252."""
    text_content = build_sample_norma43_text(include_special_chars=True)
    raw_bytes = text_content.encode("windows-1252")

    result = Norma43Parser.parse_file(raw_bytes)
    assert result.is_valid
    assert len(result.accounts) == 1
    assert result.accounts[0].is_balanced is True
    assert result.accounts[0].balance_discrepancy_cents == 0


def test_norma43_utf8_encoding():
    """Prueba decodificación nativa UTF-8."""
    text_content = build_sample_norma43_text(include_special_chars=True)
    raw_bytes = text_content.encode("utf-8")

    result = Norma43Parser.parse_file(raw_bytes)
    assert result.is_valid
    assert len(result.canonical_transactions) == 2


def test_norma43_mathematical_imbalance_detection():
    """Prueba que el parser detecta y rechaza ficheros donde el saldo final no cuadra con el saldo inicial y movimientos."""
    valid_text = build_sample_norma43_text(include_special_chars=False)
    # Alteramos el saldo final en el registro 33 haciéndolo descuadrar por 10 céntimos
    lines = valid_text.splitlines()
    # Cambiar saldo final de 00000001143525 a 00000001143535
    corrupt_line_33 = lines[5][:59] + "00000001143535" + lines[5][73:]
    lines[5] = corrupt_line_33
    corrupt_text = "\n".join(lines)

    result = Norma43Parser.parse_text(corrupt_text)
    assert result.is_valid is False
    assert len(result.validation_errors) > 0
    assert any("Descuadre" in err or "no coincide" in err for err in result.validation_errors)


def test_stripe_parser_balance_transactions():
    """Verifica el parsing de transacciones de Stripe y agrupación en Payouts."""
    sample_csv = """id,Type,Gross,Fee,Net,Currency,Created (UTC),Description,Payout ID
txn_001,charge,100.00,2.15,97.85,eur,2026-10-10 10:15:00,Cobro Pedido #101,po_batch_123
txn_002,charge,50.00,1.20,48.80,eur,2026-10-10 11:20:00,Cobro Pedido #102,po_batch_123
txn_003,refund,-20.00,0.00,-20.00,eur,2026-10-11 09:00:00,Reembolso Parcial #99,po_batch_123
"""
    transactions = StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(sample_csv)
    assert len(transactions) == 3

    t1 = transactions[0]
    assert t1.transaction_id == "txn_001"
    assert t1.gross_cents == 10000
    assert t1.fee_cents == 215
    assert t1.net_cents == 9785

    payouts = StripeParser.group_by_payout(transactions)
    assert "po_batch_123" in payouts
    batch = payouts["po_batch_123"]
    # 97.85 + 48.80 - 20.00 = 126.65 € -> 12665 céntimos
    assert batch.net_amount_cents == 12665
    assert len(batch.transactions) == 3


def test_redsys_parser_operations():
    """Verifica el parsing de operaciones de TPV Redsys con formato español (; y comas decimales)."""
    sample_csv = """Terminal;Fecha;Hora;Número Pedido;Tipo Operación;Importe Bruto;Comisión;Importe Líquido;Autorización
TPV01;12/10/2026;14:22:10;PED-5544;0000;125,50 €;1,88 €;123,62 €;987654
TPV01;12/10/2026;15:10:05;PED-5545;0900;25,00 €;0,00 €;25,00 €;112233
"""
    transactions = RedsysParser.parse_operations_csv(sample_csv, delimiter=";")
    assert len(transactions) == 2

    # Venta
    assert transactions[0].type == "charge"
    assert transactions[0].gross_cents == 12550
    assert transactions[0].fee_cents == 188
    assert transactions[0].net_cents == 12362

    # Devolución (0900)
    assert transactions[1].type == "refund"
    assert transactions[1].gross_cents == -2500
    assert transactions[1].net_cents == -2500


def test_column_mapper_autodiscovery():
    """Verifica autodescubrimiento de columnas en hojas de clientes pyme."""
    sample_pyme_csv = """Nº Factura,Fecha Emisión,Vencimiento,Cliente,CIF/NIF,Total Factura,Concepto,Forma de Pago
F2026-001,01/10/2026,15/10/2026,ACME IBERICA S.L.,B12345678,250.00 €,Servicio Consultoría,stripe
F2026-002,02/10/2026,16/10/2026,HERMANOS LOPEZ CB,E98765432,120.50 €,Mantenimiento Web,redsys
"""
    invoices = ColumnMapper.parse_invoices_csv(sample_pyme_csv)
    assert len(invoices) == 2
    assert invoices[0].invoice_number == "F2026-001"
    assert invoices[0].customer_name == "ACME IBERICA S.L."
    assert invoices[0].customer_fiscal_id == "B12345678"
    assert invoices[0].gross_amount_cents == 25000
    assert invoices[0].gateway_id == "stripe"


================================================================================
FICHERO: tests\test_phase2_reconciliation.py
================================================================================

"""
Test Suite para la Fase 2: Motor de Reconciliación Algorítmica (SSMP con Fricción)
Verifica:
1. Poda Heurística en 3 Niveles (Temporal, Pasarela y Bounding Box).
2. Solver Exacto Meet-in-the-Middle (Horowitz-Sahni).
3. Solver HiGHS MILP con Función Objetivo de Penalización de Comisiones y Delta:
   min (lambda_1 * delta + lambda_2 * sum(|c_i - c_i^teorico| * x_i))
   Demostrando que descarta combinaciones espurias y selecciona el subconjunto óptimo.
4. Cuadre Contable a Cero de Partida Doble (572 Debe, 626 Debe, 430 Haber).
5. Orquestador End-to-End de Reconciliación de Batch Payouts.
"""
from datetime import date
import pytest

from src.parsers.base import (
    CanonicalBankTransaction,
    CanonicalInvoice,
    CanonicalGatewayTransaction,
    TransactionType,
)
from src.reconciliation.pruning import ReconciliationPruner
from src.reconciliation.meet_in_the_middle import MeetInTheMiddleSolver, SubsetSumCandidate
from src.reconciliation.milp_solver import HighsMILPSolver, MILPInvoiceCandidate
from src.accounting.double_entry import AccountingEntryBuilder
from src.reconciliation.engine import BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine


def test_reconciliation_pruner():
    """Verifica que la poda descarta facturas fuera de fecha, de pasarelas ajenas o con importes imposibles."""
    payout_date = date(2026, 10, 15)
    target_net_cents = 50000  # 500.00 €

    candidates = [
        # 1. Factura válida dentro de la ventana (12 Oct, Stripe, 200 €)
        CanonicalInvoice(
            invoice_id="INV-1",
            invoice_number="F-01",
            issue_date=date(2026, 10, 12),
            due_date=date(2026, 10, 12),
            customer_id="C-1",
            customer_name="Empresa A",
            gross_amount_cents=20000,
            gateway_id="stripe",
        ),
        # 2. Factura fuera de fecha (hace 40 días, 1 Septiembre) -> Poda Temporal
        CanonicalInvoice(
            invoice_id="INV-2",
            invoice_number="F-02",
            issue_date=date(2026, 9, 1),
            due_date=date(2026, 9, 1),
            customer_id="C-2",
            customer_name="Empresa B",
            gross_amount_cents=10000,
            gateway_id="stripe",
        ),
        # 3. Factura de otra pasarela (Redsys) -> Poda de Pasarela
        CanonicalInvoice(
            invoice_id="INV-3",
            invoice_number="F-03",
            issue_date=date(2026, 10, 14),
            due_date=date(2026, 10, 14),
            customer_id="C-3",
            customer_name="Empresa C",
            gross_amount_cents=15000,
            gateway_id="redsys",
        ),
        # 4. Factura desorbitada (10.000 €) frente a un abono de 500 € -> Poda Bounding Box
        CanonicalInvoice(
            invoice_id="INV-4",
            invoice_number="F-04",
            issue_date=date(2026, 10, 14),
            due_date=date(2026, 10, 14),
            customer_id="C-4",
            customer_name="Empresa D",
            gross_amount_cents=1000000,
            gateway_id="stripe",
        ),
    ]

    pruned = ReconciliationPruner.prune(
        target_net_cents=target_net_cents,
        payout_date=payout_date,
        candidates=candidates,
        gateway_id="stripe",
        max_lookback_days=7,
    )

    # Solo INV-1 debe sobrevivir a los 3 niveles de poda
    assert len(pruned.invoices) == 1
    assert pruned.invoices[0].invoice_id == "INV-1"
    assert pruned.pruning_stats["initial_count"] == 4
    assert pruned.pruning_stats["after_temporal"] == 3
    assert pruned.pruning_stats["after_gateway"] == 2
    assert pruned.pruning_stats["after_bounding"] == 1


def test_meet_in_the_middle_exact_match():
    """Verifica resolución exacta O(2^(n/2)) mediante Horowitz-Sahni."""
    # Target: 247.50 € -> 24750 céntimos
    target = 24750
    candidates = [
        SubsetSumCandidate("I1", net_cents=10000, gross_cents=10200, fee_cents=200),
        SubsetSumCandidate("I2", net_cents=5000, gross_cents=5100, fee_cents=100),
        SubsetSumCandidate("I3", net_cents=9750, gross_cents=10000, fee_cents=250), # I1 + I2 + I3 = 24750
        SubsetSumCandidate("I4", net_cents=3000, gross_cents=3100, fee_cents=100),
        SubsetSumCandidate("I5", net_cents=8000, gross_cents=8200, fee_cents=200),
    ]

    res = MeetInTheMiddleSolver.solve(target, candidates, tolerance_cents=0)
    assert res is not None
    assert res.is_exact is True
    assert res.delta_cents == 0
    assert set(res.matched_ids) == {"I1", "I2", "I3"}
    assert res.total_net_cents == 24750


def test_highs_milp_penalizes_spurious_combinations():
    """
    CRÍTICO: Demuestra que la función objetivo con penalización de desviación de comisiones:
    min (lambda_1 * delta + lambda_2 * sum(|c_i - c_i^teorico| * x_i))
    descarta combinaciones espurias que tendrían el mismo neto pero comisiones inconsistentes.
    """
    # Objetivo: liquidación bancaria de 100.00 € (10000 céntimos)
    target_net = 10000

    # Dos candidatos idénticos en neto (10000), pero uno tiene comisión legítima (25 céntimos)
    # y el otro tiene una comisión distorsionada/espuria (300 céntimos):
    legitimate_candidate = MILPInvoiceCandidate(
        invoice_id="INV-LEGIT",
        gross_cents=10250,
        observed_fee_cents=250,
        theoretical_fee_cents=250,  # |c - c_teo| = 0
        net_cents=10000,
    )
    spurious_candidate = MILPInvoiceCandidate(
        invoice_id="INV-SPURIOUS",
        gross_cents=10550,
        observed_fee_cents=550,
        theoretical_fee_cents=250,  # |c - c_teo| = 300 (penalización severa)
        net_cents=10000,
    )

    candidates = [spurious_candidate, legitimate_candidate]

    res = HighsMILPSolver.solve(
        target_net_cents=target_net,
        invoices=candidates,
        tolerance_cents=1,
        lambda_1=100.0,
        lambda_2=1.0,
    )

    assert res.is_success is True
    # El solver DEBE seleccionar el candidato legítimo debido al coste de penalización
    assert res.matched_invoice_ids == ["INV-LEGIT"]
    assert res.total_fee_deviation_cents == 0.0


def test_double_entry_balance_and_cents_perfection():
    """Verifica que el generador contable crea un asiento PGC estrictamente cuadrado a cero (Debe == Haber)."""
    payout_net = 97500  # 975.00 € recibido en banco
    matched_invoices = [
        {"account_code": "430000001", "invoice_number": "F-101", "gross_cents": 50000, "fee_cents": 1250, "customer_name": "ACME 1"},
        {"account_code": "430000002", "invoice_number": "F-102", "gross_cents": 50000, "fee_cents": 1250, "customer_name": "ACME 2"},
    ]
    # Total Bruto = 1000.00 € (100000 céntimos)
    # Total Comisiones = 25.00 € (2500 céntimos)
    # Neto = 975.00 € (97500 céntimos)

    journal = AccountingEntryBuilder.build_payout_reconciliation_entry(
        entry_number=101,
        entry_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
        payout_net_cents=payout_net,
        matched_invoices=matched_invoices,
        gateway_name="Stripe",
    )

    assert journal.is_balanced is True
    assert journal.imbalance_cents == 0
    assert journal.total_debit_cents == 100000
    assert journal.total_credit_cents == 100000

    # Comprobar desglose de cuentas
    bank_line = [l for l in journal.lines if l.account_code == "572000001"][0]
    assert bank_line.debit_cents == 97500
    assert bank_line.credit_cents == 0

    fee_line = [l for l in journal.lines if l.account_code == "626000001"][0]
    assert fee_line.debit_cents == 2500
    assert fee_line.credit_cents == 0

    cust_lines = [l for l in journal.lines if l.account_code.startswith("430")]
    assert len(cust_lines) == 2
    assert all(l.credit_cents == 50000 for l in cust_lines)


def test_batch_payout_reconciliation_engine_end_to_end():
    """Verifica el flujo integral del motor: apunte bancario + facturas abiertas -> reconciliación y asiento."""
    engine = BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine()

    bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-N43-0099",
        account_iban="ES0000491234001234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 14),
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 14),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=19430,  # 194.30 €
        concept="STRIPE PAYOUT po_live_9988",
    )

    # Creamos un conjunto de facturas donde exactamente F-01 y F-02 suman el abono
    # F-01: 100.00 € bruto - (1.5% + 0.25€ = 1.75€ com) = 98.25 € neto -> 9825 cents
    # F-02: 98.00 € bruto - (1.5% + 0.25€ = 1.72€ com) = 96.28 € neto -> 9628 cents
    # 98.25 + 96.28 = 194.53 € -> ajustado
    invoices = [
        CanonicalInvoice("I-1", "F-01", date(2026, 10, 12), date(2026, 10, 12), "C1", "Cliente Alpha", 10000, gateway_id="stripe"),
        CanonicalInvoice("I-2", "F-02", date(2026, 10, 13), date(2026, 10, 13), "C2", "Cliente Beta", 9800, gateway_id="stripe"),
        CanonicalInvoice("I-3", "F-03", date(2026, 10, 13), date(2026, 10, 13), "C3", "Cliente Gamma", 5500, gateway_id="stripe"),
    ]

    # Pasamos las comisiones exactas observadas en el reporte de la pasarela:
    # F-01 comision = 185 céntimos -> neto = 9815 céntimos
    # F-02 comision = 185 céntimos -> neto = 9615 céntimos
    # Total neto = 9815 + 9615 = 19430 céntimos (194.30 € exactos)
    gw_txs = [
        CanonicalGatewayTransaction("GW-1", "stripe", date(2026, 10, 12), "charge", 10000, 185, 9815, order_id="F-01"),
        CanonicalGatewayTransaction("GW-2", "stripe", date(2026, 10, 13), "charge", 9800, 185, 9615, order_id="F-02"),
    ]

    report = engine.reconcile_payout(
        bank_tx=bank_tx,
        open_invoices=invoices,
        gateway_transactions=gw_txs,
        gateway_id="stripe",
    )

    assert report.is_reconciled is True
    assert set(report.matched_invoice_ids) == {"I-1", "I-2"}
    assert report.total_gross_cents == 19800
    assert report.total_fees_cents == 370
    assert report.total_net_cents == 19430
    assert report.discrepancy_cents == 0.0
    assert report.journal_entry is not None
    assert report.journal_entry.is_balanced is True


================================================================================
FICHERO: tests\test_phase3_disambiguation.py
================================================================================

"""
Test Suite para la Fase 3: Motor de Desambiguación Difusa y NLP en Castellano
Verifica:
1. Depuración léxica y supresión de literales bancarios (TRANSF. DE:, ORD:, BENEF:).
2. Validación determinista de NIF, CIF y NIE (Módulo 23 y Módulo 11) y patrones de facturas.
3. Matching fonético en castellano (B/V, C/Z/S, yeísmo) y Token Set Ratio con RapidFuzz.
4. Confidence Scorer y política de semáforo tricolor (Verde >= 95%, Amarillo 70-94%, Rojo < 70%).
"""
from datetime import date
import pytest

from src.parsers.base import CanonicalBankTransaction, CanonicalInvoice, TransactionType
from src.disambiguation.normalizer import SpanishBankTextNormalizer
from src.disambiguation.fiscal_validators import SpanishFiscalValidator
from src.disambiguation.phonetic import SpanishPhoneticEncoder
from src.disambiguation.fuzzy_matcher import SpanishFuzzyMatcher
from src.disambiguation.confidence_scorer import BankDisambiguationScorer, DisambiguationStatus


def test_bank_text_normalizer():
    """Verifica la eliminación de prefijos bancarios y normalización de tildes manteniendo Ñ."""
    raw_1 = "TRANSF. DE: TALLERES MAÑANA S.L. - PAGO FRA. 2026/01"
    cleaned_1 = SpanishBankTextNormalizer.clean(raw_1)
    assert "TRANSF" not in cleaned_1
    assert "PAGO FRA" not in cleaned_1
    assert "MAÑANA" in cleaned_1  # La Ñ se preserva
    assert "TALLERES" in cleaned_1

    raw_2 = "ORD: CONST. RODRÍGUEZ E HIJOS / BENEF: SUMINISTROS"
    cleaned_2 = SpanishBankTextNormalizer.clean(raw_2)
    assert "ORD" not in cleaned_2
    assert "BENEF" not in cleaned_2
    assert "RODRIGUEZ" in cleaned_2  # Tilde eliminada


def test_spanish_fiscal_validator_modulo11_and_23():
    """Verifica validación algorítmica de NIF (personas físicas), CIF (sociedades) y NIE (extranjeros)."""
    # 1. NIFs válidos conocidos (Módulo 23)
    assert SpanishFiscalValidator.validate_nif("12345678Z") is True
    assert SpanishFiscalValidator.validate_nif("12345678A") is False  # Letra errónea

    # 2. CIFs válidos (Módulo 11 oficial)
    # A58818501 (Telefónica) o B82387770
    assert SpanishFiscalValidator.validate_cif("B82387770") is True
    assert SpanishFiscalValidator.validate_cif("B82387779") is False  # Dígito control inválido

    # 3. NIEs válidos (X, Y, Z + Módulo 23)
    assert SpanishFiscalValidator.validate_nie("X1234567L") is True
    assert SpanishFiscalValidator.validate_nie("X1234567Z") is False

    # 4. Extracción desde texto bancario libre
    sample_text = "TRANSF DE JUAN PEREZ CON DNI 12345678Z POR SERVICIOS"
    extracted = SpanishFiscalValidator.extract_fiscal_id(sample_text)
    assert extracted is not None
    assert extracted[0] == "NIF"
    assert extracted[1] == "12345678Z"


def test_phonetic_and_fuzzy_matching():
    """Verifica que el matching fonético y Token Set Ratio superan inversiones de nombre y grafías homófonas (B/V, C/Z/S)."""
    # Homofonía B / V y C / Z
    str_1 = "BOTELLAS VELASCO"
    str_2 = "VOTELLAS BELASCO"
    assert SpanishPhoneticEncoder.encode(str_1) == SpanishPhoneticEncoder.encode(str_2)

    # Inversión de nombre de cliente («GARCIA LOPEZ JUAN» vs «JUAN GARCIA LOPEZ»)
    name_a = "GARCIA LOPEZ JUAN"
    name_b = "JUAN GARCIA LOPEZ"
    score, _ = SpanishFuzzyMatcher.match_strings(name_a, name_b)
    assert score >= 0.95  # Token Set Ratio identifica equivalencia completa


def test_confidence_scorer_traffic_light_green():
    """Verifica que una factura con importe exacto, fecha coincidente y NIF o nombre claro obtiene VERDE (>= 95%)."""
    bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-001",
        account_iban="ES0000491234001234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=50000,  # 500.00 €
        concept="TRANSF DE LOPEZ Y ASOCIADOS SL FRA 2026-44 B82387770",
        payer_or_payee="LOPEZ Y ASOCIADOS SL",
    )

    invoice = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-44",
        invoice_number="2026-44",
        issue_date=date(2026, 10, 8),
        due_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        customer_id="CUST-44",
        customer_name="LOPEZ Y ASOCIADOS S.L.",
        gross_amount_cents=50000,  # 500.00 €
        customer_fiscal_id="B82387770",
    )

    res = BankDisambiguationScorer.evaluate(bank_tx, invoice)
    assert res.status == DisambiguationStatus.GREEN
    assert res.confidence_score >= 0.95
    assert res.fiscal_match is True
    assert res.invoice_number_match is True


def test_confidence_scorer_traffic_light_yellow():
    """Verifica que una discrepancia leve de días o errata menor genera AMARILLO (70% - 94%) para 1-clic."""
    bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-002",
        account_iban="ES0000491234001234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 20),  # 15 días tras vencimiento
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 20),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=50000,
        concept="PAGO SERVICIOS JULIO GARCIA",
        payer_or_payee="GARCIA JULIO",
    )

    invoice = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-55",
        invoice_number="2026-99",
        issue_date=date(2026, 10, 1),
        due_date=date(2026, 10, 5),
        customer_id="CUST-55",
        customer_name="JULIO GARCIA PEREZ",
        gross_amount_cents=50000,
    )

    res = BankDisambiguationScorer.evaluate(bank_tx, invoice)
    assert res.status == DisambiguationStatus.YELLOW
    assert 0.70 <= res.confidence_score < 0.95


def test_confidence_scorer_traffic_light_red():
    """Verifica que un concepto o importe no coincidente resulta en ROJO (< 70%) evitando falsos positivos."""
    bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-003",
        account_iban="ES0000491234001234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=12000,  # 120.00 €
        concept="ABONO FERRETERIA CENTRAL",
        payer_or_payee="FERRETERIA CENTRAL",
    )

    invoice = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-99",
        invoice_number="2026-001",
        issue_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        due_date=date(2026, 10, 10),
        customer_id="CUST-99",
        customer_name="CLINICA DENTAL SANITAS",
        gross_amount_cents=80000,  # 800.00 €
    )

    res = BankDisambiguationScorer.evaluate(bank_tx, invoice)
    assert res.status == DisambiguationStatus.RED
    assert res.confidence_score < 0.70
    assert res.matched_invoice_id is None

FICHERO: tests\test_phase4_erp_exporters.py

"""
Test Suite para la Fase 4: Exportadores Contables Nativos para ERPs Españoles
Verifica:
1. Exportador Wolters Kluwer A3 (protocolo Suenlace / a3conv).
2. Exportador Sage 50 / Sage Despachos (estándar XDIARIO.csv).
3. Exportador DELSOL Contasol (hoja de cálculo XLSX nativa).
4. Rechazo preventivo de asientos contables no balanceados en todos los exportadores.
"""
from datetime import date
import io
import openpyxl
import pytest

from src.accounting.double_entry import AccountingEntryBuilder, DoubleEntryJournal, JournalEntryLine
from src.accounting.exporters.a3_suenlace import A3SuenlaceExporter
from src.accounting.exporters.sage_xdiario import SageXdiarioExporter
from src.accounting.exporters.contasol_xlsx import ContasolXlsxExporter


@pytest.fixture
def sample_balanced_journal() -> DoubleEntryJournal:
    """Crea un asiento de liquidación perfectamente balanceado a cero."""
    # 572 (Bancos): 98.00 € (9800 cents) al Debe
    # 626 (Comisiones): 2.00 € (200 cents) al Debe
    # 430 (Cliente): 100.00 € (10000 cents) al Haber
    journal = DoubleEntryJournal(
        entry_number=42,
        entry_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        description="Liquidación TPV Redsys 15/10/2026",
    )
    journal.lines = [
        JournalEntryLine(
            account_code="572000001",
            account_name="Banco Santander",
            concept="Cobro Liq Redsys",
            document_number="LIQ-20261015",
            debit_cents=9800,
            credit_cents=0,
            contrapartida="626000001",
        ),
        JournalEntryLine(
            account_code="626000001",
            account_name="Comisiones bancarias",
            concept="Comisión TPV Redsys",
            document_number="FEE-20261015",
            debit_cents=200,
            credit_cents=0,
            contrapartida="572000001",
        ),
        JournalEntryLine(
            account_code="430000123",
            account_name="Cliente Comercio S.L.",
            concept="Fra. F-2026-99",
            document_number="F-2026-99",
            debit_cents=0,
            credit_cents=10000,
            contrapartida="572000001",
        ),
    ]
    journal.check_balance()
    return journal


def test_a3_suenlace_exporter(sample_balanced_journal):
    """Verifica la exportación al formato de texto delimitado Suenlace de Wolters Kluwer A3."""
    output_text = A3SuenlaceExporter.export_journal_entries([sample_balanced_journal])
    lines = [l for l in output_text.splitlines() if l.strip()]

    assert len(lines) == 3
    # Formato: Asiento;Fecha;Cuenta;Signo;Importe;Concepto;Documento;Contrapartida
    # Línea 1: Banco Santander
    parts_1 = lines[0].split(";")
    assert parts_1[0] == "000042"          # Asiento formateado
    assert parts_1[1] == "15102026"        # Fecha DDMMAAAA
    assert parts_1[2] == "572000001"       # Cuenta 9 dígitos
    assert parts_1[3] == "D"               # Signo Debe
    assert parts_1[4] == "98.00"           # Importe 98.00
    assert "Cobro Liq Redsys" in parts_1[5]

    # Línea 3: Cliente (Haber)
    parts_3 = lines[2].split(";")
    assert parts_3[2] == "430000123"
    assert parts_3[3] == "H"
    assert parts_3[4] == "100.00"


def test_sage_xdiario_exporter(sample_balanced_journal):
    """Verifica la exportación a XDIARIO.csv para Sage 50 y Sage Despachos."""
    csv_text = SageXdiarioExporter.export_xdiario_csv([sample_balanced_journal], delimiter=";")
    lines = [l for l in csv_text.splitlines() if l.strip()]

    # Cabecera + 3 líneas
    assert len(lines) == 4
    header = lines[0].split(";")
    assert header == ["Asiento", "Fecha", "Cuenta", "Concepto", "Documento", "Debe", "Haber", "Factura", "Contrapartida"]

    # Fila Debe banco
    row_1 = lines[1].split(";")
    assert row_1[0] == "42"
    assert row_1[1] == "15/10/2026"
    assert row_1[2] == "572000001"
    assert row_1[5] == "98.00"
    assert row_1[6] == "0.00"

    # Fila Haber cliente
    row_3 = lines[3].split(";")
    assert row_3[2] == "430000123"
    assert row_3[5] == "0.00"
    assert row_3[6] == "100.00"


def test_contasol_xlsx_exporter(sample_balanced_journal):
    """Verifica la exportación binaria a Excel XLSX para DELSOL Contasol."""
    xlsx_bytes = ContasolXlsxExporter.export_xlsx([sample_balanced_journal])
    assert len(xlsx_bytes) > 0

    # Cargar el libro desde memoria con openpyxl para verificar celdas
    wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(io.BytesIO(xlsx_bytes))
    ws = wb.active
    assert ws.title == "Asientos Contables"

    # Fila 1: Cabeceras
    headers = [cell.value for cell in ws[1]]
    assert headers == ["Diario", "Fecha", "Asiento", "Subcuenta", "Concepto", "Documento", "Debe", "Haber", "Contrapartida"]

    # Filas de datos
    assert ws.max_row == 4
    # Fila 2: Banco
    row_2 = [cell.value for cell in ws[2]]
    assert row_2[0] == 1               # Diario
    assert row_2[1] == "15/10/2026"    # Fecha
    assert row_2[2] == 42              # Asiento
    assert row_2[3] == "572000001"     # Subcuenta
    assert row_2[6] == 98.00           # Debe
    assert row_2[7] == 0.00            # Haber

    # Fila 4: Cliente
    row_4 = [cell.value for cell in ws[4]]
    assert row_4[3] == "430000123"
    assert row_4[6] == 0.00
    assert row_4[7] == 100.00


def test_exporters_reject_unbalanced_journals():
    """Verifica que todos los exportadores bloquean la emisión si un asiento contable no suma cero."""
    unbalanced = DoubleEntryJournal(
        entry_number=99,
        entry_date=date(2026, 10, 15),
        description="Asiento corrupto",
    )
    unbalanced.lines = [
        JournalEntryLine("572000001", "Banco", "Test", "DOC1", debit_cents=10000, credit_cents=0),
        JournalEntryLine("430000001", "Cliente", "Test", "DOC2", debit_cents=0, credit_cents=9500), # Descuadre de 500 cts
    ]

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="descuadrado|no está cuadrado"):
        A3SuenlaceExporter.export_journal_entries([unbalanced])

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="descuadre"):
        SageXdiarioExporter.export_xdiario_csv([unbalanced])

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="descuadrado"):
        ContasolXlsxExporter.export_xlsx([unbalanced])

"""
Test Suite para la Fase 5: Verificación End-to-End de la API REST FastAPI
Verifica:
1. Endpoint de estado y salud /health
2. Endpoint de ingesta /api/v1/ingest/norma43 con fichero real codificado en ISO-8859-15
3. Endpoint de desambiguación /api/v1/disambiguate/transfer
4. Endpoint de reconciliación algorítmica /api/v1/reconcile/batch-payout
5. Generación de ficheros auditados de pre-asientos en modo Solo Lectura
"""
from datetime import date
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import pytest

from src.main import app
from tests.test_phase1_ingestion import build_sample_norma43_text


@pytest.fixture
def client():
    return TestClient(app)


def test_health_check(client):
    """Verifica que el servicio esté operativo y en modo Solo Lectura."""
    response = client.get("/health")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert data["status"] == "healthy"
    assert data["staging_mode"] == "read_only_zero_liability"


def test_api_ingest_norma43_file(client):
    """Verifica la subida de un extracto bancario Norma 43 codificado en ISO-8859-15."""
    n43_text = build_sample_norma43_text(include_special_chars=True)
    raw_bytes = n43_text.encode("iso-8859-15")

    response = client.post(
        "/api/v1/ingest/norma43",
        files={"file": ("extracto_octubre.n43", raw_bytes, "application/octet-stream")},
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert data["is_valid"] is True
    assert data["detected_encoding"] in ("iso-8859-15", "windows-1252", "iso-8859-1")
    assert data["movements_count"] == 2
    assert len(data["validation_errors"]) == 0


def test_api_disambiguate_transfer(client):
    """Verifica la desambiguación de una transferencia bancaria española con Confidence Scorer."""
    payload = {
        "bank_amount_cents": 35000,           # 350.00 €
        "bank_date": "2026-10-14",
        "bank_concept": "TRANSF. DE: JUAN CARLOS GARCIA FRA 2026-88",
        "invoice_number": "2026-88",
        "invoice_amount_cents": 35000,       # 350.00 €
        "invoice_due_date": "2026-10-14",
        "customer_name": "GARCIA JUAN CARLOS",
        "customer_fiscal_id": "12345678Z",
    }

    response = client.post("/api/v1/disambiguate/transfer", json=payload)
    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert data["status"] == "GREEN"
    assert data["confidence_score"] >= 0.95
    assert data["invoice_number_match"] is True


def test_api_reconcile_batch_payout(client):
    """Verifica la conciliación de liquidación agrupada mediante endpoint REST."""
    # Liquidación bancaria neta teórica: 98.25 € + 96.28 € = 194.53 € (19453 céntimos)
    payout_req = {
        "payout_amount_cents": 19453,
        "payout_date": "2026-10-14",
        "bank_account_iban": "ES0000491234001234567890",
        "gateway_id": "stripe",
        "company_id": "COMP-001",
        "bank_account_code": "572000001",
        "fee_account_code": "626000001",
    }

    invoices_payload = [
        {
            "invoice_id": "I-01",
            "invoice_number": "F-01",
            "issue_date": "2026-10-12",
            "due_date": "2026-10-12",
            "customer_id": "C-1",
            "customer_name": "Cliente A",
            "gross_amount_cents": 10000,
            "gateway_id": "stripe",
        },
        {
            "invoice_id": "I-02",
            "invoice_number": "F-02",
            "issue_date": "2026-10-13",
            "due_date": "2026-10-13",
            "customer_id": "C-2",
            "customer_name": "Cliente B",
            "gross_amount_cents": 9800,
            "gateway_id": "stripe",
        },
    ]

    response = client.post(
        "/api/v1/reconcile/batch-payout",
        json={"req": payout_req, "invoices": invoices_payload},
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200
    data = response.json()
    assert data["is_reconciled"] is True
    assert data["is_balanced"] is True
    assert set(data["matched_invoice_ids"]) == {"I-01", "I-02"}
    assert len(data["journal_lines"]) >= 3
    # Comprobar que suma de Debe == Haber
    total_debit = sum(line["debit_eur"] for line in data["journal_lines"])
    total_credit = sum(line["credit_eur"] for line in data["journal_lines"])
    assert round(total_debit, 2) == round(total_credit, 2)

    "sqlite:///:memory:",
    connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    poolclass=StaticPool,
)
TestingSessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=TEST_ENGINE)

        Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=TEST_ENGINE)
