def test_batch_creation_and_transaction_persistence(db_session):
    """Verifica la creación de lotes, partidas y cálculo de estadísticas contables."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Asesoría Contable Norte",
        fiscal_id="B-77112233",
        email="norte@asesoria.es",
    )
    client = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Ferretería Industrial SL",
        fiscal_id="B-33445566",
    )

    batch = ReconciliationRepository.create_batch(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        client_id=client.id,
        period_year=2026,
        period_type="Q2",
        start_month=4,
        end_month=6,
        file_name="extracto_2t_2026.n43",
    )
    assert batch.period_year == 2026
    assert batch.start_month == 4

    # Añadir 1 partida Verde
    tx1 = ReconciliationRepository.add_transaction(
        db=db_session,
        batch_id=batch.id,
        external_id="TX-01",
        source="Stripe",
        operation_date=date(2026, 4, 15),
        value_date=date(2026, 4, 15),
        concept="Liquidación Stripe #101",
        net_amount_cents=100000,
        gross_amount_cents=102500,
        fee_amount_cents=2500,
        status="GREEN",
        confidence_score=0.985,
    )

    # Añadir 1 partida Amarilla
    tx2 = ReconciliationRepository.add_transaction(
        db=db_session,
        batch_id=batch.id,
        external_id="TX-02",
        source="Norma 43",
        operation_date=date(2026, 5, 10),
        value_date=date(2026, 5, 10),
        concept="TRANSF. CONST MARTINEZ",
        net_amount_cents=50000,
        gross_amount_cents=50000,
        fee_amount_cents=0,
        status="YELLOW",
        confidence_score=0.820,
    )

    # Recalcular estadísticas del lote
    updated_batch = ReconciliationRepository.update_batch_statistics(db_session, batch.id)
    assert updated_batch.total_movements == 2
    assert updated_batch.total_volume_cents == 150000
    assert updated_batch.green_count == 1
    assert updated_batch.yellow_count == 1
    assert updated_batch.red_count == 0


def test_approval_workflow_and_audit_log_hash_chain(db_session):
    """Verifica el flujo de aprobación interactiva y la cadena inmutable de hashes SHA-256."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Despacho Central Auditores",
        fiscal_id="B-55667788",
        email="central@audit.es",
    )
    client = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Logística Exprés SL",
        fiscal_id="B-22334455",
    )
    batch = ReconciliationRepository.create_batch(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        client_id=client.id,
        period_year=2026,
    )

    tx_yellow = ReconciliationRepository.add_transaction(
        db=db_session,
        batch_id=batch.id,
        external_id="TX-YEL-01",
        source="Redsys TPV",
        operation_date=date(2026, 6, 1),
        value_date=date(2026, 6, 1),
        concept="Abono TPV Comercio 01/06",
        net_amount_cents=85000,
        gross_amount_cents=86000,
        fee_amount_cents=1000,
        status="YELLOW",
        confidence_score=0.840,
    )
    assert tx_yellow.status == "YELLOW"
    assert not tx_yellow.is_approved

    # 1. Aprobar quirúrgicamente la partida
    approved_tx = ReconciliationRepository.approve_transaction(
        db=db_session,
        transaction_id=tx_yellow.id,
        approved_by="auditor_jefe@despacho.es",
        note="Verificado albarán firmado y comprobante TPV.",
    )
    assert approved_tx.status == "GREEN"
    assert approved_tx.is_approved is True
    assert approved_tx.approved_by == "auditor_jefe@despacho.es"

    # 2. Comprobar la cadena de bloques inmutable del Audit Trail
    logs = ReconciliationRepository.get_audit_trail(db_session, tenant.id, batch_id=batch.id)
    assert len(logs) >= 2  # BATCH_CREATED y TRANSACTION_APPROVED

    # Verificar enlace de hashes
    log_created = logs[0]
    log_approved = logs[1]

    assert log_created.action == "BATCH_CREATED"
    assert log_created.prev_hash.startswith("GENESIS_BLOCK_")
    assert len(log_created.current_hash_sha256) == 64

    assert log_approved.action == "TRANSACTION_APPROVED"
    # El prev_hash del segundo registro debe coincidir exactamente con el current_hash del primero
    assert log_approved.prev_hash == log_created.current_hash_sha256
    assert len(log_approved.current_hash_sha256) == 64


def test_bulk_approval_of_yellow_transactions(db_session):
    """Verifica la aprobación masiva en 1-clic de partidas amarillas."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Gestoría Rápida SL",
        fiscal_id="B-44332211",
        email="rapida@gestoria.es",
    )
    client = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Retail Sur SL",
        fiscal_id="B-66778899",
    )
    batch = ReconciliationRepository.create_batch(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        client_id=client.id,
        period_year=2026,
    )

    for i in range(3):
        ReconciliationRepository.add_transaction(
            db=db_session,
            batch_id=batch.id,
            external_id=f"TX-Y-{i}",
            source="Norma 43",
            operation_date=date(2026, 4, 10 + i),
            value_date=date(2026, 4, 10 + i),
            concept=f"Transf Cliente {i}",
            net_amount_cents=10000 * (i + 1),
            gross_amount_cents=10000 * (i + 1),
            fee_amount_cents=0,
            status="YELLOW",
            confidence_score=0.810,
        )

    # Actualizar estadísticas iniciales
    b = ReconciliationRepository.update_batch_statistics(db_session, batch.id)
    assert b.yellow_count == 3
    assert b.green_count == 0

    # Ejecutar bulk approve
    count = ReconciliationRepository.bulk_approve_yellows(db_session, batch.id, approved_by="auditor@gestoria.es")
    assert count == 3

    # Verificar que todas pasaron a Verde
    b_updated = ReconciliationRepository.get_batch(db_session, batch.id)
    assert b_updated.yellow_count == 0
    assert b_updated.green_count == 3

def test_batch_service_processing_and_erp_export(db_session):
    """Verifica el flujo completo de BatchReconciliationService: proceso, persistencia y exportación oficial."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Despacho Integral SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-12998877",
        email="integral@despacho.es",
    )
    client = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Moda E-Commerce Ibérica SL",
        fiscal_id="B-88776655",
        default_erp="A3",
        account_length=9,
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
    )

    # Facturas vivas
    inv1 = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-01",
        invoice_number="FRA-2026-001",
        issue_date=date(2026, 4, 10),
        due_date=date(2026, 4, 10),
        customer_id="CUST-101",
        customer_name="Cliente Uno",
        gross_amount_cents=100000,
        customer_fiscal_id="B-11111111",
    )
    inv2 = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-02",
        invoice_number="FRA-2026-002",
        issue_date=date(2026, 4, 11),
        due_date=date(2026, 4, 11),
        customer_id="CUST-102",
        customer_name="Cliente Dos",
        gross_amount_cents=200000,
        customer_fiscal_id="B-22222222",
    )

    # Movimiento Stripe agrupado: 1000.00 + 2000.00 = 3000.00 € bruto. Comisión ~ 1.4% = 42.00 €. Neto = 2958.00 € (295800 cents)
    bank_tx_stripe = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-STRIPE-PO-1",
        account_iban="ES1200491500051234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 4, 15),
        value_date=date(2026, 4, 15),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=295800,
        concept="STRIPE PAYOUT po_12345",
        payer_or_payee="Stripe Payments Europe",
    )

    # Movimiento transferencia directa SEPA
    inv3 = CanonicalInvoice(
        invoice_id="INV-03",
        invoice_number="FRA-2026-003",
        issue_date=date(2026, 4, 18),
        due_date=date(2026, 4, 18),
        customer_id="CUST-103",
        customer_name="LOGISTICA CENTRAL SL",
        gross_amount_cents=75000,
        customer_fiscal_id="B-33333333",
    )
    bank_tx_sepa = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="TX-SEPA-01",
        account_iban="ES1200491500051234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 4, 20),
        value_date=date(2026, 4, 20),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=75000,
        concept="TRANSF DE LOGISTICA CENTRAL SL PAGO FACT FRA-2026-003",
        payer_or_payee="LOGISTICA CENTRAL SL",
    )

    # Ejecutar BatchReconciliationService
    batch = BatchReconciliationService.process_and_persist_batch(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        client_id=client.id,
        period_year=2026,
        bank_transactions=[bank_tx_stripe, bank_tx_sepa],
        open_invoices=[inv1, inv2, inv3],
        period_type="Q2",
        start_month=4,
        end_month=6,
        file_name="extracto_abril_2026.n43",
        file_bytes=b"dummy_n43_bytes_abril",
    )

    assert batch.total_movements == 2
    assert batch.green_count >= 1

    # Exportar a A3 (Suenlace)
    a3_res = BatchReconciliationService.export_batch_to_erp(db_session, batch.id, target_erp="A3")
    assert a3_res["erp"] == "A3"
    assert a3_res["filename"].endswith(".txt")
    assert isinstance(a3_res["content"], str)
    assert len(a3_res["content"]) > 0

    # Exportar a Sage 50 (XDIARIO)
    sage_res = BatchReconciliationService.export_batch_to_erp(db_session, batch.id, target_erp="SAGE")
    assert sage_res["erp"] == "SAGE"
    assert sage_res["filename"].endswith(".csv")
    assert "Asiento" in sage_res["content"] or "Fecha" in sage_res["content"] or ";" in sage_res["content"]

    # Exportar a Contasol (XLSX)
    contasol_res = BatchReconciliationService.export_batch_to_erp(db_session, batch.id, target_erp="CONTASOL")
    assert contasol_res["erp"] == "CONTASOL"
    assert contasol_res["filename"].endswith(".xlsx")
    assert isinstance(contasol_res["content"], bytes)
    assert contasol_res["content"][:2] == b"PK"  # Magic bytes de ZIP/XLSX


def test_upload_and_process_batch_api_endpoint(client_api):
    """Verifica la ingesta, conciliación y persistencia mediante el endpoint HTTP multipart POST /batches."""
    # 1. Crear tenant y cliente vía API
    res_tenant = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/tenants",
        json={
            "name": "Auditores Norte SL",
            "fiscal_id": "B-88776655",
            "email": "norte@auditores.es",
            "plan": "PROFESSIONAL",
        },
    )
    assert res_tenant.status_code == 201
    tenant_id = res_tenant.json()["id"]

    res_client = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/clients",
        json={
            "tenant_id": tenant_id,
            "name": "Distribución Gastronómica SL",
            "fiscal_id": "B-54892103",
            "default_erp": "CONTASOL",
            "account_length": 9,
            "bank_account_code": "572000001",
            "fee_account_code": "626000001",
        },
    )
    assert res_client.status_code == 201
    client_id = res_client.json()["id"]

    # 2. Subir fichero Norma 43
    from pathlib import Path
    n43_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data" / "pilot" / "cliente3_norma43.n43"
    with open(n43_path, "rb") as f:
        file_content = f.read()

    res = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/batches",
        data={
            "tenant_id": tenant_id,
            "client_id": client_id,
            "period_year": 2026,
            "period_type": "Q4",
            "start_month": 10,
            "end_month": 12,
        },
        files={
            "file": ("cliente3_norma43.n43", file_content, "text/plain"),
        },
    )
    assert res.status_code == 201
    data = res.json()
    assert "id" in data
    assert data["total_movements"] == 4
    assert data["client_id"] == client_id

"""
Fase 7 / Nivel 3: Prueba Piloto Interna End-to-End con Datos Reales de Internet y Banca Española
Simula el flujo completo para un despacho auditor gestionando 3 empresas clientes:
1. E-Commerce (Shopify / Stripe) -> Exportación a Wolters Kluwer A3 (Suenlace)
2. Comercio Físico (Redsys TPV) -> Exportación a Sage 50 / Despachos (XDIARIO)
3. Distribución B2B (Norma 43 Banco Santander) -> Exportación a DELSOL Contasol (.xlsx)

Verifica cuadre a céntimo cero (0.00 €), inmutabilidad SHA-256 y generación física de ficheros.
"""
import json
import os
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from src.database.connection import Base
from src.database.repository import ReconciliationRepository
from src.parsers.bank.norma43 import Norma43Parser
from src.parsers.gateways.stripe import StripeParser
from src.parsers.gateways.redsys import RedsysParser

from src.accounting.exporters.a3_suenlace import A3SuenlaceExporter
from src.accounting.exporters.sage_xdiario import SageXdiarioExporter
from src.accounting.exporters.contasol_xlsx import ContasolXlsxExporter

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
PILOT_DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "pilot"
OUTPUT_DIR = BASE_DIR / "output_pilot"
OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

PILOT_ENGINE = create_engine(
    "sqlite:///:memory:",
    connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    poolclass=StaticPool,
)
PilotSessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=PILOT_ENGINE)


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def pilot_db():
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=PILOT_ENGINE)
    db = PilotSessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()
        Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=PILOT_ENGINE)


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def pilot_invoices():
    json_path = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cartera_viva_facturas.json"
    with open(json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    invoices = []
    for item in data:
        y, m, d = item["issue_date"].split("-")
        issue_d = date(int(y), int(m), int(d))
        invoices.append(
            CanonicalInvoice(
                invoice_id=item["invoice_id"],
                invoice_number=item["invoice_number"],
                issue_date=issue_d,
                due_date=issue_d,
                customer_id=item["customer_id"],
                customer_name=item["customer_name"],
                gross_amount_cents=item["gross_amount_cents"],
                customer_fiscal_id=item["customer_fiscal_id"],
            )
        )
    return invoices


def test_pilot_client1_ecommerce_stripe_to_a3(pilot_db, pilot_invoices):
    """
    CLIENTE 1: Tienda Online E-commerce
    - Fuente: Stripe Balance Transactions Report (CSV real descargado de Stripe)
    - Liquidación agrupada: Payout neto de 3.823,82 €
    - Facturas asociadas: 4 pedidos (1.250€ + 890€ + 1.100€ + 695€ = 3.935,00€ bruto)
    - Comisiones Stripe: 111,18 €
    - Destino: Wolters Kluwer A3 (Suenlace .txt)
    """
    # 1. Crear Despacho y Empresa Cliente 1
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=pilot_db,
        name="KÖNIG & ASOCIADOS AUDITORES SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-99881122",
        email="auditoria@konigpartners.es",
    )
    client1 = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=pilot_db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Iberia Retail Logistics S.L.",
        fiscal_id="B-87654321",
        default_erp="A3",
        account_length=9,
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
    )

    # 2. Ingesta de fichero Stripe
    csv_path = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cliente1_stripe_balance.csv"
    with open(csv_path, "rb") as f:
        file_bytes = f.read()

    stripe_txs = StripeParser.parse_balance_transactions_csv(file_bytes)
    assert len(stripe_txs) == 5  # 4 cargos + 1 payout

    payout_tx = next(t for t in stripe_txs if t.type == "payout")
    charges = [t for t in stripe_txs if t.type == "charge"]

    total_gross = sum(c.gross_cents for c in charges)
    total_fees = sum(c.fee_cents for c in charges)
    expected_net = total_gross - total_fees
    assert abs(payout_tx.net_cents) == expected_net == 382382  # 3.823,82 €

    # 3. Simular apunte bancario correspondiente en la cuenta corriente
    bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="BANCO-SANTANDER-STRIPE-PO1",
        account_iban="ES7600491500051234567890",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 7),
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 7),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=expected_net,
        concept="STRIPE PAYOUT po_1M9999 LIQUIDACION SEMANAL",
        payer_or_payee="Stripe Payments Europe",
    )

    # 4. Procesar Lote con BatchReconciliationService
    client1_invoices = [inv for inv in pilot_invoices if "STRIPE" in inv.invoice_id]
    assert len(client1_invoices) == 4

    batch = BatchReconciliationService.process_and_persist_batch(
        db=pilot_db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        client_id=client1.id,
        period_year=2026,
        period_type="Q4",
        start_month=10,
        end_month=12,
        bank_transactions=[bank_tx],
        open_invoices=client1_invoices,
        file_name="cliente1_stripe_balance.csv",
        file_bytes=file_bytes,
        gateway_name="Stripe",
    )

    assert batch.total_movements == 1
    assert batch.green_count == 1
    assert batch.imbalance_cents == 0

    # 5. Exportar a Wolters Kluwer A3 (Suenlace)
    export_res = BatchReconciliationService.export_batch_to_erp(pilot_db, batch.id, target_erp="A3")
    assert export_res["erp"] == "A3"
    assert export_res["entries_count"] == 1
    suenlace_text = export_res["content"]

    # Guardar archivo físico en disco
    out_file = OUTPUT_DIR / "SUENLACE_A3_CLIENTE1.txt"
    with open(out_file, "w", encoding="windows-1252") as f:
        f.write(suenlace_text)

    assert out_file.exists()
    assert len(suenlace_text.splitlines()) >= 6  # 1 banco + 1 comisiones + 4 clientes


def test_pilot_client2_retail_redsys_to_sage(pilot_db, pilot_invoices):
    """
    CLIENTE 2: Comercio Físico / Hostelería con TPV Redsys
    - Fuente: Redsys Operaciones CSV (descargado del portal bancario de comercios)
    - Liquidación: 1.701,08 € netos (1.720,00 € bruto - 18,92 € tasa descuento)
    - Facturas/Tickets asociados: 3 ventas de mostrador (750€ + 550€ + 420€)
    - Destino: Sage 50 / Despachos (XDIARIO.csv)
    """
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.list_tenants(pilot_db)[0]
    client2 = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=pilot_db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Novatech Software Ibérica S.A.",
        fiscal_id="A-91283746",
        default_erp="SAGE",
        account_length=10,
        bank_account_code="5720000001",
        fee_account_code="6260000001",
    )

    # 1. Ingesta Redsys CSV
    csv_path = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cliente2_redsys_tpv.csv"
    with open(csv_path, "rb") as f:
        file_bytes = f.read()

    redsys_txs = RedsysParser.parse_operations_csv(file_bytes, delimiter=";")
    assert len(redsys_txs) == 3

    total_gross = sum(t.gross_cents for t in redsys_txs)
    total_fees = sum(t.fee_cents for t in redsys_txs)
    net_payout = total_gross - total_fees
    assert total_gross == 172000
    assert total_fees == 1892
    assert net_payout == 170108

    # 2. Apunte bancario por el abono de TPV
    bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
        transaction_id="BBVA-REDSYS-TPV-880",
        account_iban="ES9121000418450200051332",
        operation_date=date(2026, 10, 13),
        value_date=date(2026, 10, 13),
        transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
        amount_cents=net_payout,
        concept="ABONO OPERACIONES TPV REDSYS COMERCIO 12/10",
        payer_or_payee="Redsys Servicios de Procesamiento",
    )

    client2_invoices = [inv for inv in pilot_invoices if "REDSYS" in inv.invoice_id]
    assert len(client2_invoices) == 3

    # 3. Procesar y persistir lote
    batch = BatchReconciliationService.process_and_persist_batch(
        db=pilot_db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        client_id=client2.id,
        period_year=2026,
        period_type="Q4",
        start_month=10,
        end_month=12,
        bank_transactions=[bank_tx],
        open_invoices=client2_invoices,
        file_name="cliente2_redsys_tpv.csv",
        file_bytes=file_bytes,
        gateway_name="Redsys TPV",
    )

    assert batch.green_count == 1
    assert batch.imbalance_cents == 0

    # 4. Exportar a Sage 50 (XDIARIO)
    export_res = BatchReconciliationService.export_batch_to_erp(pilot_db, batch.id, target_erp="SAGE")
    assert export_res["erp"] == "SAGE"
    sage_csv = export_res["content"]

    out_file = OUTPUT_DIR / "XDIARIO_SAGE_CLIENTE2.csv"
    with open(out_file, "w", encoding="windows-1252", newline="") as f:
        f.write(sage_csv)

    assert out_file.exists()
    assert "Asiento" in sage_csv
    assert "Cuenta" in sage_csv


def test_pilot_client3_b2b_norma43_to_contasol(pilot_db, pilot_invoices):
    """
    CLIENTE 3: Distribución B2B con Extracto Bancario Norma 43
    - Fuente: Cuaderno 43 oficial emitido por Banco Santander (Windows-1252)
    - Movimientos:
      * Transf. 1: 1.450,00 € -> Cancela Fra FRA-2026-091 Hnos Martínez
      * Transf. 2: 2.410,00 € -> Cancela Fra FRA-2026-088 Amazon Services Europe
      * Abono TPV: 1.701,08 €
      * Comisión bancaria: 15,00 €
    - Destino: DELSOL Contasol (.xlsx)
    """
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.list_tenants(pilot_db)[0]
    client3 = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=pilot_db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Mediterráneo Gourmet Foods S.L.",
        fiscal_id="B-54892103",
        default_erp="CONTASOL",
        account_length=9,
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
    )

    # 1. Ingesta Norma 43
    n43_path = PILOT_DATA_DIR / "cliente3_norma43.n43"
    with open(n43_path, "rb") as f:
        raw_bytes = f.read()

    parse_res = Norma43Parser.parse_file(raw_bytes)
    assert parse_res.is_valid is True
    assert len(parse_res.canonical_transactions) == 4

    client3_invoices = [inv for inv in pilot_invoices if "B2B" in inv.invoice_id]
    assert len(client3_invoices) == 2

    # 2. Procesar y persistir lote
    batch = BatchReconciliationService.process_and_persist_batch(
        db=pilot_db,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        client_id=client3.id,
        period_year=2026,
        period_type="Q4",
        start_month=10,
        end_month=12,
        bank_transactions=parse_res.canonical_transactions,
        open_invoices=client3_invoices,
        file_name="cliente3_norma43.n43",
        file_bytes=raw_bytes,
    )

    assert batch.total_movements == 4
    # Las 2 transferencias B2B con factura coincidente pasan a Verde
    assert batch.green_count >= 2

    # 3. Exportar a DELSOL Contasol (XLSX)
    export_res = BatchReconciliationService.export_batch_to_erp(pilot_db, batch.id, target_erp="CONTASOL")
    assert export_res["erp"] == "CONTASOL"
    xlsx_bytes = export_res["content"]

    out_file = OUTPUT_DIR / "CONTASOL_CLIENTE3.xlsx"
    with open(out_file, "wb") as f:
        f.write(xlsx_bytes)

    assert out_file.exists()
    assert out_file.stat().st_size > 1000
    assert xlsx_bytes[:2] == b"PK"  # Cabecera ZIP válida de archivo .xlsx


def test_pilot_tamper_proof_audit_certificate(pilot_db):
    """
    Verifica la emisión del Certificado Legal de Auditoría con cadena de custodia SHA-256
    garantizando la inmutabilidad jurídica de todos los asientos procesados en la prueba piloto.
    """
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.list_tenants(pilot_db)[0]
    logs = ReconciliationRepository.get_audit_trail(pilot_db, tenant.id)

    assert len(logs) >= 6  # 3 BATCH_CREATED + 3 BATCH_EXPORTED

    # Verificar que la cadena SHA-256 está perfectamente intacta de principio a fin
    for i in range(1, len(logs)):
        assert logs[i].prev_hash == logs[i - 1].current_hash_sha256

    # Generar Certificado de Auditoría JSON oficial
    certificate = {
        "certificate_title": "CERTIFICADO DE AUDITORÍA Y CONCILIACIÓN CONTABLE INMUTABLE",
        "protocol": "FINDAFINITY ZERO-LIABILITY HASH-CHAIN v1.0",
        "tenant_id": tenant.id,
        "tenant_name": tenant.name,
        "tenant_fiscal_id": tenant.fiscal_id,
        "issued_at": date.today().isoformat(),
        "total_events_certified": len(logs),
        "genesis_hash": logs[0].prev_hash,
        "head_hash_sha256": logs[-1].current_hash_sha256,
        "audit_events": [
            {
                "event_id": l.id,
                "action": l.action,
                "actor": l.actor,
                "timestamp": l.timestamp.isoformat(),
                "sha256_hash": l.current_hash_sha256,
                "details": json.loads(l.details_json) if l.details_json else {},
            }
            for l in logs
        ],
    }

    cert_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "CERTIFICADO_AUDITORIA_PILOTO.json"
    with open(cert_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(certificate, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    assert cert_path.exists()
    assert len(certificate["head_hash_sha256"]) == 64


================================================================================
FICHERO: tests\test_scale_and_algorithmic_stress.py
================================================================================

"""
Test Suite de Estrés de Escala y Auditoría Algorítmica (Combinatorial & High-Volume Stress Test)

Evalúa y audita rigurosamente:
1. Estrés de Escala en Poda Heurística:
   - Universo de 2.000 facturas históricas abiertas distribuidas en 6 meses y 4 pasarelas.
   - Demostración cuantitativa: reducción de 2.000 facturas a <= 25 candidatos en < 50 ms.
2. Estrés Combinatorio en HiGHS MILP y Meet-in-the-Middle:
   - Lotes reales complejos de 15 a 30 facturas con trampas combinatorias (facturas señuelo).
   - Medición de latencia de HiGHS MILP (< 2.0 s según especificación técnica del PDF).
   - Verificación de inmunidad a facturas señuelo mediante la función objetivo regularizada.
   - Evaluación de rendimiento de Meet-in-the-Middle (Horowitz-Sahni).
3. Estrés de Asientos Compuestos Masivos:
   - Generación de asientos de partida doble para lotes de 50 facturas simultáneas.
   - Verificación estricta al céntimo: sum(Debe) - sum(Haber) == 0.00 € (desviación = 0).
4. Benchmark de Throughput y Memoria:
   - Ejecución de 20 liquidaciones de payouts en serie con medición de latencia media y memoria (tracemalloc).
"""
import random
import time
import tracemalloc
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import List, Tuple

import pytest

from src.parsers.base import (
    CanonicalBankTransaction,
    CanonicalInvoice,
    CanonicalGatewayTransaction,
    TransactionType,
)
from src.reconciliation.pruning import ReconciliationPruner
from src.reconciliation.meet_in_the_middle import MeetInTheMiddleSolver, SubsetSumCandidate
from src.reconciliation.milp_solver import HighsMILPSolver, MILPInvoiceCandidate
from src.accounting.double_entry import AccountingEntryBuilder
from src.reconciliation.engine import BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine


def test_heuristic_pruning_scale_2000_invoices():
    """
    a) Estrés de Escala en Poda Heurística:
    - Universo de 2.000 facturas históricas abiertas distribuidas en 6 meses (180 días)
      y 4 pasarelas de pago (Stripe, Redsys, PayPal, TPV).
    - Demuestra cuantitativamente que la poda reduce el espacio de 2.000 facturas
      a <= 25 candidatos en menos de 50 ms.
    """
    random.seed(2026)
    start_date = date(2026, 4, 15)
    gateways = ["stripe", "redsys", "paypal", "tpv"]

    # Generar universo de 2.000 facturas distribuidas uniformemente en 180 días
    # con importes variados y algunas facturas fuera de escala (> 10.000 €)
    universe_size = 2000
    candidates: List[CanonicalInvoice] = []

    for i in range(universe_size):
        day_offset = random.randint(0, 180)
        inv_date = start_date + timedelta(days=day_offset)
        gw = gateways[i % len(gateways)]

        # 95% importes operativos ordinarios (10 € a 1.200 €), 5% importes gigantescos atípicos (> 10.000 €)
        if random.random() < 0.05:
            gross = random.randint(1000000, 5000000)  # 10.000 € - 50.000 €
        else:
            gross = random.randint(1500, 120000)      # 15 € - 1.200 €

        candidates.append(
            CanonicalInvoice(
                invoice_id=f"INV-SCALE-{i:04d}",
                invoice_number=f"F-2026-{i:05d}",
                issue_date=inv_date,
                due_date=inv_date,
                customer_id=f"CUST-{i:04d}",
                customer_name=f"Cliente Empresa {i}",
                gross_amount_cents=gross,
                gateway_id=gw,
            )
        )

    # Liquidación a auditar: fecha final del horizonte (Día 180), pasarela Stripe, abono neto de 2.500,00 €
    payout_date = start_date + timedelta(days=180)  # 2026-10-12
    target_net_cents = 250000                        # 2.500,00 €
    target_gateway = "stripe"
    lookback_days = 7

    # Medición de latencia de alta precisión
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    pruned_result = ReconciliationPruner.prune(
        target_net_cents=target_net_cents,
        payout_date=payout_date,
        candidates=candidates,
        gateway_id=target_gateway,
        max_lookback_days=lookback_days,
    )
    pruning_latency_sec = time.perf_counter() - t0
    pruning_latency_ms = pruning_latency_sec * 1000.0

    stats = pruned_result.pruning_stats
    surviving_count = len(pruned_result.invoices)

    # Imprimir reporte cuantitativo en consola (-s)
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(" [BENCHMARK 1] AUDITORÍA DE PODA HEURÍSTICA EN 3 NIVELES (2.000 FACTURAS)")
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f" Universo inicial de facturas : {stats['initial_count']:,} facturas (6 meses, 4 pasarelas)")
    print(f" Nivel 1 (Filtro Temporal {lookback_days}d): {stats['after_temporal']} facturas retenidas")
    print(f" Nivel 2 (Filtro Pasarela '{target_gateway}'): {stats['after_gateway']} facturas retenidas")
    print(f" Nivel 3 (Bounding Box Comisiones) : {stats['after_bounding']} facturas retenidas")
    print(f" Total Candidatos Finales     : {surviving_count} facturas")
    print(f" Porcentaje de Reducción      : {stats['reduction_percentage']:.2f} %")
    print(f" Latencia de Ejecución        : {pruning_latency_ms:.3f} ms (Límite tolerado: 50.0 ms)")
    print("=" * 80)

    # Aserciones cuantitativas estrictas requeridas
    assert stats["initial_count"] == 2000, "El universo inicial debe ser exactamente de 2.000 facturas."
    assert surviving_count <= 25, f"La poda debe reducir el espacio a <= 25 candidatos, obtenido: {surviving_count}"
    assert pruning_latency_ms < 50.0, f"La latencia ({pruning_latency_ms:.2f} ms) debe ser inferior a 50.0 ms."
    assert stats["reduction_percentage"] >= 98.5, f"La reducción ({stats['reduction_percentage']}%) debe ser >= 98.5%"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_size", [15, 20, 25, 30])
def test_combinatorial_stress_highs_milp_with_decoys(batch_size: int):
    """
    b) Estrés Combinatorio en HiGHS MILP:
    - Agrupación de lotes de 15, 20, 25 y 30 facturas reales legítimas.
    - Inyección de 15 a 20 facturas señuelo con importes netos idénticos o cuasi-idénticos (trampas combinatorias),
      pero con comisiones alteradas/falsas (|c_i - c_i^teorico| >> 0).
    - Verifica:
      1. Tiempo de resolución por payout de HiGHS MILP < 2.0 segundos (especificación del PDF).
      2. Inmunidad matemática: La función objetivo regularizada nunca selecciona facturas señuelo.
      3. Identificación exacta al 100% del subconjunto legítimo.
    """
    random.seed(42 + batch_size)
    num_decoys = 15

    # 1. Crear facturas legítimas
    genuine_invoices: List[MILPInvoiceCandidate] = []
    for i in range(batch_size):
        gross = random.randint(2500, 35000)  # 25 € a 350 €
        theo_fee = int(round(gross * 0.015 + 25))  # Comisión legítima Stripe 1.5% + 0.25€
        obs_fee = theo_fee                         # Desviación cero en legítimas
        net = gross - obs_fee
        genuine_invoices.append(
            MILPInvoiceCandidate(
                invoice_id=f"GENUINE_{batch_size}_{i:02d}",
                gross_cents=gross,
                observed_fee_cents=obs_fee,
                theoretical_fee_cents=theo_fee,
                net_cents=net,
            )
        )

    target_net_cents = sum(g.net_cents for g in genuine_invoices)

    # 2. Inyectar facturas señuelo (Trampas combinatorias)
    # Poseen exactamente el mismo neto que una factura legítima, pero con comisiones arbitrarias distorsionadas
    decoy_invoices: List[MILPInvoiceCandidate] = []
    for j in range(num_decoys):
        template = genuine_invoices[j % batch_size]
        # Distorsión de comisión: 2.00 € a 5.00 € de sobrecoste artificial
        fake_deviation = random.choice([200, 350, 500, -80])
        distorted_fee = max(5, template.theoretical_fee_cents + fake_deviation)
        distorted_gross = template.net_cents + distorted_fee
        decoy_invoices.append(
            MILPInvoiceCandidate(
                invoice_id=f"DECOY_TRAP_{batch_size}_{j:02d}",
                gross_cents=distorted_gross,
                observed_fee_cents=distorted_fee,
                theoretical_fee_cents=template.theoretical_fee_cents,
                net_cents=template.net_cents,  # Mismo neto exacto que la legítima -> trampa combinatoria
            )
        )

    # Mezclar candidatos en orden aleatorio para no sesgar el solver
    all_candidates = genuine_invoices + decoy_invoices
    random.shuffle(all_candidates)

    total_pool_count = len(all_candidates)

    # 3. Resolver con HiGHS MILP y medir tiempo
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    solver_result = HighsMILPSolver.solve(
        target_net_cents=target_net_cents,
        invoices=all_candidates,
        tolerance_cents=0,  # Búsqueda exacta
        lambda_1=100.0,
        lambda_2=1.0,
    )
    solver_duration_sec = time.perf_counter() - t0
    solver_duration_ms = solver_duration_sec * 1000.0

    # Imprimir métricas
    print("\n" + "-" * 80)
    print(f" [BENCHMARK 2.MILP] HiGHS MILP CON TRAMPAS COMBINATORIAS (Lote = {batch_size} facturas)")
    print("-" * 80)
    print(f" Pool total evaluado          : {total_pool_count} candidatos ({batch_size} legítimos + {num_decoys} señuelos)")
    print(f" Neto objetivo liquidado      : {target_net_cents / 100:.2f} €")
    print(f" Éxito del Solver             : {solver_result.is_success}")
    print(f" Facturas seleccionadas       : {len(solver_result.matched_invoice_ids)}")
    print(f" Tiempo resolución HiGHS      : {solver_duration_ms:.2f} ms ({solver_duration_sec:.4f} s)")
    print(f" Límite exigido por PDF       : < 2.000,00 ms (2.0 segundos)")
    print(f" Desviación comisiones total  : {solver_result.total_fee_deviation_cents:.2f} céntimos")
    print(f" Valor función objetivo       : {solver_result.objective_value:.4f}")

    # Aserciones de rigor matemático
    assert solver_result.is_success is True, f"HiGHS MILP falló en resolver el lote de {batch_size} facturas."
    assert solver_duration_sec < 2.0, f"HiGHS excedió el límite de 2.0s: {solver_duration_sec:.4f}s"
    assert len(solver_result.matched_invoice_ids) == batch_size, "El solver no seleccionó el número exacto de facturas."

    # Inmunidad a señuelos: NINGUNA factura señuelo debe ser seleccionada
    selected_decoys = [iid for iid in solver_result.matched_invoice_ids if iid.startswith("DECOY_TRAP")]
    print(f" Facturas señuelo seleccionadas: {len(selected_decoys)} (Esperado: 0)")
    assert len(selected_decoys) == 0, f"¡Vulnerabilidad combinatoria! El solver seleccionó señuelos: {selected_decoys}"

    # Todos los IDs seleccionados deben coincidir con el conjunto legítimo
    genuine_ids = {g.invoice_id for g in genuine_invoices}
    assert set(solver_result.matched_invoice_ids) == genuine_ids


def test_combinatorial_stress_meet_in_the_middle():
    """
    b) Estrés Combinatorio en Meet-in-the-Middle (Horowitz-Sahni):
    - Evalúa el solver determinista O(2^(n/2) * (n/2)) con n = 24 candidatos.
    - Demuestra convergencia ultrarrápida (< 50 ms) para subconjuntos exactos.
    """
    random.seed(1234)
    n_candidates = 24
    candidates: List[SubsetSumCandidate] = []

    for i in range(n_candidates):
        gross = random.randint(2000, 25000)
        fee = int(round(gross * 0.015 + 25))
        net = gross - fee
        candidates.append(
            SubsetSumCandidate(
                item_id=f"MITM_INV_{i:02d}",
                net_cents=net,
                gross_cents=gross,
                fee_cents=fee,
            )
        )

    # Seleccionar un subconjunto de 12 facturas aleatorias como objetivo
    chosen_indices = [0, 2, 4, 7, 9, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 23]
    target_net_cents = sum(candidates[idx].net_cents for idx in chosen_indices)

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    mitm_res = MeetInTheMiddleSolver.solve(
        target_net_cents=target_net_cents,
        candidates=candidates,
        tolerance_cents=0,
    )
    mitm_latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0

    print("\n" + "-" * 80)
    print(" [BENCHMARK 2.MITM] MEET-IN-THE-MIDDLE HOROWITZ-SAHNI (N = 24)")
    print("-" * 80)
    print(f" Espacio combinatorial 2^24   : 16.777.216 combinaciones")
    print(f" Complejidad evaluada 2^(n/2) : 2x 2^12 = 8.192 subconjuntos")
    print(f" Tiempo resolución MITM       : {mitm_latency_ms:.2f} ms")
    print(f" Coincidencia exacta (delta=0): {mitm_res.is_exact if mitm_res else False}")
    if mitm_res:
        print(f" Facturas recuperadas         : {len(mitm_res.matched_ids)} facturas")
        print(f" Neto total recuperado        : {mitm_res.total_net_cents / 100:.2f} €")

    assert mitm_res is not None, "Meet-in-the-Middle no encontró solución exacta."
    assert mitm_res.is_exact is True
    assert mitm_res.delta_cents == 0
    assert mitm_res.total_net_cents == target_net_cents

    # Verificar que el subconjunto devuelto suma exactamente el neto objetivo al céntimo
    cand_dict = {c.item_id: c.net_cents for c in candidates}
    reconstructed_sum = sum(cand_dict[iid] for iid in mitm_res.matched_ids)
    # El PDF establece un umbral interactivo de < 2.0 segundos para resolución combinatoria
    assert mitm_latency_ms < 2000.0, f"MITM tardó más de 2.0 s: {mitm_latency_ms:.2f} ms"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("lot_iteration", [1, 2, 3])
def test_massive_composite_journal_entry_balance_50_invoices(lot_iteration: int):
    """
    c) Estrés de Asientos Compuestos Masivos:
    - Asientos contables de partida doble generados para lotes de 50 facturas simultáneas.
    - Verificar a nivel de 1 céntimo que sum(Debe) - sum(Haber) == 0.00 € sin la menor desviación.
    - Valida que la estructura del asiento PGC (572 Debe, 626 Debe, 430 Haber) sea inmaculada.
    """
    random.seed(5000 + lot_iteration)
    batch_size = 50

    matched_invoices_data = []
    total_gross_cents = 0
    total_fees_cents = 0

    for i in range(batch_size):
        gross = random.randint(1500, 75000)  # 15.00 € a 750.00 €
        fee = int(round(gross * 0.015 + 25))  # Comisión Stripe
        total_gross_cents += gross
        total_fees_cents += fee

        matched_invoices_data.append(
            {
                "account_code": f"43000{i:04d}",
                "invoice_number": f"F-MASS-2026-{lot_iteration:02d}-{i:03d}",
                "gross_cents": gross,
                "fee_cents": fee,
                "customer_name": f"Corporación Cliente {i}",
            }
        )

    # Neto liquidado abonado en banco: Total Bruto - Total Comisiones
    payout_net_cents = total_gross_cents - total_fees_cents
    entry_date = date(2026, 10, 15)

    # Construcción del asiento compuesto masivo
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    journal = AccountingEntryBuilder.build_payout_reconciliation_entry(
        entry_number=500 + lot_iteration,
        entry_date=entry_date,
        bank_account_code="572000001",
        fee_account_code="626000001",
        payout_net_cents=payout_net_cents,
        matched_invoices=matched_invoices_data,
        gateway_name="Stripe",
    )
    journal_gen_latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0

    # Auditoría estricta de balance contable al céntimo
    total_debit = sum(l.debit_cents for l in journal.lines)
    total_credit = sum(l.credit_cents for l in journal.lines)
    imbalance_cents = total_debit - total_credit

    total_debit_eur = Decimal(total_debit) / Decimal(100)
    total_credit_eur = Decimal(total_credit) / Decimal(100)
    imbalance_eur = total_debit_eur - total_credit_eur

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(f" [BENCHMARK 3] ASIENTO COMPUESTO MASIVO DE 50 FACTURAS (Lote #{lot_iteration})")
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f" Número de apuntes generados  : {len(journal.lines)} líneas contables")
    print(f" Cuenta 572 (Banco Neto)      : {payout_net_cents / 100:,.2f} € (Debe)")
    print(f" Cuenta 626 (Comisiones)      : {total_fees_cents / 100:,.2f} € (Debe)")
    print(f" Cuentas 430 (50 Clientes)    : {total_gross_cents / 100:,.2f} € (Haber)")
    print(f" Total DEBE (Bancos + Gastos) : {total_debit_eur:,.2f} €")
    print(f" Total HABER (50 Facturas)    : {total_credit_eur:,.2f} €")
    print(f" Desviación Cuadre Contable   : {imbalance_eur:.2f} € ({imbalance_cents} céntimos)")
    print(f" Estado de Balance Cuadrado   : {journal.is_balanced}")
    print(f" Tiempo generación asiento    : {journal_gen_latency_ms:.3f} ms")
    print("=" * 80)

    # Aserciones de exactitud matemática
    assert len(journal.lines) == 52, "Debe contener 1 línea banco + 1 línea comisiones + 50 líneas clientes = 52"
    assert journal.is_balanced is True, "El asiento compuesto debe estar estrictamente cuadrado."
    assert imbalance_cents == 0, f"El descuadre en céntimos debe ser 0, obtenido: {imbalance_cents}"
    assert imbalance_eur == Decimal("0.00"), f"Desviación en euros no nula: {imbalance_eur} €"
    assert total_debit == total_credit == total_gross_cents


def test_throughput_and_memory_benchmark_20_payouts_series():
    """
    d) Benchmark de Throughput y Memoria:
    - Ejecuta 20 conciliaciones de payouts en serie simulando una jornada de cierre contable.
    - Cada payout agrupa entre 8 y 28 facturas, probando tanto Meet-in-the-Middle como HiGHS MILP.
    - Mide tiempo medio por payout, latencia mínima/máxima, throughput y uso de memoria mediante tracemalloc.
    """
    tracemalloc.start()
    random.seed(9999)

    engine = BatchPayoutReconciliationEngine()
    total_payouts = 20

    latencies_ms: List[float] = []
    matched_invoice_counts: List[int] = []
    total_volume_gross_cents = 0
    total_volume_net_cents = 0
    solvers_used = []

    print("\n" + "=" * 90)
    print(" [BENCHMARK 4] PROCESAMIENTO EN SERIE DE 20 BATCH PAYOUTS (THROUGHPUT & MEMORIA)")
    print("=" * 90)
    print(f"{'#':<3} | {'Payout ID':<14} | {'Neto (€)':<10} | {'Facturas':<8} | {'Solver':<28} | {'Latencia (ms)':<12} | {'Cuadre'}")
    print("-" * 90)

    benchmark_start_sec = time.perf_counter()

    for p in range(total_payouts):
        p_date = date(2026, 10, 1) + timedelta(days=p)
        # Variar intencionalmente el tamaño de lote para ejercitar ambos motores algorítmicos
        # Lotes <= 24 facturas sin discrepancia -> Meet-in-the-Middle
        # Lotes > 24 facturas o con ligera discrepancia -> HiGHS MILP
        batch_size = random.choice([10, 14, 18, 22, 26, 28])

        open_invoices: List[CanonicalInvoice] = []
        gw_txs: List[CanonicalGatewayTransaction] = []

        batch_gross = 0
        batch_fees = 0

        for i in range(batch_size):
            gross = random.randint(2500, 45000)
            fee = int(round(gross * 0.015 + 25))
            net = gross - fee

            batch_gross += gross
            batch_fees += fee

            inv_id = f"INV-RUN{p:02d}-{i:02d}"
            inv_num = f"F-RUN{p:02d}-{i:03d}"
            inv_date = p_date - timedelta(days=random.randint(0, 5))
            created_datetime = datetime.combine(inv_date, datetime.min.time())

            open_invoices.append(
                CanonicalInvoice(
                    invoice_id=inv_id,
                    invoice_number=inv_num,
                    issue_date=inv_date,
                    due_date=inv_date,
                    customer_id=f"CUST-{p}-{i}",
                    customer_name=f"Cliente {p}-{i}",
                    gross_amount_cents=gross,
                    gateway_id="stripe",
                )
            )

            gw_txs.append(
                CanonicalGatewayTransaction(
                    transaction_id=f"GW-TX-{p:02d}-{i:02d}",
                    gateway_id="stripe",
                    created_at=created_datetime,
                    type="charge",
                    gross_cents=gross,
                    fee_cents=fee,
                    net_cents=net,
                    order_id=inv_num,
                )
            )

        # Añadir ruido con facturas de otras pasarelas y fechas fuera de ventana
        for j in range(20):
            open_invoices.append(
                CanonicalInvoice(
                    invoice_id=f"NOISE-{p:02d}-{j:02d}",
                    invoice_number=f"F-NOISE-{p:02d}-{j:02d}",
                    issue_date=p_date - timedelta(days=random.randint(20, 60)),
                    due_date=p_date,
                    customer_id=f"CUST-NOISE-{j}",
                    customer_name=f"Cliente Ajeno {j}",
                    gross_amount_cents=random.randint(5000, 20000),
                    gateway_id=random.choice(["redsys", "paypal", "tpv"]),
                )
            )

        target_payout_net_cents = batch_gross - batch_fees
        bank_tx_id = f"TX-BANK-N43-{p:03d}"
        bank_tx = CanonicalBankTransaction(
            transaction_id=bank_tx_id,
            account_iban="ES9121000418450200051332",
            operation_date=p_date,
            value_date=p_date,
            transaction_type=TransactionType.DEPOSIT,
            amount_cents=target_payout_net_cents,
            concept=f"STRIPE PAYOUT BATCH #{p:03d}",
        )

        t_payout_0 = time.perf_counter()
        report = engine.reconcile_payout(
            bank_tx=bank_tx,
            open_invoices=open_invoices,
            gateway_transactions=gw_txs,
            gateway_id="stripe",
            entry_number=1000 + p,
        )
        lat_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_payout_0) * 1000.0

        latencies_ms.append(lat_ms)
        matched_invoice_counts.append(len(report.matched_invoice_ids))
        total_volume_gross_cents += report.total_gross_cents
        total_volume_net_cents += report.total_net_cents
        solvers_used.append(report.solver_used)

        assert report.is_reconciled is True, f"Liquidación #{p} falló al conciliar."
        assert report.journal_entry is not None
        assert report.journal_entry.is_balanced is True, f"Asiento contable de #{p} descuadrado."
        assert len(report.matched_invoice_ids) == batch_size

        solver_short = "HiGHS MILP" if "HiGHS" in report.solver_used else "Meet-in-Middle"
        balance_flag = "OK (0.00€)" if report.journal_entry.is_balanced else "FAIL"

        print(
            f"{p+1:<3} | {bank_tx_id:<14} | {target_payout_net_cents / 100:>8.2f} € | "
            f"{len(report.matched_invoice_ids):<8} | {solver_short:<28} | {lat_ms:>8.2f} ms   | {balance_flag}"
        )

    total_benchmark_duration_sec = time.perf_counter() - benchmark_start_sec
    current_mem_bytes, peak_mem_bytes = tracemalloc.get_traced_memory()
    tracemalloc.stop()

    mean_lat_ms = sum(latencies_ms) / len(latencies_ms)
    min_lat_ms = min(latencies_ms)
    max_lat_ms = max(latencies_ms)
    total_matched = sum(matched_invoice_counts)
    payouts_per_sec = total_payouts / total_benchmark_duration_sec
    invoices_per_sec = total_matched / total_benchmark_duration_sec
    peak_mem_mb = peak_mem_bytes / (1024 * 1024)
    current_mem_mb = current_mem_bytes / (1024 * 1024)

    print("-" * 90)
    print(" RESUMEN GLOBAL DEL BENCHMARK DE RENDIMIENTO Y ESCALA")
    print("-" * 90)
    print(f" Total Liquidaciones Conciliadas : {total_payouts} / {total_payouts} (100.0 % éxito)")
    print(f" Total Facturas Conciliadas      : {total_matched} facturas")
    print(f" Volumen Total Bruto Procesado   : {total_volume_gross_cents / 100:,.2f} €")
    print(f" Volumen Total Neto Liquidado    : {total_volume_net_cents / 100:,.2f} €")
    print(f" Tiempo Total del Benchmark      : {total_benchmark_duration_sec:.4f} segundos")
    print(f" Latencia Media por Liquidación  : {mean_lat_ms:.2f} ms (Target: < 2.000 ms)")
    print(f" Latencia Mínima / Máxima        : {min_lat_ms:.2f} ms / {max_lat_ms:.2f} ms")
    print(f" Throughput Liquidaciones        : {payouts_per_sec:.2f} liquidaciones / seg")
    print(f" Throughput Facturas             : {invoices_per_sec:.2f} facturas / seg")
    print(f" Consumo de Memoria Pico (Peak)  : {peak_mem_mb:.2f} MB")
    print(f" Consumo de Memoria Final        : {current_mem_mb:.2f} MB")
    print(f" Desviación Contable Acumulada   : 0.00 € (100% cuadre exacto)")
    print("=" * 90)

    # Aserciones de rendimiento y calidad
    assert mean_lat_ms < 200.0, f"La latencia media ({mean_lat_ms:.2f} ms) debe ser < 200 ms."
    assert max_lat_ms < 2000.0, f"Ninguna liquidación puede tardar >= 2.0s: {max_lat_ms:.2f} ms."
    assert peak_mem_mb < 50.0, f"El consumo de memoria ({peak_mem_mb:.2f} MB) excede 50 MB."
    assert total_matched > 0

"""
Test Suite: Módulo de Suscripción, Monetización y Cuotas de Uso (Pricing del PDF)

    db = TestingSessionLocal()

    finally:
        db.close()
        Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=TEST_ENGINE)

def test_pricing_plans_specification_matches_pdf():
    """Verifica que las especificaciones de precios coincidan exactamente con la Sección 4 del PDF."""
    starter = PLAN_CONFIGS[PlanTier.STARTER.value]
    assert starter["price_eur_month"] == 49.0
    assert starter["max_clients"] == 10
    assert starter["max_monthly_transactions"] == 2500
    assert "Norma43" in starter["supported_gateways"]

    pro = PLAN_CONFIGS[PlanTier.PROFESSIONAL.value]
    assert pro["price_eur_month"] == 119.0
    assert pro["max_clients"] == 30
    assert pro["max_monthly_transactions"] == 12000
    assert "Stripe" in pro["supported_gateways"]
    assert "Redsys" in pro["supported_gateways"]
    assert pro["has_sha256_audit"] is True

    pyme = PLAN_CONFIGS[PlanTier.PYME_DIRECTO.value]
    assert pyme["price_eur_month"] == 39.0
    assert pyme["max_clients"] == 1
    assert pyme["max_monthly_transactions"] == 2000

    assert EXTRA_BLOCK_PRICE_EUR == 25.0
    assert EXTRA_BLOCK_CLIENTS == 10
    assert EXTRA_BLOCK_TRANSACTIONS == 5000


def test_tenant_billing_status_and_economics(db_session):
    """Verifica el cálculo del estado de suscripción y métricas de cuota."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Auditoría Fiscal Costa del Sol SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-29001122",
        email="contacto@costasol.es",
        plan="STARTER",
    )

    # 1. Estado inicial sin clientes
    status = BillingQuotaManager.get_tenant_billing_status(db_session, tenant.id)
    assert status["plan_tier"] == "STARTER"
    assert status["base_price_eur"] == 49.0
    assert status["total_monthly_fee_eur"] == 49.0
    assert status["clients_count"] == 0
    assert status["max_clients_limit"] == 10
    assert status["can_add_client"] is True
    assert status["monthly_transactions_count"] == 0
    assert status["max_monthly_transactions_limit"] == 2500


def test_client_quota_limit_enforcement(db_session):
    """Verifica que no se puedan superar los clientes contratados sin adquirir bloques adicionales."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Asesoría Velázquez SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-28990011",
        email="velazquez@asesoria.es",
        plan="STARTER",  # Límite: 10 clientes
    )

    # Crear 10 clientes (límite máximo de Starter)
    for i in range(10):
        ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
            db=db_session,
            tenant_id=tenant.id,
            name=f"Cliente {i+1} SL",
            fiscal_id=f"B-{10000000+i}",
        )

    status = BillingQuotaManager.get_tenant_billing_status(db_session, tenant.id)
    assert status["clients_count"] == 10
    assert status["can_add_client"] is False

    # Intentar añadir el cliente 11 debe lanzar QuotaLimitExceeded
    with pytest.raises(QuotaLimitExceeded) as exc_info:
        BillingQuotaManager.assert_can_add_client(db_session, tenant.id)
    assert "Límite de empresas clientes alcanzado" in str(exc_info.value)

    # Adquirir 1 bloque extra (+10 empresas por 25€)
    updated_status = BillingQuotaManager.update_tenant_plan(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        new_plan="STARTER",
        extra_blocks=1,
    )
    assert updated_status["max_clients_limit"] == 20
    assert updated_status["total_monthly_fee_eur"] == 49.0 + 25.0
    assert updated_status["can_add_client"] is True

    # Ahora sí permite añadir el cliente 11
    BillingQuotaManager.assert_can_add_client(db_session, tenant.id)
    c11 = ReconciliationRepository.create_client(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        name="Cliente 11 SL",
        fiscal_id="B-10000011",
    )
    assert c11.id is not None


def test_transaction_quota_limit_enforcement(db_session):
    """Verifica la validación de transacciones mensuales frente a la cuota contratada."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Gestoría Rápida SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-41882233",
        email="rapida@gestoria.es",
        plan="STARTER",  # Límite: 2500 tx
    )

    # Intentar procesar 3.000 transacciones debe ser rechazado
    with pytest.raises(QuotaLimitExceeded) as exc:
        BillingQuotaManager.assert_can_process_transactions(db_session, tenant.id, new_txs_count=3000)
    assert "Límite mensual de transacciones excedido" in str(exc.value)

    # Procesar 500 transacciones es permitido
    BillingQuotaManager.assert_can_process_transactions(db_session, tenant.id, new_txs_count=500)


def test_plan_upgrade_and_checkout_session(db_session):
    """Verifica la subida de plan a Professional y la generación de sesión de pago Stripe Checkout."""
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Despacho Moderno SLP",
        fiscal_id="B-50112233",
        email="moderno@despacho.es",
        plan="STARTER",
    )

    # Generar checkout session para plan Professional
    checkout = BillingQuotaManager.create_simulated_checkout_session(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        target_plan="PROFESSIONAL",
        extra_blocks=0,
    )
    assert "cs_test" in checkout["checkout_session_id"]
    assert checkout["amount_total_eur"] == 119.0
    assert "https://checkout.stripe.com" in checkout["checkout_url"]

    # Aplicar upgrade
    upgraded = BillingQuotaManager.update_tenant_plan(
        db=db_session,
        tenant_id=tenant.id,
        new_plan="PROFESSIONAL",
        extra_blocks=0,
    )
    assert upgraded["plan_tier"] == "PROFESSIONAL"
    assert upgraded["base_price_eur"] == 119.0
    assert upgraded["max_clients_limit"] == 30
    assert upgraded["max_monthly_transactions_limit"] == 12000
    assert upgraded["has_sha256_audit"] is True


def test_rest_endpoints_subscription_api(client_api):
    """Verifica los endpoints REST de facturación /api/v1/pricing-plans y /api/v1/tenants/{id}/subscription."""
    # 1. Catálogo público de precios
    res_plans = client_api.get("/api/v1/pricing-plans")
    assert res_plans.status_code == 200
    plans = res_plans.json()
    assert len(plans) >= 3

    # 2. Crear Tenant
    res_t = client_api.post(
        "/api/v1/tenants",
        json={
            "name": "Auditores de Levante SLP",
            "fiscal_id": "B-03998811",
            "email": "levante@auditores.es",
            "plan": "STARTER",
        },
    )
    assert res_t.status_code == 201
    tenant_id = res_t.json()["id"]

    # 3. Consultar suscripción del Tenant
    res_sub = client_api.get(f"/api/v1/tenants/{tenant_id}/subscription")
    assert res_sub.status_code == 200
    sub_data = res_sub.json()
    assert sub_data["plan_tier"] == "STARTER"
    assert sub_data["base_price_eur"] == 49.0
    assert sub_data["can_add_client"] is True

    # 4. Actualizar plan vía REST (Upgrade a Professional con 1 paquete adicional de 25€)
    res_up = client_api.post(
        f"/api/v1/tenants/{tenant_id}/subscription/upgrade",
        json={
            "new_plan": "PROFESSIONAL",
            "extra_blocks": 1,
        },
    )
    assert res_up.status_code == 200
    up_data = res_up.json()
    assert up_data["plan_tier"] == "PROFESSIONAL"
    assert up_data["extra_blocks_count"] == 1
    assert up_data["total_monthly_fee_eur"] == 119.0 + 25.0
    assert up_data["max_clients_limit"] == 30 + 10  # 40 empresas

    # 5. Generar Stripe Checkout Session
    res_cs = client_api.post(
        f"/api/v1/tenants/{tenant_id}/subscription/checkout-session",
        json={
            "target_plan": "PROFESSIONAL",
            "extra_blocks": 1,
        },
    )
    assert res_cs.status_code == 200
    cs_data = res_cs.json()
    assert "checkout_url" in cs_data
    assert cs_data["amount_total_eur"] == 144.0


def test_stripe_public_config_and_webhook_handling(client_api, db_session):
    """Verifica el endpoint de configuración pública de Stripe y el procesamiento de webhooks."""
    # 1. Configuración pública
    res_conf = client_api.get("/api/v1/subscription/config")
    assert res_conf.status_code == 200
    conf_data = res_conf.json()
    assert "publishable_key" in conf_data
    assert "products" in conf_data
    assert "PYME_DIRECTO" in conf_data["products"]
    assert conf_data["products"]["PYME_DIRECTO"]["price_eur"] == 39.0

    # 2. Crear Tenant en plan Starter
    tenant = ReconciliationRepository.create_tenant(
        db=db_session,
        name="Asesoría Mediterráneo SL",
        fiscal_id="B-44556677",
        email="info@mediterraneo.es",
        plan="STARTER",
    )
    assert tenant.plan == "STARTER"

    # 3. Disparar webhook de Stripe simulando checkout.session.completed para upgrade a PRO
    webhook_payload = {
        "id": "evt_test_12345",
        "type": "checkout.session.completed",
        "data": {
            "object": {
                "id": "cs_test_session_completed",
                "client_reference_id": tenant.id,
                "customer": "cus_test_999",
                "metadata": {
                    "tenant_id": tenant.id,
                    "target_plan": "PROFESSIONAL",
                    "extra_blocks": "2",
                },
            }
        },
    }

    res_wh = client_api.post("/api/v1/subscription/webhook", json=webhook_payload)
    assert res_wh.status_code == 200
    wh_data = res_wh.json()
    assert wh_data["handled"] is True
    assert wh_data["action"] == "PLAN_UPGRADED"

    # 4. Verificar que en la base de datos el plan ha sido actualizado a PROFESSIONAL con 2 bloques
    db_session.refresh(tenant)
    assert tenant.plan == "PROFESSIONAL"
    assert tenant.extra_blocks == 2
